The galley belonged to the captain of the port, a mincing figure of a mariner, whose highest appetite in life was to lick the feet of the great, and he began to fawn and prostrate himself at once, and to wish that his eyes had been blinded before he saw the Empress in such deadly peril.

===Chinese===

这桨船是港务长官的。那是个矫揉扭捏的水手形人物，此生最高的嗜好，便是舔权贵们的脚。他立刻谄媚着拜倒下去，只恨不得自己的眼睛，在见到女皇陷于这般生死危难之前就先瞎掉。

===Original===

“The peril may pass,” said she. “It’s nothing mortal that will ever kill me. But I have spoiled my pretty clothes, and shed a jewel or two, and that’s annoying enough as you say, good man.”

===Chinese===

「危不危的，随它去，」她说。「能杀我的，从来不是什么致命的东西。只是我糟蹋了几件漂亮衣裳，还丢了一两件首饰，这确实够叫人心疼的，也难为你这么说，好人儿。」

===Original===

The silly fellow repeated a wish that he might be blinded before the Empress was ever put to such discomfort again.

===Chinese===

这蠢货又把那个愿望重说了一遍：女皇若再受这般不自在，情愿自己的眼睛先瞎了才好。

===Original===

But it seemed she could be cloyed with flattery. “If you are tired of your eyes,” said she, “let me tell you that you have gone the way to have them plucked out from their sockets. Kill my mammoth, would you, because he has shown himself a trifle frolicsome? You and your sort want more education, my man. I shall have to teach you that port-captains and such small creatures are very easy to come by, and very small value when got, but that my mammoth is mine﻿—mine, do you understand?﻿—the property of Goddess Phorenice, and as such is sacred.”

===Chinese===

可她似乎奉承也会吃腻。「你既然嫌你那双眼睛碍事，」她说，「那我不妨告诉你，你方才已经走到了让它们被剜出眼眶的边上。就因为我这猛犸稍稍撒了点野，你们就要杀它，是么？你，还有你们这路人，都欠调教，我的好人。我得教教你：港务长官之流的小玩意儿，要多少有多少，弄到手也一文不值；可我的猛犸是我的——是我的，你明白么？——是女神福伦尼刻的产业，既是女神之物，便是神圣不可犯的。」

===Original===

The port-captain abased himself before her. “I am an ignorant fellow,” said he, “and heaven was robbed of its brightest ornament when Phorenice came down to Atlantis. But if reparation is permitted me, I have two prisoners in the cabin of the boat here who shall be sacrificed to the mammoth forthwith. Doubtless it would please him to make sport with them, and spill out the last lees of his rage upon their bodies.”

===Chinese===

港务长官在她面前卑躬屈膝。「我是个无知的人，」他说，「福伦尼刻降临亚特兰蒂斯那天，上天便被夺去了它最明亮的妆饰。可若容我补过，我这条船的舱里现押着两个俘虏，可以立刻宰了献给猛犸。拿他们耍个把戏，把它胸中最后一点余怒尽数倾在他们身上，想必它会觉得有趣。」

===Original===

“Prisoners you’ve got, have you? How taken?”

===Chinese===

「俘虏，你有俘虏？怎么拿住的？」

===Original===

“Under cover of last night they were trying to pass in between the two forts which guard the harbour mouth. But their boat fouled the chain, and by the light of the torches the sentries spied them. They were caught with ropes, and put in a dungeon. There is an order not to abuse prisoners before they have been brought before a judgment?”

===Chinese===

「昨夜趁着夜色，他们想从把守港口入口的两座堡垒之间钻进来。可他们的船缠上了拦港铁链，哨兵们打着火把照见了他们。他们被绳索拿住，投进了地牢。不是有令在先：囚犯未经带上堂来听断，不得擅加凌虐么？」

===Original===

“It was my order. Did these prisoners offer to buy their lives with news?”

===Chinese===

「那道令是我下的。这两个俘虏可曾想拿消息来赎命？」

===Original===

“The man has not spoken. Indeed, I think he got his death-wound in being taken. The woman fought like a cat also, so they said in the fort, but she was caught without hurt. She says she has got nothing that would be of use to tell. She says she has tired of living like a savage outside the city, and moreover that, inside, there is a man for whose nearness she craves most mightily.”

===Chinese===

「那男人一言不发。说实在的，我看他被拿住时就带了致命伤。那女人也像猫儿似的又抓又咬，堡垒里的人这么说，不过她毫发无伤地被拿住了。她说，她没有任何有用的东西可讲。她说，她过腻了城外野人般的日子，再者说，城里面有一个男人，她朝思暮想，一心要到他跟前来。」

===Original===

“Tut!” said Phorenice. “Is this a romance we have swum to? You see what affectionate creatures we women are, Deucalion.”﻿—The galley was brought up against the royal quay and made fast to its golden rings. I handed the Empress ashore, but she turned again and faced the boat, her garments still yielding up a slender drip of water.﻿—“Produce your woman prisoner, master captain, and let us see whether she is a runaway wife, or a lovesick girl mad after her sweetheart. Then I will deliver judgment on her, and as like as not will surprise you all with my clemency. I am in a mood for tender romance today.”

===Chinese===

「啧！」福伦尼刻说。「咱们这是游进一出风流戏文里来了？瞧瞧我们女人是多情到什么地步的造物吧，德乌卡利翁。」——桨船靠上王家码头，系牢在码头的金环上。我搀女皇登岸，可她又回过身，面对那条船，衣裳上还淋漓着细细的水滴。——「把你那个女俘虏带上来，船长先生，让我们瞧瞧，她是个跟人私逃的媳妇呢，还是个害着相思、为情郎发痴的姑娘。然后我来给她断案，多半还要以我的宽仁，叫你们大家吃一惊。今天我正有兴致听些温柔的情话。」

===Original===

The port-captain went into the little hutch of a cabin with a white face. It was plain that Phorenice’s pleasantries scared him. “The man appears to be dead, Your Majesty. I see that his wounds﻿—”

===Chinese===

港务长官脸色刷白地钻进那间小笼子似的舱房。福伦尼刻的打趣显然吓着了他。「那男的看样子是死了，陛下。我看他那些伤——」

===Original===

“Bring out the woman, you fool. I asked for her. Keep your carrion where it is.”

===Chinese===

「把那女人带出来，你这蠢货。我要的是她。你那具臭尸首，留在原地别动。」

===Original===

I saw the fellow stoop for his knife to cut a lashing, and presently who should he bring out to the daylight but the girl I had saved from the cave-tigers in the circus, and who had so strangely drawn me to her during the hours that we had spent afterwards in companionship. It was clear, too, that the Empress recognised her also. Indeed, she made no secret about the matter, addressing her by name, and mockingly making inquiries about the ménage of the rebels, and the success of the prisoner’s amours.

===Chinese===

我看见那家伙俯身拔刀，割断一条捆索，不多时，他领到日光下来的，不是别人，正是我在圆场上从洞虎爪下救出的那个姑娘——就是在随后我们相伴的那几个时辰里，那样奇妙地把我牵向她的那一个。女皇显然也认出了她。真的，她在这事上毫不避讳，直呼其名，还带着讥笑，打听叛党营里的家计，以及这囚徒情场的收获。

===Original===

“This good port-captain tells me that you made a most valiant attempt to return, Nais, and for an excuse you told that it was your love for some man in the city here which drew you. Come, now, we are willing to overlook much of your faults, if you will give us a reasonable chance. Point me out your man, and if he is a proper fellow, I will see that he weds you honestly. Yes, and I will do more for you, Nais, since this day brings me to a husband. Seeing that all your estate is confiscate as a penalty for your late rebellion, I will charge myself with your dowry, and give it back to you. So come, name me the man.”

===Chinese===

「这位好心的港务长官告诉我，你这一趟回城扑得够勇的，娜伊丝，你还编了个由头，说是城里哪个男人对你的情意把你引来的。好了，现在，只要你给我们一个说得过去的台阶，你那些过错，我们大半都可以不问。把你那个男人指给我看，只要他是个像样的人，我保险让他正正经经娶你。不止呢，娜伊丝，既然今天我自己也要得一个丈夫，我还要为你做更多。念你新近叛乱之罪，你的家产已尽数充公，你的嫁妆就包在我身上，我替你出，再还给你。来吧，把那男人的名字说给我。」

===Original===

The girl looked at her with a sullen brow. “I spoke a lie,” she said; “there is no man.”

===Chinese===

姑娘郁沉地蹙着眉望她。「我说了谎，」她说，「没有什么男人。」

===Original===

I tried myself to give her advocacy. “The lady doubtless spoke what came to her lips. When a woman is in the grip of a rude soldiery, any excuse which can save her for the moment must serve. For myself, I should think it like enough that she would confess to having come back to her old allegiance, if she were asked.”

===Chinese===

我也试着替她开脱。「这位夫人说的，无疑是当时脱口而出的话。一个女子落在凶悍兵卒手里，凡能救得一时，什么由头都只好用上。至于我自己，我想，若有人问她，她多半会承认自己是回来重归旧日臣属的。」

===Original===

“Sir,” said the Empress, “keep your peace. Any interest you may show in this matter will go far to offend me. You have spoken of Nais in your narrative before, and although your tongue was shrewd and you did not say much, I am a woman and I could read between the lines. Now regard, my rebel, I have no wish to be unduly hard upon you, though once you were my fan-girl, and so your running away to these ill-kempt malcontents, who beat their heads against my city walls, is all the more naughty. But you must meet me halfway. You must give an excuse for leniency. Point me out the man you would wed, and he shall be your husband tomorrow.”

===Chinese===

「先生，」女皇说，「你少开口。你在这件事上露出的一丝兴趣，都大有可能触怒我。你先前讲述经历时，已提到过娜伊丝，虽然你的口风谨慎，说得也不多，可我是个女人，字里行间我读得出话来。现在听着，我的小叛贼，我并不想对你过分苛酷，虽然你一度是我的执扇侍女，你之私逃到那些拿脑袋往我城墙上撞的蓬头垢面的怨军中去，就愈发可恶。可你也得向我让半步。你总得给个从宽的由头。把你愿意嫁的那个男人指给我，明天他就做你的丈夫。」

===Original===

“There is no man.”

===Chinese===

「没有什么男人。」

===Original===

“Then name me one at random. Why, my pretty Nais, not ten months ago there were a score who would have leaped at the chance of having you for a wife. Drop your coyness, girl, and name me one of those. I warrant you that I will be your ambassadress and will put the matter to him with such delicacy that he will not make you blush by refusal.”

===Chinese===

「那就随口给我说一个。怎么，我美丽的娜伊丝，不到十个月以前，为得着你做妻子这个机会，扑上来的男人足足二十个。收起你的忸怩吧，姑娘，从那里面给我挑一个。我担保亲自做你的媒使，把这桩事说得那样婉转，保准他不至于回绝你、叫你红脸。」

===Original===

The prisoner moistened her lips. “I am a maiden, and I have a maiden’s modesty. I will die as you choose, but I will not do this indecency.”

===Chinese===

女囚润了润嘴唇。「我是个闺女，有闺女的廉耻。随你们怎么处死我，我不干这种有失体面的事。」

===Original===

“Well, I am a maiden too, and though because I am Empress also, questions of State have to stand before questions of my private modesty, I can have a sympathy for yours﻿—although in truth it did not obtrude unduly when you were my fan-girl, Nais. No, come to think of it, you liked a tender glance and a pretty phrase as well as any when you were fan-girl. You have grown wild and shy, amongst these savage rebels, but I will not punish you for that.

===Chinese===

「哼，我也是个闺女，而且——虽因我兼是女皇，国务须得排在我个人廉耻前面——我对你的廉耻倒能体恤——虽说实话讲，你做我执扇侍女那阵子，这廉耻也并不怎么碍事，娜伊丝。不，细想起来，你做执扇侍女时，温柔的眼波、漂亮的话，你哪样不爱听。在这些野蛮叛贼中间，你养野了、养怯了，可我不为此罚你。

===Original===

“Let me call your favourites to memory now. There was Tarca, of course, but Tarca had a difference with that ill-dressed father of yours, and wears a leprosy on half his face instead of that beard he used to trim so finely. And then there is Tatho, but Tatho is away overseas. Eron, too, you liked once, but he lost an arm in fighting t’other day, and I would not marry you to less than a whole man. Ah, by my face! I have it, the dainty exquisite, Rota! He is the husband! How well I remember the way he used to dress in a change of garb each day to catch your proud fancy, girl. Well, you shall have Rota. He shall lead you to wife before this hour tomorrow.”

===Chinese===

「且让我数数你从前属意过的那些人。塔尔卡嘛，本来算一个，可塔尔卡同你那位衣着寒酸的父亲闹了一场别扭，如今半张脸长着麻风，取代了他从前修剪得那么漂亮的那部胡须。再有是塔托，可塔托远在海外。埃龙（Eron），你一度也喜欢过，可他前两天打仗少了一条胳膊，而我不肯把你嫁给一个不全乎的男人。啊，凭我这张脸起誓！我想着了，那个娇滴滴的浮华公子，罗塔（Rota）！丈夫就是他！他从前为讨你那高傲的青睐，一天换一套衣裳，那光景我记得多清楚啊，姑娘。好，你就得着罗塔吧。明天这个时辰之前，他就要娶你为妻。」

===Original===

Again the prisoner moistened her lips. “I will not have Rota, and spare me the others. I know why you mock me, Phorenice.”

===Chinese===

女囚又润了润嘴唇。「我不要罗塔，其余的人也免了吧。我知道你为什么戏弄我，福伦尼刻。」

===Original===

“Then there are three of us here who share one knowledge.”﻿—She turned her eyes upon me. Gods! who ever saw the like of Phorenice’s eyes, and who ever saw them lit with such fire as burned within them then?﻿—“My lord, you are marrying me for policy; I am marrying you for policy, and for another reason which has grown stronger of late, and which you may guess at. Do you wish still to carry out the match?”

===Chinese===

「那么这儿就有三个人，共守着同一个秘密了。」——她把眼睛转到我身上。诸神啊！谁曾见过福伦尼刻那双眼睛一般的眸子？谁又曾见过它们燃起过彼时在眼底烧着的那样的火？——「大人，你娶我，是为了国策；我嫁你，也是为了国策，还为了另一个缘由，这缘由近来愈发强了，你不妨猜上一猜。这门亲事，你还愿意成么？」

===Original===

I looked once at Nais, and then I looked steadily back to Phorenice. The command given by the mouth of Zaemon from the High Council of the Sacred Mountain had to outweigh all else, and I answered that such was my desire.

===Chinese===

我望了娜伊丝一眼，随即把目光稳稳收回福伦尼刻身上。泽蒙借圣山祭司最高议事会之口传下的训令，必须重于其余一切，于是我回答说，那确是我的意愿。

===Original===

“Then,” said she, glowering at me with her eyes, “you shall build me up the pretty body of Nais beneath a throne of granite as a wedding gift. And you shall do it too with your own proper hands, my Deucalion, whilst I watch your devotion.”

===Chinese===

「那么，」她说，双眼灼灼地瞪着我，「作为给我的婚礼赠礼，你要把娜伊丝这娇美的身子，砌到一座花岗岩御座的底下去。而且要你亲自动手，我的德乌卡利翁，做的时候，我就在旁边，看着你的深情。」

===Original===

And to Nais she turned with a cruel smile. “You lied to me, my girl, and you spoke truth to the soldiers in the harbour forts. There is a man here in the city you came after, and he is the one man you may not have. Because you know me well, and my methods very thoroughly, your love for him must be very deep, or you would not have come. And so, being here, you shall be put beyond mischief’s reach. I am not one of those who see luxury in fostering rivals.

===Chinese===

她又转向娜伊丝，带着残忍的笑。「你对我撒了谎，我的姑娘，对港上堡垒里的兵卒倒说了实话。城里有一个你为此而来的男人，而他恰恰是你唯一不能得到的男人。因为你深知我的为人，把我的手段摸得一清二楚，你对他的爱必定极深，不然你不会来。所以呢，人既然到了这里，就得把你安置到闯不出祸事的地方。我可不是那种把豢养情敌当享受的人。」

===Original===

“You came for attention at the hands of Deucalion. By my face! you shall have it. I will watch myself whilst he builds you up living.”

===Chinese===

「你来，为的是要德乌卡利翁的眷顾。凭我这张脸起誓！你会得着的。他把你活着砌进去的时候，我要亲自在旁看着。」

## XI. An Affair with the Barbarous Fishers / 十一　与野蛮渔人的事端
===Original===

So this mighty Empress chose to be jealous of a mere woman prisoner!

===Chinese===

瞧这位权势熏天的女皇，竟偏偏要吃一个区区女囚的醋！

===Original===

Now my mind has been trained to work with a soldierly quickness in these moments of stress, and I decided on my proper course on the instant the words had left her lips. I was sacrificing myself for Atlantis by order of the High Council of the Priests, and, if needful, Nais must be sacrificed also, although in the same flash a scheme came to me for saving her.

===Chinese===

我的头脑素来训练有素，在这等危急关头能以军人的迅捷运转；她话音才离唇，我当下便定下了应走的路数。我既奉祭司最高议事会之命为亚特兰蒂斯牺牲自己，若有必要，娜伊丝也须一并牺牲——虽说就在同一瞬，一条救她的计策已在我心里闪现。

===Original===

So I bowed gravely before the Empress, and said I, “In this, and in all other things where a mere human hand is potent, I will carry out your wishes, Phorenice.” And she on her part patted my arm, and fresh waves of feeling welled up from the depths of her wondrous eyes. Surely the Gods won for her half her schemes and half her battles when they gave Phorenice her shape, and her voice, and the matters which lay within the outlines of her face.

===Chinese===

于是我向女皇郑重一躬，说道：「在这件事上，以及在凡人一只手能奏效的一切别的事情上，我都愿遂你的心愿，福伦尼刻。」她呢，则拍了拍我的手臂，那双妙目深处又涌起新的层层情澜。诸神赋予福伦尼刻这身段、这嗓音、这眉目轮廓之间蕴藏的万般意态时，想必已替她赢下了她一半的图谋、一半的战局。

===Original===

By this time the merchants, and the other dwellers adjacent to this part of the harbour, where the royal quay stands, had come down, offering changes of raiment, and houses to retire into. Phorenice was all graciousness, and though it was little enough I cared for mere wetness of my coat, still that part of the harbour into which we had been thrown by the mammoth was not over savoury, and I was glad enough to follow her example. For myself, I said no further word to Nais, and refrained even from giving her a glance of farewell. But a small sop like this was no meal for Phorenice, and she gave the port-captain strict orders for the guarding of his prisoner before she left him.

===Chinese===

这时，商贾们和毗邻王家码头一带而居的其他住户都已赶了下来，献上替换的衣裳，还请出屋子让我们歇息。福伦尼刻满身雍容和悦。衣裳湿了这点小事我本不大在意，只是我们被猛犸甩进去的那一角海港，实在说不上气味雅致，我便也乐得效仿她。至于我自己，再没对娜伊丝多说半句话，连一道道别的眼色也克制着不曾给。可这样一小片面包心，是填不饱福伦尼刻的；临走前，她又给港务长官下了严令，命他看守好他的囚徒。

===Original===

At the house into which I was ushered they gave me a bath, and I eased my host of the plainest garment in his store, and he was pleased enough at getting off so cheaply. But I had an hour to spend outside on the pavement listening to the distant din of bombardment before Phorenice came out to me again, and I could not help feeling some grim amusement at the face of the merchant who followed. The fellow was clearly ruined. He had a store of jewels and gauds of the most costly kind, which were only in fraction his own, seeing that he had bought them (as the custom is) in partnership with other merchants. These had pleased Phorenice’s eye, and so she had taken all and disposed them on her person.

===Chinese===

我被引进的那户人家给我备了浴汤，我顺手把主人库中最素朴的一件衣裳「减轻」到他身上，他只破费这么点便脱了身，已是喜出望外。可我在门外的街面上枯等了一个钟头，听着远处炮轰的喧响，福伦尼刻才重新出来见我；瞧着跟在她身后出来的那商人的脸色，我不由得生出一丝冷然的兴味。这家伙分明是毁了。他藏着一批极贵重的珠宝饰物，却只小半是自家财产，因为（照通行的规矩）他是与其他商人合伙置办的。这些东西入了福伦尼刻的眼，于是她尽数取走，披挂了一身。

===Original===

“Are they not pretty?” said she, showing them to me. “See how they flash under the sun. I am quite glad now, Deucalion, that the mammoth gave us that furious ride and that spill, since it has brought me such a bonny present. You may tell the fellow here that some day when he has earned some more, I will come and be his guest again. Ah! They have brought us litters, I see. Well, send one away and do you share mine with me, sir. We must play at being lovers today, even if love is a matter which will come to us both with more certainty tomorrow. No; do not order more bearers. My own slaves will carry us handily enough. I am glad you are not one of your gross, overfed men, Deucalion. I am small and slim myself, and I do not want to be husbanded by a man who will overshadow me.”

===Chinese===

「这些不漂亮吗？」她把它们展示给我看。「瞧它们在日头底下多么闪亮。德乌卡利翁，如今我倒真高兴猛犸带我们疯了那一程、摔了那一跤，因为它给我挣来这么一件好礼。你可以告诉这儿这家伙，等哪天他又挣下一些，我再来做他的客人。啊！我看他们给我们抬轿子来了。好，打发掉一乘，你与我合乘我这一乘吧，先生。今天我们得扮一扮情人，哪怕恋爱这桩事，要等到明天才更有把握落到你我头上。不；不用多叫轿夫。我自己的奴隶抬我们绰绰有余。我很高兴你不是那种粗蠢痴肥的男人，德乌卡利翁。我自己生得娇小苗条，可不想嫁一个把我整个罩住的丈夫。」

===Original===

“Back to the royal pyramid?” I asked.

===Chinese===

「回王家金字塔去？」我问。

===Original===

“No, nor to the walls. I neither wish to fight nor to sit as Empress today, sir. As I have told you before, it is my whim to be Phorenice, the maiden, for a few hours, and if someone I wot of would woo me now, as other maidens are wooed, I should esteem it a luxury. Bid the slaves carry us round the harbour’s rim, and give word to these starers that, if they follow, I will call down fire upon them as I did upon the sacrifice.”

===Chinese===

「不，也不回城墙去。今天我既不想打仗，也不想端坐为女皇，先生。我从前便对你说过，我这会儿的兴致，是做几个时辰的少女福伦尼刻；倘若有那么一位我心知肚明的人，此刻肯像追求别的少女一般追求我，那在我便是极奢侈的享受了。吩咐奴隶们抬着我们沿港湾绕行，再给那些盯梢的递句话：谁若跟着，我便引天火烧他，就像方才烧那祭牲一般。」

===Original===

Now, I had seen something of the unruliness of the streets myself, and I had gathered a hint also from the officer at the gate of the royal pyramid that night of Phorenice’s welcoming banquet. But as whatever there was in the matter must be common knowledge to the Empress, I did not bring it to her memory then. So I dismissed the guard which had come up, and drove away with a few sharp words the throng of gaping sightseers who always, silly creatures, must needs come to stare at their betters; and then I sat in the litter in the place where I was invited, and the bearers put their heads to the pole.

===Chinese===

说到街头的不驯，我自己已见识过一二；福伦尼刻接风宴那晚，王家金字塔门前那位军官的话，也让我听出几分端倪。但此事无论有什么内情，女皇必定了然，我便没有在当时向她提起。于是我遣散了赶上来的卫队，又用几句厉声呵斥赶走了那群张口呆看的闲人——这些蠢货，见了体面人总忍不住要凑上来盯瞧。随后我在受让的位子上坐进轿中，轿夫们把头颈拱进了轿杠。

===Original===

They swung away with us along the wide pavement which runs between the houses of the merchants and the mariner folk and the dimpling waters of the harbour, and I thought somewhat sadly of the few ships that floated on that splendid basin now, and of the few evidences of business that showed themselves on the quays. Time was when the ships were berthed so close that many had to wait in the estuary outside the walls, and memorials had been sent to the King that the port should be doubled in size to hold the glut of trade. And that, too, in the old days of oar and sail, when machines drawing power from our Lord the Sun were but rarely used to help a vessel speedily along her course.

===Chinese===

轿夫们抬着我们悠悠而行，沿着商贾水手人家与微漾港水之间那条宽阔的石道；我想起此刻浮在那片壮丽港池上的寥寥几艘船、码头上稀见的生意迹象，心头不免生出几分怅然。想当年，船只停得密密匝匝，许多船只好在外墙外的河口候泊，请愿文书一封封递到老国王面前，求他把港口扩充一倍，以容那泛滥的贸易。何况那还是桨帆用力的旧时光，借我们太阳神主之力驱动的机械，还极少用来帮船只快捷地走完航程。

===Original===

The Egypt voyage and a return was a matter of a year then, as against a brace of months now, and of three ships that set out, one at least could be reckoned upon succumbing to the dangers of the wide waters and the terrible beasts that haunt them. But in those old days trade roared with lusty life, and was ever growing wider and more heavy. Your merchant then was a portly man and gave generously to the Gods. But now all the world seemed to be in arms, and moreover trade was vulgar. Your merchant, if he was a man of substance, forgot his merchandise, swore that chaffering was more indelicate than blasphemy and curled his beard after the new fashion, and became a courtier. Where his father had spent anxious days with cargo tally and ship-master, the son wasted hours in directing sewing men as they adorned a coat, and nights in vapouring at a banquet.

===Chinese===

那时跑一趟埃及再返回，是足足一年的事，如今不过两个月；而每三艘出海的船，至少可料定有一艘要葬身在茫茫大水的凶险和出没其间可怕巨兽的口腹之中。但在那旧日时光，贸易怀着旺盛的生命力轰然前行，地盘越做越宽，分量越压越重。那时的商人是个脑满肠肥的体面人，向诸神的供奉也出手大方。可如今，普天之下似乎都在兵戈相见，况且经商也已成了俗气事。你的商人，但凡有点家底，便把货物抛在脑后，赌咒说讨价还价比亵渎神明更失体面，把胡须按新式样卷起来，摇身成了朝臣。他的父亲当年为货单和船主愁白了多少个白天，这儿子却把时辰虚掷在指点裁缝装点一件外袍上，把夜晚浪掷在宴席的大话里。

===Original===

Of the smaller merchants who had no substance laid by, taxes and the constant bickerings of war had well-nigh ground them into starvation. Besides, with the country in constant uproar, there were few markets left for most merchandise, nor was there aught made now which could be carried abroad. If your weaver is pressed as a fire-tube man he does not make cloth, and if your farmer is playing at rebellion, he does not buy slaves to till his fields. Indeed, they told me that a month before my return, as fine a cargo of slaves had been brought into harbour as ever came out of Europe, and there was nothing for it but to set them ashore across the estuary, and leave them free to starve or live in the wild ground there as they chose. There was no man in all Atlantis who would hold so much as one more slave as a gift.

===Chinese===

至于那些没有积蓄的小商人，赋税和连绵不断的战事摩擦，几乎把他们碾到了饿死的边缘。再者，国内动乱不休，大多数货物已剩不下几个市场，如今也造不出什么可以运销海外的物件。你的织工若被拉去充了火管兵，他便织不成布；你的农夫若在忙于谋反，他便不买奴隶耕田。可不是么——他们对我说，我返回前一个月，港里曾运进一批上好奴隶，品质之佳不亚于历来从欧罗巴贩来的任何一批，却也毫无办法，只好把他们卸在河口对岸的滩上，任他们自便，或饿死，或在那片荒地里讨生活。整个亚特兰蒂斯，再没有一个人肯多收留一个奴隶，白送也不要。

===Original===

But though I was grieved at this falling away, all schemes for remedy would be for afterwards. It would only make ill worse to speak of it as we rode together in the litter. I was growing to know Phorenice’s moods enough for that. Still, I think that she too had studied mine, and did her best to interest me between her bursts of trifling. We went out to where the westernmost harbour wall joins the land, and there the panting bearers set us down. She led me into a little house of stone which stood by itself, built out on a promontory where there is a constant run of tide, and when we had been given admittance, after much unbarring, she showed me her new gold collectors.

===Chinese===

这些衰败景象虽然令我痛心，但一切补救方略都只好留待日后。同乘一轿之时提起这些，只会使病情更重。对此，我已渐渐摸得透福伦尼刻的性子。不过，我想她也研读过我的心思，在嬉闹玩笑的间隙，总尽力找些让我感兴趣的话头。我们一路行到最西端的港湾隔墙与陆地相接之处，气喘吁吁的轿夫在那里把我们放下。她领我走进一座孤零零的小石屋；石屋筑在一处海岬上向外伸出，那里潮流终日奔涌不休。等一道道门闩陆续卸下、我们获准进屋之后，她领我看她的新式集金所（gold collectors）。

===Original===

In the dry knowledge taught in the colleges and groves of the Sacred Mountain it had been a common fact to us that the metal gold was present in a dissolved state in all sea water, but of plans for dragging it forth into yellow hardness, none had ever been discussed. But here this field-reared upstart of an Empress had stumbled upon the trick as though it had been written in a book.

===Chinese===

在圣山的学院和林苑里传授的枯燥学问告诉我们，金属黄金以溶解之态存在于一切海水之中，这原是个人尽皆知的事实；但要说有什么法子能把它拖拽出来、凝成黄色的坚硬之物，却从未有人讨论过。可在这儿，这位田垄间养大的暴发户女皇，竟一头撞破了这诀窍，仿佛它是白纸黑字写在一本书里似的。

===Original===

She patted my arm laughingly as I stared curiously round the place. “I tell all others in Atlantis that only the Gods have this secret,” said she, “and that They gave it to me as one of themselves. But I am no Goddess to you, am I, Deucalion? And, by my face! I have no other explanation of how this plan was invented. We’ll suppose I must have dreamed it. Look! The seawater sluices in through that culvert, and passes over these rough metal plates set in the floor, and then flows out again yonder in its natural course. You see the yellow metal caught in the ridges of the plates? That is gold. And my fellows here melt it with fire into bars, and take it to my smith’s in the city. The tides vary constantly, as you priests know well, as the quiet moon draws them, and it does not take much figuring to know how much of the sea passes through these culverts in a month and how much gold to a grain should be caught in the plates. My fellows here at first thought to cheat me, but I towed two of them in the water once behind a galley till the cannibal fish ate them, and since then the others have given me credit for﻿—for what do you think?”

===Chinese===

我好奇地四下打量这地方时，她笑着拍我的手臂。「我对亚特兰蒂斯所有其余的人都说，这秘密唯有诸神才有，」她道，「还说他们把它给了我，把我当诸神中的一位。可在你眼里我并不是女神，对吧，德乌卡利翁？再说，凭我这张脸起誓！这法子是怎么想出来的，我自己也拿不出别的解释。我们就假定我准是梦来的吧。瞧！海水从那条暗渠里冲进来，淌过铺在地面上的这些粗糙金属板，再顺着天然的流向从那边流出去。你看见板槽里兜住的黄色金属了吗？那就是黄金。我这些伙计在这儿用火把它熔成金条，送到城里的金匠作坊去。潮汐起落不定，这一点你们祭司最清楚——宁静的月亮牵引着它们——稍稍一算便知道，一个月里有多少海水流过这些暗渠，板里按理该兜住多少黄金、精确到几厘几毫。我这儿的手下起先还想欺瞒我，可我有一回把他们两个拴在水里，拖在一条桨船后面，直到食人鱼（cannibal fish）把他们吃了个干净。打那以后，其余的人便信了我——你猜信了我什么？」

===Original===

“More divinity.”

===Chinese===

「更多的神性。」

===Original===

“I suppose it is that. But I am letting you see how it is done. Just have the head to work out a little sum, and see what an effect can be gained. You will be a God yet yourself, Deucalion, with these silly Atlanteans, if only you will use your wit and cleverness.”

===Chinese===

「我猜也就是这么回事。不过我这是让你瞧瞧它是怎么弄成的。只消动动脑子，算一笔小小的账，看看能收多大的成效。德乌卡利翁，只要你肯使出你的机智与聪明，在这些蠢笨的亚特兰蒂斯人当中，你自己迟早也会成一位神。」

===Original===

Was she laughing at me? Was she in earnest? I could not tell. Sometimes she pointed out that her success and triumphs were merely the reward of thought and brilliancy, and next moment she gave me some impossible explanation and left me to deduce that she must be more than mortal or the thing could never have been found. In good truth, this little woman with her supple mind and her supple body mystified me more and more the longer I stayed by her side; and more and more despairing did I grow that Atlantis could ever be restored by my agency to peace and the ancient Gods, even after I had carried out the commands of the High Council, and taken her to wife.

===Chinese===

她是在嘲弄我吗？还是当真的？我说不准。有时她指出，她的成功与凯旋不过是思想与才思的报偿；下一刻，她又给我一个根本讲不通的解释，让我自己去推想：她必定非止凡人，否则这法子绝无从发现。说实在的，这个心智柔韧、身段也柔韧的小女人，我在她身边待得越久，就越发被她弄得云山雾罩；而想凭我之手让亚特兰蒂斯重归太平、重回古老诸神之怀，我也越发灰心——哪怕在我执行了祭司最高议事会的训令、娶她为妻之后，也是如此。

===Original===

Only one plan seemed humanly possible, and that was to curb her further mischievousness by death and then leave the wretched country naturally to recover. It was just a dagger-stroke, and the thing was done. Yet the very idea of this revolted me, and when the desperate thought came to my mind (which it did ever and anon), I hugged to myself the answer that if it were fitting to do this thing, the High Gods in Their infinite wisdom would surely have put definite commands upon me for its carrying out.

===Chinese===

人力可行的方略看来只有一条：用死亡遏住她进一步的为祸，然后让这可怜的国家自然而然地休养生息。不过一匕首下去，事情便告完成。可这念头本身就令我憎恶；每当这孤注一掷的想法又冒上心头（它总是不时冒上来），我便抱着这样一句答复自我宽慰：倘若此举当真合宜，至上诸神以其无穷的智慧，必定早已给我下达明令，命我执行。

===Original===

Yet, such was the fascination of Phorenice, that when presently we left her gold collectors, and stumbled into such peril, that a little withholding of my hand would have gained her a passage to the nether Gods, I found myself fighting when she called upon me, as seldom I have fought before. And though, of course, some blame for this must be laid upon that lust of battle which thrills even the coldest of us when blows begin to whistle and war-cries start to ring, there is no doubt also that the pleasure of protecting Phorenice, and the distaste for seeing her pulled down by those rude, uncouth fishers put special nerve and vehemence into my blows.

===Chinese===

然而福伦尼刻的魔力就是如此：随后我们离开她的集金所，一头撞进了那样的险境——只要我稍稍袖一袖手，便可送她去冥间诸神那里——可当她召唤我时，我竟奋勇厮杀，狠过我平生少有的几回。当然，这其中有一部分得归咎于那股嗜战的血热：兵刃开始呼啸、喊杀声开始震响时，连我们中间最冷静的人也会为之激颤；但同样毫无疑问的是，护卫福伦尼刻的快意，和眼看她要被那些粗野笨拙的渔人拖倒的憎厌，给我的击打添了额外的狠劲与猛力。

===Original===

The cause of the matter was the unrest and the prevalency to street violence which I have spoken of above, and the desperate poverty of the common people, which led them to take any risk if it showed them a chance of winning the wherewithal to purchase a meal. We had once more mounted the litter, and once more the bearers, with their heads beneath the pole, bore us on at their accustomed swinging trot. Phorenice was telling me about her new supplies of gold. She had made fresh sumptuary laws, it appeared.

===Chinese===

事情的起因，便是我上文说过的那种动荡与街头暴乱的盛行，再加上平民赤贫已极——只要能见到一线挣得一顿饭钱的指望，他们便肯冒任何风险。我们又上了轿，轿夫们又把头颈拱在杠下，照他们惯常那种摇摆的小跑抬着我们前行。福伦尼刻正对我讲她黄金的新进项。看样子，她又新订了几条奢侈禁令（sumptuary laws）。

===Original===

“In the old days,” said she, “when yellow gold was tediously dredged up grain by grain from river gravels in the dangerous lands, a quill full would cost a rich man’s savings, and so none but those whose high station fitted them to be so adorned could wear golden ornaments. But when the seawater gave me gold here by the double handful a day, I found that the price of these river hoards decreased, and one day﻿—could you credit it?﻿—a common fellow, who was one of my smiths, came to me wearing a collar of yellow gold on his own common neck. Well, I had that neck divided, as payment for his presumption; and as I promised to repeat the division promptly on all other offenders, that special species of forwardness seems to be checked for the time. There are many exasperations, Deucalion, in governing these common people.”

===Chinese===

「旧时，」她道，「黄澄澄的金子，要人们在险地的河沙里一粒一粒烦闷地淘出来，一小羽毛管的分量便要吃掉一个富人的积蓄，所以唯有多少高门显位、配得上这般装点的人，才佩戴黄金饰物。可自从海水在这儿一天给我两捧两捧地送上黄金，我便发觉这些河藏的价钱跌了下来。后来有一天——你能信么？——一个下等人，我金匠作坊里的一个匠人，竟在自己那根下等脖颈上戴着一圈黄金领圈来见我。好啊，我便叫人把那根脖子分了家，算作对他僭越的偿价；我又许下话来，其余犯规的人一律照此即刻分家，于是这一路冒进的歪风，眼下总算是刹住了。德乌卡利翁，治理这些小民，气人的事可多着呢。」

===Original===

She had other things to say upon the matter, but at this point I saw two clumsy boats of fishers paddling to us from over the ripples, and at the same time amongst the narrow lanes which led between the houses on the other side of us, savage-faced men were beginning to run after the litter in threatening clusters.

===Chinese===

这事她还有别的话要说，可就在这时，我看见两条渔人的笨拙小船拨开涟漪朝我们划来；与此同时，在我们另一侧房屋之间的窄巷里，凶相毕露的汉子们正成群结伙、气势汹汹地追赶着轿子。

===Original===

“With permission,” I said, “I will step out of the conveyance and scatter this rabble.”

===Chinese===

「还请恕我自专，」我说，「我这就下轿，把这群乌合之众轰散。」

===Original===

“Oh, the people always cluster round me. Poor ugly souls, they seem to take a strange delight in coming to stare at my pretty looks. But scatter them. I have said I did not wish to be followed. I am taking holiday now, Deucalion, am I not, whilst you learn to woo me?”

===Chinese===

「哦，百姓们总要围拢到我身边来。这些可怜的丑八怪，来一睹我的花容月貌，于他们仿佛是一桩奇异的乐事。不过轰散就轰散吧。我说过我不愿被人跟着。我这会儿是在休假，德乌卡利翁，不是吗——趁着这工夫，你正好学学怎么向我求爱？」

===Original===

I stepped to the ground. The rough fishers in the boats were beginning to shout to those who dodged amongst the houses to see to it that we did not escape, and the numbers who hemmed us in on the shore side were increasing every moment. The prospect was unpleasant enough. We had come out beyond the merchants’ quarters, and were level with those small huts of mud and grass which the fishing population deem sufficient for shelter, and which has always been a spot where turbulence might be expected. Indeed, even in those days of peace and good government in the old King’s time, this part of the city had rarely been without its weekly riot.

===Chinese===

我跨到地上。船里那些粗野的渔人开始朝房屋间躲闪的同伙吆喝，叫他们看住我们，别让我们逃脱；从岸上一面围拢过来的人数，每一刻都在增加。前景相当不妙。我们已行出商贾聚居的地界，到了与那些泥墙草顶小屋平齐的地方——打渔人家觉得这样的栖身之屋已尽够用——而这一带从来就是个可能闹乱子的所在。说真的，即便在老国王治下太平善政的那些年月，城中的这一隅也很少有哪个星期不闹上一场暴乱。

===Original===

The life of the fisherman is the most hard that any human toilers have to endure. Violence from the wind and waves, and pelting from firestones out of the sky are their daily portion; the great beasts that dwell in the seas hunt them with savage persistence, and it is a rare day when at least someone of the fishers’ guild fails to come home to answer the tally.

===Chinese===

渔人的生活，是人间一切劳作者当中最苦的一种。风浪的暴虐、天上火石（firestones）的砸击，是他们每日的定份；栖居海中的巨兽怀着凶残的执拗猎食他们，渔人行会（fishers' guild）里难得有一天不至少短少一个人回来应点。

===Original===

Moreover, the manner which prevails of catching fish is not without its risks.

===Chinese===

再者，时下通行的捕鱼之法，也并非全无风险。

===Original===

To each man there is a large sea-fowl taken as a nestling, and trained to the work. A ring of bronze is round its neck to prevent its swallowing the spoil for which it dives, and for each fish it takes and flies back with to the boat, the head and tail and inwards are given to it for a reward, the ring being removed whilst it makes the meal.

===Chinese===

每个渔人手里都有一只雏鸟时便捉来、训练熟了干这活计的大海鸟（sea-fowl）。它颈上箍着一圈青铜环，免得它把潜下水去捉的渔获吞进自己肚里；它每衔起一条鱼飞回船边，人们便把鱼头、鱼尾和内脏赏给它作犒劳，让它进餐时取下铜环。

===Original===

The birds are faithful, once they have got a training, and are seldom known to desert their owners; but, although the fishers treat them more kindly than they do their wives, or children of their own begetting, the life of the birds is precarious like that of their masters. The larger beasts and fish of the sea prey on them as they prey on the smaller fish, and so whatever care may be lavished upon them, they are most liable to sudden cutting off.

===Chinese===

这些鸟一旦受了训练，便很忠心，鲜有背弃主人的名声；不过，渔人待它们虽比待自己的妻儿还亲厚，鸟的性命却和主人一样朝不保夕。海中更大的巨兽和大鱼捕食它们，正如它们捕食更小的鱼；所以无论人们把怎样的疼爱倾注在它们身上，它们总是极易骤然丧命。

===Original===

And here is another thing that makes the life of the fisher most precarious: if his fishing bird be slain, and the second which he has in training also come by ill fortune, he is left suddenly bereft of all utensils of livelihood, and (for aught his guild-fellows care) he may go starve. For these fishers hold that the Gods of the sea regulate their craft, and that if one is not pleasing to Them They rob him of his birds; after which it would be impious to have any truck or dealing with such a fellow; and accordingly he is left to starve or rob as he chooses.

===Chinese===

还有一桩事，把渔人的性命愈发悬在一线：他的渔鸟（fishing bird）若被杀了，正在训练的第二只又遭了横祸，他便骤然丧尽了一切谋生的家伙什，（行会的同伙是不管的）便只好挨饿去。因为这些渔人认定，海中诸神掌管着他们这门营生；谁若不讨诸神的欢心，诸神便夺走他的鸟；此后再与这等人有半点瓜葛往来，便是渎神；于是他就被撇下，或饿死，或为盗，任凭他自选。

===Original===

All of which circumstances tend to make the fishers rude, desperate men, who have been forced into the trade because all other callings have rejected them. They are fellows, moreover, who will spend the gains of a month on a night’s debauch, for fear that the morrow will rob them of life and the chance of spending; and, moreover, it is their one point of honour to be curbed in no desire by an ordinary fear of consequences. As will appear.

===Chinese===

凡此种种，都把渔人陶冶成粗野亡命之徒——他们是被一切别的行当拒之门外，才被迫吃上这碗饭的。再说，这帮家伙肯把一个月的进项花在一夜的荒唐上，生怕明朝夺走他们的性命和花钱的机会；而且，不受寻常的后怕拘束，正是他们唯一讲究的荣誉点。下文自见分晓。

===Original===

I went quickly towards the largest knot of these people, who were skulking behind the houses, leaving the litter halted in the path behind me, and I bade them sharply enough to disperse. “For an employment,” I added, “put your houses in order, and clean the fish offal from the lanes between them. Tomorrow I will come round here to inspect, and put this quarter into a better order. But for today the Empress (whose name be adored) wishes for a privacy, so cease your staring.”

===Chinese===

我快步走向人堆最密的一团——他们正躲在房屋后面探头探脑——把轿子留在身后的小径上停着，厉声喝令他们散开。「给你们派个正经活计，」我添了一句，「把各自的屋子收拾利落，把巷子里的鱼肚鱼肠清扫干净。明天我会来这一带巡查，把这地方整顿得像样些。至于今天，女皇（愿她的圣名受万世敬仰）要图个清静，所以收起你们那副贼眼。」

===Original===

“Then give us money,” said a shrill voice from amongst the huts.

===Chinese===

「那就给钱，」小屋之间一个尖利的嗓门嚷道。

===Original===

“I will send you a torch in an hour’s time,” I said grimly, “and rig you a gallows, if you give me more annoyance. To your kennels, you!”

===Chinese===

「一个钟头后，我就给你们送一支火把，」我冷冷道，「你们再敢搅扰，我还给你们搭一座绞架。滚回你们的狗窝去！」

===Original===

I think they would have obeyed the voice of authority if they had been left to themselves. There was a quick stir amongst them. Those that stood in the sunlight instinctively slipped into the shadow, and many dodged into the houses and cowered in dark corners out of sight. But the men in the two hide-covered fisher-boats that were paddling up, called them back with boisterous cries.

===Chinese===

我想，倘若任其自便，他们是会服从这威严的声口的。人群里起了一阵快速的骚动。站在日头底下的那些人本能地溜进了阴影，许多人闪身躲进屋里，缩在看不见的黑暗角落里。可是那两条蒙着兽皮的渔船划上来，船里的汉子们又用粗声大气的呼喊把他们召了回去。

===Original===

I signed to the litter-bearers to move on quickly along their road. There was need of discipline here, and I was minded to deal it out myself with a firm hand. I judged that I could prevent them following the Empress, but if she still remained as a glittering bait for them to rob, and I had to protect her also, it might be that my work would not be done so effectively.

===Chinese===

我打手势叫轿夫们顺路赶紧前行。此地需要一顿教训，我打算亲手用铁腕发放。我料定自己能够拦住他们不追女皇；可她若还留在那里，当着一件亮闪闪的诱人赃物，我又得分神护她，那我的活计兴许就干不那么利索了。

===Original===

But it seems I was presumptuous in giving an order which dealt with the person of Phorenice. She bade the bearers stand where they were, and stepped out, and drew her weapons from beneath the cushions. She came towards me strapping a sword on to her hip, and carrying a well-dinted target of gold on her left forearm. “An unfair trick,” cries she, laughing. “If you will keep a fight to yourself now, Deucalion, where will your greediness carry you when I am your shrinking, wistful little wife? Are these fools truly going to stand up against us?”

===Chinese===

可看来，我竟擅自发号施令，管到福伦尼刻头上，是僭越了。她吩咐轿夫原地站住，自己跨出轿来，从坐垫底下抽出兵器。她朝我走来，一面把一柄剑挂上胯骨，左小臂上挎着一面瘢痕累累的金圆盾。「不地道呀，」她笑着嚷道，「德乌卡利翁，你现在就把一场厮杀独吞了，等我成了你那位娇怯怯、意切切的小娘子，你的贪心还不知要涨到哪一步去呢？这些蠢货当真要跟我们动手吗？」

===Original===

I was not coveting a fight, but it seemed as if there would be no avoidance of it now. The robe and the glittering gauds of which Phorenice had recently despoiled the merchant, drew the eyes of these people with keen attraction. The fishers in the boats paddled into the surf which edged the beach, and leaped overside and left the frail basket-work structures to be spewed up sound or smashed, as chance ordered. And from the houses, and from the filthy lanes between them, poured out hordes of others, women mixed with the men, gathering round us threateningly.

===Chinese===

我并不贪求一场厮杀，可眼下属实已无从避开了。福伦尼刻新近从那商人身上剥来的衣袍与亮闪闪的饰物，把这干人眼里的贪火勾得炽旺。船上的渔人把船划进镶在滩边的浪沫里，翻身越过船舷跳下，任那两副脆弱的柳条骨架凭浪头摆布，或完好吐上岸，或砸个粉碎。而从那些房屋里、从房屋之间污秽的窄巷里，又涌出一伙伙旁的人，女的混在男的当中，气势汹汹地朝我们围拢过来。

===Original===

“Have a care,” shouted one on the outskirts of the crowd. “She called down fire for the sacrifice once today, and she can burn up others here if she chooses.”

===Chinese===

「留神，」人群外圈有一人高喊，「她今天已为祭牲引过一回天火了；她要是乐意，在这儿也能把其余的人烧成灰。」

===Original===

“So much the more for those that are left,” retorted another. “She cannot burn all.”

===Chinese===

「那剩下的人不正好多得些，」另一人反唇相讥，「她又烧不尽所有人。」

===Original===

“Nay, I will not burn any,” said Phorenice, “but you shall look upon my swordplay till you are tired.”

===Chinese===

「不，我一个也不烧，」福伦尼刻道，「只是我的剑法，你们要看到腻烦为止。」

===Original===

I heard her say that with some malicious amusement, knowing (as one of the Seven) how she had called down the fires of the sky to burn that cloven-hoofed horse offered in sacrifice, and knowing too, full well, that she could bring down no fire here. But they gave us little enough time for wordy courtesies. Their Empress never went far unattended, and, for aught the wretches knew, an escort might be close behind. So what pilfering they did, it behoved them to get done quickly.

===Chinese===

听她这话，我心里怀着几分恶意的兴味——我深知她是怎样引下天火，烧掉那匹献作祭牲的偶蹄马的，也深知她在这里半点天火也引不下来。可他们没给我们多少工夫来斗口客套。他们的女皇出行从不会走远而无随侍，这些可怜虫拿不准扈从是不是就跟在近处。所以，要偷要抢，便得赶紧下手。

===Original===

They closed in, jostling one another to be first, and the reek of their filthy bodies made us cough. A grimy hand launched out to seize some of the jewels which flashed on Phorenice’s breast, and I lopped it off at the elbow, so that it fell at her feet, and a second later we were engaged.

===Chinese===

他们围逼上来，你推我搡抢在前头，那一身身脏臭皮囊的气味熏得我们直咳嗽。一只污黑的手探出来，要抓福伦尼刻胸前闪亮的珠宝，我一剑把它从肘部剁了下来，那手便落在她脚边；下一瞬，我们便交上了手。

===Original===

“Your back to mine, comrade,” cried she, with a laugh, and then drew and laid about her with fine dexterity. Bah! but it was mere slaughter, that first bout.

===Chinese===

「你的背贴上我的背，伙计，」她笑着喊道，随即拔剑在手，身手伶俐地四下里挥砍。呸！头一回合，简直就是一面倒的屠戮。

===Original===

The crowd hustled inwards with such greediness to seize what they could, that none had space to draw back elbow for a thrust, and we two kept a circle round us by sheer whirling of steel. It is necessary to do one’s work cleanly in these bouts, as wounded left on the ground unnoticed before one are as dangerous as so many snakes. But as we circled round in our battling I noted that all of Phorenice’s quarry lay peaceful and still. By the Gods! but she could play a fine sword, this dainty Empress. She touched life with every thrust.

===Chinese===

人群贪婪地向里挤，人人都想抢捞一把，结果谁也没有抽肘送剑的空当，我们两个全凭一片翻飞的刀光剑影，在身周守住了一个圆圈。这等混战里，活必须干得干净利落，因为落在身前地上、无人留意的伤者，就像一条条毒蛇一样危险。可我在厮杀中转着圈时，留意到福伦尼刻剑下的猎物个个躺得安安静静。诸神啊！这位玲珑娇艳的女皇，一手剑耍得真叫漂亮。她每一刺都点到命门。

===Original===

Yes, it was plain to see, now an example was given, that the throne of Atlantis had been won, not by a lovely face and a subtle tongue alone; and (as a fighter myself) I did not like Phorenice the less for the knowledge. I could but see her out of the corner of my eye, and that only now and again, for the fishers, despite their ill-knowledge of fence, and the clumsiness of their weapons, had heavy numbers, and most savage ferocity; and as they made so confident of being able to pull us down, it required more than a little hard battling to keep them from doing it. Ay, by the Gods! it was at times a fight my heart warmed to, and if I had not contrived to pluck a shield from one fool who came too vain-gloriously near me with one, I could not swear they would not have dragged me down by sheer ravening savageness.

===Chinese===

是的，如今有了实例摆在眼前，谁都看得分明：亚特兰蒂斯的御座，并非单凭一张俏脸和一条巧舌赢来的；而我自己也是习武之人，知道了这一点，对福伦尼刻的喜欢非但不减，反而更增。我只能用眼角余光瞥她，还只能是偶尔一瞥，因为这些渔人虽然剑术拙劣、兵器粗笨，人数却压得死沉，凶暴至极；他们既那般自信能把我们扑倒，要叫他们扑不成，就少不了一场硬仗。是啊，凭诸神起誓！这一场厮杀有时打得我心头发热；倘若我没有从一个过于逞英雄、举着盾逼得太近的蠢货手里夺下一面盾，我可不敢起誓说，他们那种饿狼般的凶蛮就不会把我生生拖倒。

===Original===

And always above the burly uproar of the fight came very pleasantly to my ears Phorenice’s cry of “Deucalion!” which she chose as her battle shout. I knew her, of course, to be a past-mistress of the art of compliment, and it was no new thing for me to hear the name roared out above a battle din, but it was given there under circumstances which were peculiar, and for the life of me I could not help being tickled by the flattery.

===Chinese===

而在那场打斗粗粝的喧腾之上，福伦尼刻一声声「德乌卡利翁！」总是悦耳地传进我耳中——她把它选作了自己的战场呐喊。我自然知道，她是谀颂之道的一位宗师；在战阵的喧嚣之上听人吼出这个名字，于我也并非新鲜事；可它此刻响起的情境实在别致，任我怎样克制，也禁不住被这记奉承搔得心痒。

===Original===

Condemn my weakness how you will, but I came very near then to liking the Empress of Atlantis in the way she wished. And as for that other woman who should have filled my mind, I will confess that the stress of the moment, and the fury of the engagement, had driven both her and her strait completely out beyond the marches of my memory. Of such frail stuff are we made, even those of us who esteem ourselves the strongest.

===Chinese===

随你们怎样谴责我的软弱吧，可在那一刻，我已险些照福伦尼刻指望的那般，当真喜欢上这位亚特兰蒂斯的女皇了。至于那位本该占满我心智的另一个女人，我供认不讳：那一刻的紧张和接战的狂烈，早把她和她的困境一并逐出了我记忆的边疆之外。我们就是用这样脆弱的材料做成的，连我们中间那些自诩最强的人，也不例外。

===Original===

Now it is a temptation few men born to the sword can resist, to throw themselves heart and soul into a fight for a fight’s sake, and it seems that women can be bitten with the same fierce infection. The attack slackened and halted. We stood in the middle of a ring of twisted dead, and the rest of the fishers and their women who hemmed us in shrank back out of reach of our weapons.

===Chinese===

为打而打、全身心扑进一场厮杀，这份诱惑，天生佩剑之人少有抵挡得住的；看来女人也同样会被这凶猛的热病咬中。攻势松懈了下来，停住了。我们站在一圈横七竖八的尸首中央，其余的渔人和他们的女人把我们团团围住，却缩回了我们兵器够不着的地方。

===Original===

It was the moment for a truce, and the moment when a few strong words would have sent them back cowering to their huts, and given us free passage to go where we chose. But no, this Phorenice must needs sing a hymn to her sword and mine, gloating over our feats and invulnerability; and then she must needs ask payment for the bearers of her litter whom they had killed, and then speak balefully of the burnings, and the skinnings, and the sawings asunder with which this fishers’ quarter would be treated in the near future, till they learned the virtues of deportment and genteel manners.

===Chinese===

这正是讲和的时刻，也是几句斩钉截铁的话便可把他们赶回小屋瑟缩、给我们让出随便去哪儿的通路的时刻。可是不，我们这位福伦尼刻偏要对着她和我两柄剑唱一首颂歌，夸耀我们的武功和刀枪不入；接着又偏要向他们讨还轿夫的人命钱——轿夫已叫他们杀了；然后又恶狠狠地历数这渔人区不久将受的待遇：烧的烧，剥皮的剥皮，锯成两截的锯成两截，直到他们学会举止规矩与斯文礼数的种种美德。

===Original===

“It makes your backs creep, does it?” said Phorenice. “I do not wonder. This severity must have its unpleasant side. But why do you not put it beyond my power to give the order? Either you must think yourselves Gods or me no Goddess, or you would not have gone on so far. Come now, you nasty-smelling people, follow out your theory, and if you make a good fight of it, I swear by my face I will be lenient with those who do not fall.”

===Chinese===

「听得脊背发凉了，是吗？」福伦尼刻道，「我不奇怪。这等严刑总免不了有叫人不痛快的一面。可你们为什么不干脆叫我下不了这道命令呢？要不是你们把自己当成诸神，或把我当成假女神，断不至于闹到这般地步。来吧，你们这些浑身臭气的人，把你们的道理行到底；要是你们打得像样，我凭我这张脸起誓，对倒不下的人一定从宽发落。」

===Original===

But there was no pressing up to meet our swords. They still ringed us in, savage and sullen, beyond the ring of their own dead, and would neither run back to the houses, nor give us the game of further fight. There was a certain stubborn bravery about them that one could not but admire, and for myself I determined that next time it became my duty to raise troops, I would catch a handful of these men, and teach them handiness with the utensils of war, and train them to loyalty and faithfulness. But presently from behind their ranks a stone flew, and though it whizzed between the Empress and myself, and struck down a fisher, it showed that they had brought a new method into their attack, and it behoved us to take thought and meet it.

===Chinese===

可是并没有人上来迎战我们的剑。他们仍旧把我们围在圈里，凶悍而阴沉，隔着自家死人的那道环，既不肯逃回屋去，也不肯再给我们一场厮杀的戏乐。他们身上有一股子倔强的胆气，令人不能不佩服；就我自己而言，我已打定主意，下回轮到我奉命募兵，定要抓来一小撮这样的人，教他们熟练运用战争的器械，把他们训得忠贞可靠。可不多时，一石块从他们队伍后面飞来，虽则它嗖地从女皇和我之间穿过去，放倒了一个渔人，却表明他们已给进攻添了新招，我们不得不掂量掂量、想个对策。

===Original===

I looked round me up and down the beach. There was no sign of a rescue. “Phorenice,” I said in the court tongue, which these barbarous fishers would know little enough of, “I take it that a whiff of the sea-breeze would come very pleasant after all this warm play. As you can show such pretty sword work, will you cut me a way down to the beach, and I will do my poor best to keep these creatures from snapping at our heels?”

===Chinese===

我朝滩头滩尾环顾了一遍。全无救兵的踪影。「福伦尼刻，」我用宫廷语（court tongue）说——这些野蛮渔人听不懂几句——「我想，经过这一场热身的戏耍，来一口海风必定十分受用。你的剑招这般漂亮，可否替我砍开一条通到滩头的路？我也好尽我菲薄之力，防着这些畜生咬我们的脚跟？」

===Original===

“Oh!” cried she. “Then I am to have a courtier for a husband after all. Why have you kept back your flattering speeches till now? Is that your trick to make me love you?”

===Chinese===

「哦！」她叫起来，「这么说，我到底还是要嫁一个朝臣做丈夫了。你怎么把奉承话扣到现在才说？这就是你哄我爱你的手段吗？」

===Original===

“I will think out the reason for it another time.”

===Chinese===

「这缘由，我改日再想。」

===Original===

“Ah, these stern, commanding husbands,” said she, “how they do press upon their little wives!” and with that leaped over the ring of dead before her, and cut and stabbed a way through those that stood between her and the waters which creamed and crashed upon the beach. Gods! what a charge she made. It made me tingle with admiration as I followed sideways behind her, guarding the rear. And I am a man that has spent so many years in battling, that it takes something far out of the common to move me to any enthusiasm in this matter.

===Chinese===

「啊，这些威风凛凛、发号施令的丈夫们，」她道，「他们压起自家小娘子来可真够呛！」话音未落，她已跃过身前的死人环，又劈又刺，从拦在她与那拍岸翻腾的浪头之间的人堆里杀出一条路来。诸神啊！她那一冲何等了得。我在她侧后方跟着、断后，佩服得浑身发麻。我这个人，戎马半生，要在这上头激起半点热忱，非得有些远超寻常的东西不可。

===Original===

There were two boats creaking and washing about in the edge of the surf, but in one, happily, the wickerwork which made its frame was crushed by the weight of the waves into a shapeless bundle of sticks, and would take half a day to replace. So that, let us but get the other craft afloat, and we should be free from further embroiling. But the fishers were quick to see the object of this new manoeuvre. “Guard the boat,” they shouted. “Smash her; slit her skin with your knives! Tear her with your fingers! Swim her out to sea! Oh, at least take the paddles!”

===Chinese===

浪沫边上还有两条船在吱嘎摇晃，幸而其中一条的柳条骨架已被浪头的分量压成一捆不成形的柴棒，修好它得费半天工夫。这样一来，只要我们把另一条弄下水，便可脱开这场纠缠。可渔人们眼尖，一眼看穿这新调度的用意。「守住船，」他们大喊，「砸烂它！拿刀子割开它的皮！用手指撕了它！把它拖到海里去！哦，至少把桨抢走！」

===Original===

But, if these clumsy fishers could run, Phorenice was like a legged snake for speed. She was down beside the boat before any could reach it, laughing and shouting out that she could beat them at every point. Myself, I was slower of foot; and, besides, there was some that offered me a fight on the road, and I was not wishful to baulk them; and moreover, the fewer we left clamouring behind, the fewer there would be to speed our going with their stones. Still I came to the beach in good order, and laid hands on the flimsy boat and tipped her dry.

===Chinese===

不过，这些笨手笨脚的渔人固然能跑，比起脚程，福伦尼刻却像一条长了腿的蛇。谁都还没挨着船边，她已先到了，一路笑着嚷着，说样样都要赢过他们。我自己脚头慢些；再者，路上还有人主动要陪我过招，我也不愿拂了他们的好意；何况，留在身后叫嚣的越少，用石块给我们的离场助兴的也就越少。饶是如此，我仍好整以暇地到了海滩边，一把攥住那条单薄的小船，把里面的水倒干。

===Original===

“Fighting is no trade for me,” I cried, “whilst you are here, Phorenice. Guard me my back and walk out into the water.”

===Chinese===

「有你在这儿，福伦尼刻，厮杀这行当我就不干了，」我喊道，「替我守住背后，往水里走吧。」

===Original===

I took the boat, thrusting it afloat, and wading with it till two lines of the surf were past. The fishers swarmed round us, active as fish in their native element, and strove mightily to get hands on the boat and slit the hides which covered it with their eager fingers. But I had a spare hand, and a short stabbing-knife for such close-quarter work, and here, there, and everywhere was Phorenice the Empress, with her thirsty dripping sword. By the Gods! I laughed with sheer delight at seeing her art of fence.

===Chinese===

我推着船，一边趟水，直到越过两道浪线。渔人们围着我们蜂拥打转，在水里灵活得如同游鱼归了本行，拼命要把手搭上船舷，用急切的指头割开蒙船的兽皮。好在我还腾得出一只手，还有一柄专用于这等贴身近斗的短刺刀；而女皇福伦尼刻东一剑西一剑，无处不在，那柄饮血滴水的剑从不停歇。凭诸神起誓！看她这身剑术，我由衷地笑出了声。

===Original===

But the swirl of a great fish into the shallows, and the squeal of a fisher as he was dragged down and home away into the deep, made me mindful of foes that no skill can conquer, and no bravery avoid. Without taking time to give the Empress a word of warning, I stooped, and flung an arm round her, and threw her up out of the water into the boat, and then thrust on with all my might, driving the flimsy craft out to sea, whilst my legs crept under me for fear of the beasts which swam invisible beneath the muddied waters.

===Chinese===

可是一条大鱼搅着水花卷进浅滩，一个渔人尖叫着被拖下去、拖进深水老巢，这景象提醒了我：还有技能无从战胜、勇力无从躲开的对头。我来不及给女皇一句警告，便俯下身，一把搂住她，把她从水里抛进船中，随即拼尽全力往前推，把这条单薄的小船直往外海送去；与此同时，我的两条腿在身子底下直打软——唯恐那些看不见的巨兽正在搅浑的水底游弋。

===Original===

To the fishers, inured to these horrid perils by daily association, the seizing of one of their number meant little, and they pressed on, careless of their dull lives, eager only to snatch the jewels which still flaunted on Phorenice’s breast. Of the vengeance that might come after they recked nothing; let them but get the wherewithal for one night’s good debauch, and they would forget that such a thing as the morning of a morrow could have existence.

===Chinese===

渔人们日日与这类可怖的凶险厮混，早已处之泰然，同类被卷走一个，在他们算不得什么；他们继续扑上来，把自家那条贱命置之度外，眼里只惦着福伦尼刻胸前仍招摇闪亮的珠宝。事后会遭什么报复，他们全不理会；只要捞到够痛痛快快荒唐一夜的盘缠，他们连「明天还有早晨」这种事的存在，都会忘个一干二净。

===Original===

Two fellows I caught and killed that, diving down beneath, tried to slit the skin of the boat out of sight under the water; and Phorenice cared for all those that tried to put a hand on the gunwales. Yes, and she did more than that. A huge long-necked turtle that was stirred out of the mud by the turmoil, came up to daylight, and swung its great horn-lipped mouth to this side and that, seeking for a prey. The fishers near it dodged and dived. I, thrusting at the stern of the boat, could only hope it would pass me by and so offered an easy mark. It scurried towards me, champing its noisy lips, and beating the water into spray with its flippers.

===Chinese===

有两个家伙潜到水下，想在水底人不知鬼不觉地割开船皮，叫我逮住杀了；凡是想往船舷上搭一搭手的，福伦尼刻都一一打发了。是的，她干的不止这些。一头被这场骚乱从泥里搅起的巨型长颈龟浮上亮水，那张角质阔嘴左右摆荡，寻觅猎物。它近旁的渔人闪转腾挪、扎水逃避。我在船尾推着船，只能指望它放我过去，因为我正是一个现成的易袭之靶。它咂着那张聒噪的嘴朝我冲来，鳍肢把水拍成飞沫。

===Original===

But Phorenice was quick with a remedy and a rescue. She passed her sword through one of the fishers that pressed her, and then thrust the body towards the turtle. The great neck swooped towards it; the long slimy feelers which protruded from its head quivered and snuffled; and then the horny green jaws crunched on it, and drew it down out of sight.

===Chinese===

可福伦尼刻救急解围来得飞快。她一剑捅穿一个逼近她的渔人，随即把那尸首朝巨龟推了过去。那巨颈倏地俯冲下来；头上探出的黏滑长须颤动着四下嗅探；随即那两片角质的绿颚咔嚓一合，把它拖下水去，没了踪影。

===Original===

The boat was in deep water now, and Phorenice called upon me to come in over the side, she the while balancing nicely so that the flimsy thing should not be overset. The fishers had given up their pursuit, finding that they earned nothing but lopped-off arms and split faces by coming within swing of this terrible sword of their Empress, and so contented themselves with volleying jagged stones in the hopes of stunning us or splitting the boat. However, Phorenice crouched in the stern, holding the two shields﻿—her own golden target, and the rough hide buckler I had won﻿—and so protected both of us whilst I paddled, and though many stones clattered against the shields, and hit the hide covering of the boat, so that it resounded like a drum, none of them did damage, and we drew quickly out of their range.

===Chinese===

船已进了深水，福伦尼刻唤我从船舷翻进去，她则细心地稳着重心，免得这单薄的玩意儿翻掉。渔人们已放弃了追赶——他们发现，只要进入女皇这柄可怕长剑的挥及范围，挣到的便只有断臂和开花的脸——于是便改为成阵投掷尖棱的石块，指望砸晕我们，或把船砸裂。好在福伦尼刻蹲在船尾，高举那两面盾——她自己那面金圆盾，和我夺来的那面粗皮盾——在我划桨时把我们两个都护住了；许多石块哐哐当当地砸在盾上，砸在蒙船的兽皮上，震得船皮咚咚如鼓，却没有一块伤得了我们，我们很快便驶出了他们的射程。

## XII. The Drug of Our Lady the Moon / 十二　月神夫人的迷药
===Original===

Our Lord the Sun was riding towards the end of His day, and the smoke from a burning mountain fanned black and forbidding before His face. Phorenice wrung the water from her clothes and shivered. “Work hard with those paddles, Deucalion, and take me in through the water-gate and let me be restored to my comforts again. That merchant would rue if he saw how his pretty garments were spoiled, and I rue, too, being a woman, and remembering that he at least has no others I can take in place of these.” She looked at me sidelong, tossing back the short red hair from her eyes. “What think you of my wisdom in coming where we have come without an escort?”

===Chinese===

太阳神主正驰向祂这一日的尽头，一座燃烧的山喷出的浓烟，黑沉沉地在祂面前扇展开来，大煞风景。福伦尼刻拧着衣裳上的水，打了个寒噤。「德乌卡利翁，好好划你的桨，带我进水门，让我重新回到我的安逸里去。那商人若是瞧见他那身漂亮衣裳糟蹋成了什么样子，准会懊悔不迭；我是个女人，也懊悔——何况我还记得，他家里再没有旁的衣裳，可供我拿来替换这一身。」她斜眼看我，把挡眼的短红发甩了回去。「我们这样不带一名护卫就跑到此地来，你对我这份明智作何评价？」

===Original===

“The Empress can do no wrong,” I quoted the old formula with a smile.

===Chinese===

「女皇是不会做错的。」我微笑着引了那句老套话。

===Original===

“At least I have shown you that I can fight. I caught you looking your approval of me quite pleasantly once or twice. You were a difficult man to thaw, Deucalion, but you warm perceptibly as you keep on being near me. La, sir, we shall be a pair of rustic sweethearts yet, if this goes on. I am glad I thought of the device of going near those smelly fishers.”

===Chinese===

「至少我已经向你表明，我是能打仗的。有一两回，我还撞见你目光里颇为愉快地流露出对我的赞许。你这人很难焐热，德乌卡利翁，可你在我身边待得久了，也明显地回暖了。哎呀，先生，再这样下去，我们真要变成一对乡下情侣了。幸亏我灵机一动，想出到那些臭烘烘的渔人跟前去的妙计。」

===Original===

So she had taken me out in the litter unattended for the plain purpose of inviting a fight, and showing me her skill at arms, and perhaps, too, of seeing in person how I also carried myself in a moment of stress. Well, if we were to live on together as husband and wife, it was good that each should know to a nicety the other’s powers; and also, I am too much of an old battler and too much enamoured with the glorious handling of arms to quarrel very deeply with anyone who offers me a tough upstanding fight. Still for the life of me, I could not help comparing Phorenice with another woman. With a similar chance open before us, Nais had robbed me of the struggle through a sheer pity for those squalid rebels who did not even call her chieftain; whilst here was this Empress frittering away two score of the hardiest of her subjects merely to gratify a whim.

===Chinese===

如此说来，她不带随从，用轿子把我抬出去，明摆着就是要招惹一场厮杀，好向我炫耀她的武艺，或许还要亲眼看看我在危急关头又是如何自处的。唉，倘若我们要以夫妻之实长相厮守，那么彼此把对方的本事摸个一清二楚，倒也不坏；何况我这人本就是个老于行伍的，又太迷恋兵刃交手的荣光，谁若肯与我痛痛快快打一场硬仗，我便与谁深怨不起来。可我拼了命也忍不住，总要把福伦尼刻同另一个女人相比。当年同样的机缘摆在我们面前，娜伊丝纯出于怜悯，生生剥夺了我那一场争斗——怜悯那帮连酋长都不肯认她的腌臜叛军；而这边这位女皇，却只为满足一时兴致，便糟蹋掉四十名她麾下最硬朗的臣民。

===Original===

Yet, loyal to my vow as a priest, and to the commands set upon me by the high council on the Sacred Mountain, I tried to put away these wayward thoughts and comparisons. As I rowed over the swingings of the waves towards the forts which guard the harbour’s mouth, I sent prayers to the High Gods to give my tongue dexterity, and They through Their love for the country of Atlantis, and the harassed people, whom it was my deep wish to serve, granted me that power of speech which Phorenice loved. Her eyes glowed upon me as I talked.

===Chinese===

然而，我既忠于祭司的誓愿，又忠于圣山上祭司最高议事会加于我的训令，便竭力摈除这些任性的念头与比较。我划桨越过荡漾的波涛，向扼守港口入口的堡垒驶去，一路向上天祷告，求至上诸神赋予我舌头以机变；祂们出于对亚特兰蒂斯国土的眷爱，对那些我心深处渴望效劳的困顿黎民的眷爱，赐予了我那种福伦尼刻所喜爱的口才。我说话的时候，她的双眼灼灼放光地望着我。

===Original===

This beach of the fishers where we had had our passage at arms is safe from ship attack from without, by reason of a chain of jagged rocks which spring up from the deep, and run from the harbour side to the end of the city wall. The fishers know the passes, and can oftentimes get through to the open water beyond without touching a stone; or if they do see a danger of hitting on the reef, leap out and carry their light boats in their hands till the water floats them again. But here I had neither the knowledge nor the dexterity, and, thought I, now the High Gods will show finally if They wish this woman who has defiled them to reign on in Atlantis, and if also They wish me to serve as her husband.

===Chinese===

我们刚才交过手的这片渔人滩头，外面的船只攻打不到，因为有一列嶙峋的礁石从深水中拔起，从港口一侧一直延到城墙尽头。渔人们熟悉水道，往往一路蹭不到一块石头，便穿出到外面的开阔水面；倘若眼见要撞上礁石，他们便跳下水，把轻巧的小船扛在手里，走到水再度浮得起来船的地方。可我于此道既无知识，也无巧技，我心想：如今至上诸神就要最终显明，祂们究竟愿不愿意让这个玷辱了祂们的女人继续君临亚特兰蒂斯，也愿不愿意让我做她的丈夫。

===Original===

I cried these things in my heart, and waited to receive the omen. There was no half-answer. A great wave rose in the lagoon behind us, a wave such as could have only been caused by an earth tremor, and on its sleek back we were hurled forward and thrown clear of the reefs with their seaweeds licking round us, without so much as seeing a stone of the barrier. I bowed my head as I rowed on towards the harbour forts. It was plain that not yet would the High Gods take vengeance for the insults which this lovely woman had offered Them.

===Chinese===

我在心里如此呼告，静候征兆降临。回答没有半点含糊。环礁湖里在我们身后腾起一道巨浪——这样的浪，只能是地皮震颤所致——浪头光滑的脊背把我们猛地掀向前去，抛过了礁石群，海藻在我们周身舔拂，而我们竟连那道屏障的一块石头也没瞧见。我朝港口的堡垒继续划去，一路垂着头。很明显，对于这个可爱女人加于祂们的种种侮辱，至上诸神还不到报仇的时候。

===Original===

The sentries in the two forts beat drums at one another in their accustomed rotation, and in the growing dusk were going to pay little enough attention to the fishingboat which lay against the great chain clamouring to have it lowered. But luckily a pair of officers were taking the air of the evening in a stone-dropping turret of the roof of the nearer fort, and these recognised the tone of our shouts. They silenced the drums, torches were lowered to make sure of our faces, and then with a splash the great chain was dropped into the water to give us passage.

===Chinese===

两座堡垒里的哨兵按他们惯常的轮次对击着鼓，暮色渐浓，对那条贴着大铁链、嚷嚷着要放链的小渔船，本不会怎么理会。所幸有一对军官正在较近那座堡垒屋顶的投石塔楼里乘晚凉，听出了我们喊话的腔调。他们叫停了鼓，火把放下来照清了我们的脸，随后哗啦一声，大铁链沉入水中，放我们通行。

===Original===

A galley lay inside, nuzzling the harbour wall, and presently the ladder of ropes was let down from the top of the nearest fort, and a crew came down to man the oars. There were the customary changes of raiment too, given as presents by the officers of the fort, and these we put on in the cabin of the galley in place of the sodden clothes we wore. There are fevers to be gained by carrying wet clothes after sunset, and though from personal experience I have learned that these may be warded off with drugs, I noticed with some grim amusement that the Empress had sufficiently little of the Goddess about her to fear very much the ailments which are due to frail humanity.

===Chinese===

港内泊着一条桨船，正偎着港湾的堤壁，不多时，绳梯从最近那座堡垒顶上放了下来，一队桨手下船来操桨。照例还有替换的衣裳，是堡垒的军官们赠送的礼物，我们便在桨船的舱里换上，替下身上湿透的衣裳。日落之后还穿着湿衣，是会招热病的；我虽凭切身经验晓得这些病可以用药物挡开，却也带着几分冷冷的兴味注意到：女皇身上女神的成分实在够少的，竟非常害怕这些源于凡人弱质的疾病。

===Original===

The galley rowed swiftly across the calm waters of the harbour, and made fast to the rings of gold on the royal quay, and whilst we were waiting for litters to be brought, I watched a lantern lit in the boat which stood guard over Phorenice’s mammoth. The huge red beast stood shoulder-deep in the harbour water, with trunk upturned. It was tamed now, and the light of the boat’s lantern fell on the little ripples sent out by its tremblings. But I did not choose to intercede or ask mercy for it. If the mammoth sank deeper in the harbour mud, and was swallowed, I could have borne the loss with equanimity.

===Chinese===

桨船飞快地划过平静的港面，系牢在王家码头的金环上。等轿子抬来的当口，我望着守看福伦尼刻那头猛犸的小船上点起了一盏灯笼。那头红色巨兽立在港水中，水深及肩，长鼻上扬。它如今驯服了，船上灯笼的光，正落在它微微战栗时荡出的一圈圈细涟上。可我不打算替它求情，也不打算替它讨饶。哪怕这猛犸陷进港湾的泥里越陷越深，整个被吞没，我也能心平气和地承受这损失。

===Original===

To tell the truth, that ride on the great beast’s back had impressed me unfavourably. In fact, it put into me a sense of helplessness that was well-nigh intolerable. Perhaps circumstances have made me unduly self-reliant: on that others must judge. But I will own to having a preference for walking on my own proper feet, as the Gods in fashioning our shapes most certainly intended. On my own feet I am able to guard my own head and neck, and have done on four continents, throughout a long and active life, and on many a thousand occasions. But on the back of that detestable mammoth, pah! I grew as nervous as a child or a dastard.

===Chinese===

说实话，在那巨兽背上骑的那一程，给我的印象很坏。它着实叫我生出一种几乎无法忍受的无助之感。或许是种种际遇把我造就得过分倚仗自己了：这要由旁人来评断。但我愿意承认，我偏爱用自己的脚走路——诸神塑造我们这副身形时，无疑正是这个用意。凭自己的一双脚，我能护住自己的头颅脖颈，在四大洲上，在漫长而活跃的一生里，在千百回的场合，我都做到了。可在那头可憎的猛犸背上——呸！我变得像个孩子、一个懦夫一样心惊胆战。

===Original===

However, I had little enough leisure for personal megrims just then. Whilst we waited, Phorenice asked the port-captain (who must needs come up officiously to make his salutations) after the disposal of Nais, and was told that she had been clapped into a dungeon beneath the royal pyramid, and the officer of the guard there had given his bond for her safekeeping.

===Chinese===

不过，那时节我哪有多少闲工夫顾自己的小性子。等候间，福伦尼刻向港务长官（他少不得又凑上来殷勤请安）问起娜伊丝的发落，得知她已被关进王家金字塔底下的地牢，那里的卫戍官已立下保状，担保她万无一失。

===Original===

“It is to be hoped he understands his work,” said the Empress. “That pretty Nais knows the pyramid better than most, and it may be he will be sent to the tormentors for putting her in a cell which had a secret outlet. You would feel pleasure if the girl escaped, Deucalion?”

===Chinese===

「但愿他懂行，」女皇说，「那个标致的娜伊丝对金字塔的熟稔胜过大多数人，说不定就因为把她关进了一间带暗出口的牢房，他自己倒要被送去见施刑的人了。这姑娘若是逃了出去，你会高兴吧，德乌卡利翁？」

===Original===

“Assuredly,” said I, knowing how useless it would be to make a secret of the matter. “I have no enmity against Nais.”

===Chinese===

「当然高兴，」我说，心里明白这件事瞒也是白瞒。「我对娜伊丝并无仇怨。」

===Original===

“But I have,” said she viciously, “and I am still minded to lock your faith to me by that wedding gift you know of.”

===Chinese===

「可我有，」她恶狠狠地说，「而且我仍旧存心要用你知道的那件婚礼赠礼，把你的忠诚锁死在我身上。」

===Original===

“The thing shall be done,” I said. “Before all, the Empress of Atlantis.”

===Chinese===

「这件事定会办到，」我说。「亚特兰蒂斯的女皇，高于一切。」

===Original===

“Poof! Deucalion, you are too stiff and formal. You ought to be mightily honoured that I condescend to be jealous of your favours. Your hand, sir, please, to help me into the litter. And now come in beside me, and keep me warm against the night air. Ho! you guards there with the torches! Keep farther back against the street walls. The perfume you are burning stifles me.”

===Chinese===

「啧！德乌卡利翁，你太僵硬、太拘板了。我肯屈尊来吃你那些恩宠的醋，你该感到无上的荣幸才是。先生，劳你的手，扶我上轿。现在进来坐到我身边来，替我挡挡夜里的寒气。喂！你们那些打火把的卫兵！退远些，靠到街墙那边去。你们熏的这香，闷死我了。」

===Original===

Again there was a feast that night in the royal banqueting-hall; again I sat beside Phorenice on the raised dais which stands beneath the symbols of the snake and the outstretched hand. What had been taken for granted before about our forthcoming relationship was this time proclaimed openly; the Empress herself acknowledged me as her husband that was to be; and all that curled and jewelled throng of courtiers hailed me as greater than themselves, by reason of this woman’s choice. There was method, too, in their salutation. Some rumour must have got about of my preference for the older and simpler habits, and there was no drinking wine to my health after the new and (as I considered) impertinent manner. Decorously, each lord and lady there came forward, and each in turn spilt a goblet at my feet; and when I called any up, whether man or woman, to receive titbits from my platter, it was eaten simply and thankfully, and not kissed or pocketed with any extravagant gesture.

===Chinese===

当夜，王家宴厅里又开了一场盛宴；我又坐在福伦尼刻身旁，坐在蛇徽与张手徽记之下的高台上。先前我们之间那层将成未成的关系，人们原本只是心照不宣，这一回却公然宣告了出去；女皇亲口认了我做她未来的丈夫；满堂鬈发油亮、浑身珠玉的朝臣，因着这个女人的拣选，都敬我为高于他们自身的人。他们的礼数中也自有章法。想是有些风声传了出去，说我不喜新巧、偏爱旧简，于是无人再按那种新派的（在我看来实属放肆的）方式为我健康举杯。在场的勋贵与命妇个个端端正正地上前，依次在我脚边泼洒一盏酒；我无论唤谁上来——男人也好，女人也好——从我的盘里领些珍馐，他们都只朴朴实实地领受致谢，再没有吻食、纳袖那一套夸张的姿态。

===Original===

The flaring jets of earth-breath showed me, too, so I thought, a plainer habit of dress, and a more sober mien amongst this thoughtless mob of banqueters. And, indeed, it must have been plain to notice, for Phorenice, leaning over till the ruddy curls on her shoulder brushed my face, chided me in a playful whisper as having usurped her high authority already.

===Chinese===

地息喷出的烈焰也让我看出——至少我自以为看出——这班不动脑子的宴客中间，衣着的习尚素净了些，神情也收敛了些。而且这变化必定显而易见，因为福伦尼刻俯身过来，肩头的红鬈发直拂到我的脸上，嬉笑着低声嗔我，说我已经僭用了她的至高权威。

===Original===

“Oh, sir,” she pleaded mockingly, “do not make your rule over us too ascetic. I have given no orders for this change, but tonight there are no perfumes in the air; the food is so plain and I have half a mind to burn the cook; and as for the clothes and gauds of these diners, by my face! they might have come straight from the old King’s reign before I stepped in here to show how tasteful could be colours on a robe, or how pretty the glint of a jewel. It’s done by no orders of mine, Deucalion. They have swung round to this change by sheer courtier instinct. Why, look at the beards of the men! There is not half the curl about many of them today that they showed with such exquisiteness yesterday. By my face! I believe they’d reap their chins tomorrow as smooth as yours, if you go on setting the fashions at this prodigious rate and I do not interfere.”

===Chinese===

「哦，先生，」她装腔作势地央告道，「你治理我们，可别太过苦行。这场变化我并未下过什么旨意，可是今夜空气里没有一丝香气；饭菜素成这个样子，我都想一把火烧了厨子；至于这些赴宴人的衣裳首饰——凭我这张脸起誓！倒像是从老国王的时代直接穿来的，那时节我还没进宫来示范：袍子上的色彩可以何等雅致，宝石的光芒可以何等明媚。这可不是奉我的旨意办的，德乌卡利翁。他们是全凭朝臣的本能，一下扭转到这变化上来的。瞧瞧这些男人的胡子！今天其中好些人胡子上的鬈曲，还抵不上昨天他们妆出的那般精妙的一半。凭我这张脸起誓！你要照这个惊人的速度继续领袖风尚、我又不出手干预的话，我相信明天他们就要把下巴刮得同你一样光了。」

===Original===

“Why hinder them if they feel more cleanly shaven?”

===Chinese===

「他们若觉得刮了胡子更清爽，何苦拦着？」

===Original===

“No, sir. There shall be only one clean chin where a beard can grow in all Atlantis, and that shall be carried by the man who is husband to the Empress. Why, my Deucalion, would you have no sumptuary laws? Would you have these good folk here and the common people outside imitate us in every cut of the hair and every fold of a garment which it pleases us to discover? Come, sir, if you and I chose to say that our sovereignty was marked only by our superior strength of arm and wit, they would hate us at once for our arrogance; whereas, if we keep apart to ourselves a few mere personal decorations, these become just objects to admire and pleasantly envy.”

===Chinese===

「不，先生。全亚特兰蒂斯，凡是长得出胡子的下巴，只许有一个是光的，那就是女皇的丈夫其人。再说，我的德乌卡利翁，难道你要连奢侈禁令也一并废了不成？难道你要让这里的好人们、让外面的平民百姓，连我们兴之所至发明的每一种发式、每一道衣褶都一一仿效？得了，先生，倘若你我宣布，我们的君权只以臂力与心智的过人作标记，他们会立刻恨我们的傲慢；反过来，我们若把区区几样贴身的饰物留给自己专用，这些东西便恰成众人钦慕、又可以艳羡得舒舒服服的对象了。」

===Original===

“You show me that there is more in the office of a ruler than meets the eye.”

===Chinese===

「你让我看到，为君之道，远不止眼睛看到的这些。」

===Original===

“And yet they tell me, and indeed show me, that you have ruled with some success.”

===Chinese===

「可他们跟我说，也确实做给我看过，你治国还颇有几分成效呢。」

===Original===

“I employed the older method. It requires a Phorenice to invent these nicer flights.”

===Chinese===

「我用的是更古老的法子。这般精微的花样，须得一个福伦尼刻才发明得出。」

===Original===

“Flatterer!” said she, and smote me playfully with the back of her little fingers on my arm. “You are becoming as great a courtier as any of them. You make me blush with your fine pleasantries, Deucalion, and there is no fan-girl here tonight to cool my cheek. I must choose me another fan-girl. But it shall not be Ylga. Ylga seems to have more of a kindness for you than I like, and if she is wise she will go live in her palace at the other side of the city, and there occupy herself with the ordering of her slaves, and the makings of embroideries. I shall not be hard on Ylga unless she forces me, but I will have no woman in this kingdom treat you with undue civility.”

===Chinese===

「马屁精！」她说着，用小手的指背顽皮地在我臂上拍了一下。「你快要赶上他们中间最大的朝臣了。你这些漂亮的妙语说得我脸上发烧，德乌卡利翁，偏偏今夜没有执扇侍女在这里替我扇脸。我须得另挑一位执扇侍女了。但不能是伊尔加。伊尔加待你的那点情分，似乎超出了我喜欢的限度；她若识相，就该搬去城那头她的宫里住，在那里安心打理她的奴隶，做做刺绣。只要她不逼我，我不会难为伊尔加，但这个王国里，我不许任何女人对你有逾分的殷勤。」

===Original===

“And how am I to act,” said I, falling in with her mood, “when I see and hear all the men of Atlantis making their protestations before you? By your own confession they all love you as ardently as they seem to have loved you hopelessly.”

===Chinese===

「那我该怎么办呢，」我顺着她的性子说，「每当我看见、听见全亚特兰蒂斯的男子都在你面前表露心迹？照你自己的招认，他们个个都炽热地爱着你——正如他们似乎一直都无可救药地爱着你那样。」

===Original===

“Ah, now,” she said, “you must not ask me to do impossibilities. I am powerful if you will. But I have no force which will govern the hearts of these poor fellows on matters such as that. But if you choose, you make proclamation that I am given now body and inwards to you, and if they continue to offend your pride in this matter, you may take your culprits, and give them over to the tormentors. Indeed, Deucalion, I think it would be a pretty attention to me if you did arrange some such ceremony. It seems to me a present,” she added with a frown, “that the jealousy is too much on one side.”

===Chinese===

「啊，这就难了，」她说，「你不能要求我做办不到的事。你若要说我有权势，我确有权势。可是在这种事情上，我没有一种力量管得住这些可怜人的心。不过你若愿意，可以颁下公告，说我如今身心都交付给了你；他们若还在此事上触犯你的自尊，你便可以拿住你的人犯，把他们交给施刑者。说真的，德乌卡利翁，你若果真安排出这样一场典礼来，我认为倒是对我的一番可爱的殷勤。」她皱着眉又补了一句：「依我看，这吃醋的事，眼下未免太偏在一边了。」

===Original===

“You must not expect that a man who has been divorced from love for all of a busy life can learn all its niceties in an instant. Myself, I was feeling proud of my progress. With any other schoolmistress than you, Phorenice, I should not be near so forward. In fact (if one may judge by my past record), I should not have begun to learn at all.”

===Chinese===

「你不能指望一个忙碌一生、与爱情绝缘的人，转眼之间就把爱情的一切精微都学会。就我自己说，我正为自己的进境得意呢。换任何一个别的女教师来教，福伦尼刻，我都到不了这么先进的程度。事实上（倘若可以凭我过往的履历来判断），我压根就不会开这个头。」

===Original===

“I suppose you think I should be satisfied with that? Well, I am not. I can be finely greedy over some matters.”

===Chinese===

「我估摸着，你以为我听了这话就该心满意足了？哼，我才不呢。有些事情上，我是贪得可以的。」

===Original===

The banquet this night did not extend to inordinate length. Phorenice had gone through much since last she slept, and though she had declared herself Goddess in the meantime, it seemed that her body remained mortal as heretofore. The black rings of weariness had grown under her wondrous eyes, and she lay back amongst the cushions of the divan with her limbs slackened and listless. When the dancers came and postured before us, she threw them a jewel and bade them begone before they had given a half of their performance, and the poet, a silly swelling fellow who came to sing the deeds of the day, she would not hear at all.

===Chinese===

这一晚的宴席没有拖到离谱的长度。自上次睡眠以来，福伦尼刻经历了太多，虽说这其间她已自封为女神，她的身子骨看去却仍同先前一样是凡胎。她那双妙目底下已熬出了黑黑的眼圈，她仰靠在长榻的软垫间，四肢松懈，恹恹无力。舞人上来在我们跟前起舞弄姿，她抛给他们一颗宝石，不等他们演到一半便打发走；至于那诗人——一个来唱当日功业的、蠢而自负的家伙——她干脆一个字也不肯听。

===Original===

“Tomorrow,” she said wearily, “but for now grant me peace. My Lord Deucalion has given me much food for thought this day, and presently I go to my chamber to muse over the future policies of this State throughout the night. Tomorrow come to me again, and if your poetry is good and short, I will pay you surprisingly. But see to it that you are not long-winded. If there are superfluous words, I will pay you for those with the stick.”

===Chinese===

「明天再说，」她倦倦地说，「眼下且容我清净。我的德乌卡利翁大人今天给了我许多要费心思量的事，稍顷我便回寝宫去，通宵筹谋这个国度的方略。明天再来见我，你的诗若是又好又短，我会付你一笔惊人的酬劳。不过你可得留神，别啰唆。多出来的废话，我拿棍子付账。」

===Original===

She rose to her feet then, and when the banqueters had made their salutation to us, I led her away from the banqueting-hall and down the passages with their secret doors which led to her private chambers. She clung on my arm, and once when we halted whilst a great stone block swung slowly ajar to let us pass, she drooped her head against my shoulder. Her breath came warm against my cheek, and the loveliness of her face so close at hand surpasses the description of words. I think it was in her mind that I should kiss the red lips which were held so near to mine, but willing though I was to play the part appointed, I could not bring myself to that. So when the stone block had swung, she drew away with a sigh, and we went on without further speech.

===Chinese===

随后她站起身来，宴客们向我们行礼毕，我便引她离了宴厅，循着那些暗门密布的甬道，走向她的私室。她挽住我的手臂；有一回我们停步，等一方巨石缓缓旋开放我们过去，她把头垂到了我的肩上。她呼出的气息暖暖地拂在我颊上，她那张脸近在咫尺的可爱，不是言语所能描摹的。我想她心里是盼着我去吻那双送得离我如此之近的红唇的，我虽甘愿扮演派定给我的角色，却怎么也做不到这一步。于是巨石旋过之后，她叹一口气缩了回去，我们再没说话，继续前行。

===Original===

“May the High Gods treat you tenderly,” I said, when we came to the door of her bedchamber.

===Chinese===

「愿至上诸神善待于你。」走到她寝宫门前时，我说。

===Original===

“I am my own God,” said she, “in all things but one. By my face! you are a tardy wooer, Deucalion. Where do you go now?”

===Chinese===

「除了某一样，万事我都自任为神。」她说，「凭我这张脸起誓！你可真是个慢吞吞的求爱者，德乌卡利翁。你这会儿上哪儿去？」

===Original===

“To my own chamber.”

===Chinese===

「回我自己的居室。」

===Original===

“Oh, go then, go.”

===Chinese===

「哦，那就去吧，去吧。」

===Original===

“Is there anything more I could do?”

===Chinese===

「还有什么我能效劳的吗？」

===Original===

“Nothing that your wit or your will would prompt you to. Yes, indeed, you are finely decorous, Deucalion, in your old-fashioned way, but you are a mighty poor wooer. Don’t you know, my man, that a woman esteems some things the more highly if they are taken from her by rude force?”

===Chinese===

「凭你的机灵或你的心性，都不会提示你做出来的事，那是没有了。是啊，你确实是端方得漂亮，德乌卡利翁，端方得古派，可你求爱的本事实在差劲透顶。你难道不晓得，我的好人，有些东西若是被粗蛮的强力从女人那里夺了去，她反而愈发珍视么？」

===Original===

“It seems I know little enough about women.”

===Chinese===

「看来我对女人实在懂得太少。」

===Original===

“You never said a truer word. Bah! And I believe your coldness brings you more benefit in a certain matter than any show of passion could earn. There, get you gone, if the atmosphere of a maiden’s bedchamber hurts your rustic modesty, and your Gods keep you, Deucalion, if that’s the phrase, and if you think They can do it. Get you gone, man, and leave me solitary.”

===Chinese===

「你这辈子没说过一句比这更真的话了。呸！而且我相信，在某件事情上，你这副冷淡带给你的好处，比任何热恋的做派挣来的都多。好了，去吧，若是闺女寝宫里的空气刺伤了你乡下人的羞怯，就走吧；愿你的诸神保佑你，德乌卡利翁——如果该用这句话的话，如果你也当真想得出祂们保佑得了的话。走吧，先生，留我一个人独处。」

===Original===

I had taken the plan of the pyramid out of the archives before the banquet and learned it thoroughly, and so was able to thread my way through its angular mazes without pause or blunder. I, too, was heavily wearied with what I had gone through since my last snatch of sleep, but I dare set apart no time for rest just then. Nais must be sacrificed in part for the needs of Atlantis; but a plan had come to me by which it seemed that she need not be sacrificed wholly; and to carry this through there was need for quick thought and action.

===Chinese===

宴前我已从档案库里调出金字塔的图样，记了个烂熟，因而能在它棱角错杂的迷宫里穿行，既不停顿，也不出错。自上回打盹以来，我经受了这么多劳顿，也早已困乏不堪，但那时节我丝毫不敢留出歇息的工夫。为了亚特兰蒂斯的需要，娜伊丝必须部分地被牺牲；但我想出了一个法子，依它看，她未必要被全然牺牲；而要把这法子贯彻到底，就须得思谋快、动手快。

===Original===

Help came to me also from a quarter I did not expect. As I passed along the tortuous way between the ponderous stones of the pyramid, which led to the apartments that had been given me by Phorenice, a woman glided up out of the shadows of one of the side passages, and when I lifted my hand lamp, there was Ylga.

===Chinese===

援助还从我没有料到的一处来了。我正沿着金字塔巨石间那条曲折的通道，走向福伦尼刻拨给我的居所，一个女子从一条侧廊的暗影里悄然滑出，我举起手提灯一照——是伊尔加。

===Original===

She regarded me half-sullenly. “I have lost my place,” she said, “and it seems I need never have spoken. She intended to have you all along, and it was not a thing like that which could put her off. And you﻿—you just think me officious, if, indeed, you have ever given me another thought till now.”

===Chinese===

她半含愠怒地打量我。「我的位子丢了，」她说，「而且看来，我那番话原本就不用说。她从头到尾就存心要得到你，那样一桩小事根本打消不了她。而你呢——你只消把我当成多事就完了，倘若你直到此刻果真还想起过我第二回的话。」

===Original===

“I never forget a kindness.”

===Chinese===

「恩惠，我从不忘记。」

===Original===

“Oh, you will learn that trick soon now. And you are going to marry her, you! The city is ringing with it. I thought at least you were honest, but when there is a high place to be got by merely taking a woman with it, you are like the rest. I thought, too, that you would be one of those men who have a distrust for ruddy hair. And, besides she is little.”

===Chinese===

「哦，这门本事你如今转眼也快学会了。你居然要娶她，你！全城都传遍了。我原以为你至少是诚实的，可只要顺手带走一个女人便能换得高位，你就同旁人一个样了。我还以为，你是那种天生信不过红头发女人的男人呢。何况，她个子又矮。」

===Original===

“Ylga,” I said, “you have taught me that these walls are full of crannies and ears. I will listen to no word against Phorenice. But I would have further converse with you soon. If you still have a kindness for me, go to the chamber that is mine and wait for me there. I will join you shortly.”

===Chinese===

「伊尔加，」我说，「是你教会了我，这些墙里到处是缝隙和耳朵。凡诋毁福伦尼刻的话，我一概不听。但我不久还要与你细谈。你若对我尚有情分，就到我的居室去，在那里等我。我随后就来。」

===Original===

She drooped her eyes. “What do you want of me, Deucalion?”

===Chinese===

她垂下眼帘。「你要我做什么，德乌卡利翁？」

===Original===

“I want to say something to you. You will learn who it concerns later.”

===Chinese===

「我有话要对你说。关乎何人，你稍后自会知道。」

===Original===

“But is it﻿—is it fitting for a maiden to come to a man’s room at this hour?”

===Chinese===

「可是这……这个时辰，一个闺女到男人房里去，合适吗？」

===Original===

“I know little of your conventions here in this new Atlantis. I am Deucalion, girl, and if you still have qualms, remembering that, do not come.”

===Chinese===

「你们这新亚特兰蒂斯的规矩，我知之甚少。我是德乌卡利翁，姑娘，你若还有顾虑，念着这一点，便不要来。」

===Original===

She looked up at me with a sneer. “I was foolish,” she said. “My lord’s coldness has grown into a proverb, and I should have remembered it. Yes; I will come.”

===Chinese===

她抬眼看我，带着讥诮。「我方才糊涂了，」她说，「大人的冷淡早已成了一句谚语，我本该记得的。好；我来。」

===Original===

“Go now, then,” said I, and waited till she had passed on ahead and was out of sight and hearing. With Ylga to help me, my tasks were somewhat lightened, and their sequence changed. In the first instance, now, I had got to make my way with as little delay and show as possible into a certain sanctuary which lay within the temple of our Lady the Moon. And here my knowledge as one of the Seven stood me in high favour.

===Chinese===

「那就现在去吧。」我说，等她先行过去，走得看不见、听不见了才动。有伊尔加相助，我的各项事务轻省了些，次序也变了。眼下第一件，是我须尽量不耽搁、不声张地潜入月神夫人（our Lady the Moon）神庙中的一处圣所。在这上头，我身为七人之一的那门知识，帮了我大忙。

===Original===

All the temples of the city of Atlantis are in immediate and secret connection with the royal pyramid, but the passages are little used, seeing that they are known only to the Seven and to the Three above them, supposing that there are three men living at one time sufficiently learned in the highest of the highest mysteries to be installed in that sublime degree of the Three. And, even by these, the secret ways may only be used on occasions of the greatest stress, so that a generation well may pass without their being trodden by a human foot.

===Chinese===
