And here lay the keynote of the weakness in our defence. The most perfect ramparts that brain can invent are useless without men to line them, and it was men we lacked. Of students entering into the colleges of the Sacred Mountain, there had been none now for many a year. The younger generation thought little of the older Gods. Of the men that had grown up amongst the sacred groves, and filled offices there, many had become lukewarm in their faith and remained on only through habit, and because an easy living stayed near them there; and these, when the siege began, quickly made their way over to the other side.

===Chinese===

我们防务软弱的症结便在这里。人脑所能想出的最完善的壁垒，没有人在后面守，也是无用，而我们缺的正是人。进圣山各学院的学生，多年来已一个也没有。年轻一代把老辈诸神不放在心上。在圣树林里长大、在那里供职的人当中，许多人的信仰早已温吞，留下来只是出于积习，还因为那里近旁便有一口安逸饭吃；围城一开始，这班人便纷纷溜到了对面。

===Original===

Phorenice was no fool to fight against unnecessary strength. Her heralds made proclamation that peace and a good subsistence would be given to those who chose to come out to her willingly; and as an alternative she would kill by torture and mutilation those she caught in the place when she took it by storm, as she most assuredly would do before she had finished with it. And so great was the prestige of her name, that quite one-half of these that remained on the mountain took themselves away from the defence.

===Chinese===

福伦尼刻不是那种专跟不必要的强硬硬碰的蠢人。她的传令官四处宣示：自愿出山投她的人，可得太平与一份好口粮；不然的话，待她强攻破山之时——在她料理完这座山之前，那是一定要来的——她捉住的守山者，都将被酷刑和肢解处死。她名头的威势竟这样大，留在山上的人里，足有一半从防务中抽身去了。

===Original===

There was no attempt to hold back these sorry priests, nor was there any punishing them as they went. Zaemon, indeed, was minded (so he told me with grim meaning himself) to give them some memento of their apostasy to carry away which would not wear out, but the others of the High Council made him stay his vengeful hand. And so when I came to the place the garrison numbered no more than eighty, counting even feeble old dotards who could barely walk; and of men not past their prime I could barely command a score.

===Chinese===

没有人试图拦阻这些丧气的祭司，他们离去时也没有人加以惩罚。泽蒙倒是起过意（他亲口向我讲起时，语意森然），要给他们留下一件随身带走、永磨不灭的叛教纪念；但最高议事会的其他人勒住了他那只报复的手。于是我到任之时，守军总共不过八十人，连走路都摇摇晃晃的老朽也算了进去；未过盛年之人，我勉勉强强凑不足二十。

===Original===

Still, seeing the narrowness of the passages which led to each of the gates, up which in no place could more than two men advance together, we were by no means in desperate straits for the defence as yet; and if my new-given kingdom was so far small, consisting as it did in effect of the Sacred Mountain and no other part of Atlantis, at any rate there seemed little danger of its being further contracted.

===Chinese===

不过，看到通往各道门的甬道都那样窄狭——任何一处并排都进不了两人——我们的防务眼下还远不到山穷水尽的地步；我新得的王国虽说到如今还小得可怜，实际不过一座圣山，再无亚特兰蒂斯的寸土别地，但至少，看来它再要缩水，也没有多少危险了。

===Original===

Another of the wise precautions of the men of old stood us in good stead then. In the ancient times, when grain first was grown as food, it came to be looked upon as the acme of wealth. Tribute was always paid from the people to their Priests, and presently, so the old histories say, it was appointed that this should take the form of grain, as this was a medium both dignified and fitting. And those of the people who had it not, were forced to barter their other produce for grain before they could pay this tribute.

===Chinese===

古人的又一桩深谋远虑，这时也大施其利。上古之时，谷子初次被种来当粮食，便被奉为财富的极顶。庶民向他们的祭司纳贡，从来如此；据古史所载，不久又钦定贡物须以谷子的形式缴纳，因为那是一种既尊贵又合宜的中介。没有谷子的百姓，便只得先拿别的出产换谷，才能完纳这份贡赋。

===Original===

On the Sacred Mountain itself vast storehouses were dug in the rock, and here the grain was teemed in great yellow heaps, and each generation of those that were set over it, took a pride in adding to the accumulation.

===Chinese===

圣山本体的岩石里凿出一座座巨大的仓廪，谷子倾泻进去，堆成一座座金黄的山；每一代经手掌管的人，都以能再添上几堆为荣。

===Original===

In more modern days it had been a custom amongst the younger and more forward of the Priests to scoff at this ancient provision, and to hold that a treasure of gold, or weapons, or jewels would have more value and no less of dignity; and more than once it has been a close thing lest these innovators should not be outvoted. But as it was, the old constitution had happily been preserved, and now in these years of trial the Clan reaped the benefit. And so with these granaries, and a series of great tanks and cisterns which held the rainfall, there was no chance of Phorenice reducing our stronghold by mere close investment, even though she sat down stubbornly before it for a score of years.

===Chinese===

到了较近的年代，祭司中年轻而趋新的一辈，惯于讥嘲这份古老的储备，主张换成金子、兵器或珠宝的窖藏，说那样价值更大而尊贵不减；不止一次，这些新派人物险些便没有被表决压下去，其间只差一线。但事实是，古老的旧制侥幸得以保全，如今在这试炼的年月里，氏族坐收其利。有这些粮仓，再加上一系列蓄积雨水的大水箱水窖，福伦尼刻休想单靠久围困死我们这座堡垒，哪怕她在山下死死坐上二十年。

===Original===

But it was the paucity of men for the defence which oppressed me most. As I took my way about the head of the Mountain, inspecting all points, the emptiness of the place smote me like a succession of blows. The groves, once so trim, were now shaggy and unpruned. Wind had whirled the leaves in upon the temple floors, and they lay there unswept. The college of youths held no more now than a musty smell to bear witness that men had once been grown there. The homely palaces of the higher Priests, at one time so ardently sought after, lay many of them empty, because not even one candidate came forward now to canvass for election.

===Chinese===

但最压在我心头的，还是守御人手的短缺。我在山顶各处巡视时，此地的空旷像连珠的棍棒，一下下打在我身上。那些树丛，从前修剪得那样齐整，如今蓬乱无章。风把落叶卷进庙里的地坪，堆在那里无人扫除。青年学院如今只剩一股霉味，作证曾有人在里面养育成人。高阶祭司那些家常的宅邸，当年人人趋之若鹜，如今空着的不在少数，因为连一个出来钻营候选的人也没有了。

===Original===

Evil thoughts surged up within me as I saw these things, that were direct promptings from the nether Gods. “There must be something wanting,” these tempters whispered, “in a religion from which so many of its Priests fled at the first pinch of persecution.”

===Chinese===

眼见这些光景，种种恶念便在我胸中翻涌——那都是地底诸神（the nether Gods）直接的挑动。「一门宗教，」这些诱惑者切切低语，「若头一阵逼害的风声袭来，便有这许多祭司弃之而逃，它内里必有欠缺。」

===Original===

I did what I could to thrust these waverings resolutely behind me; but they refused to be altogether ousted from my brain; and so I made a compromise with myself: First, I would with the help that might be given me, destroy this wanton Phorenice, and regain the kingdom which had been given me to my own proper rule; and afterwards I would call a council of the Seven and council of the Three, and consider without prejudice if there was any matter in which our ancient ritual could be amended to suit the more modern requirements. But this should not be done till Phorenice was dead and I was firmly planted in her room. I would not be a party, even to myself, to any plan which smacked at all of surrender.

===Chinese===

我竭力把这些动摇坚决抛到身后；可它们死活不肯从我的脑中全然退去；于是我同自己作了个折中：第一步，我要凭着可能给我的助力，除掉这淫荡的福伦尼刻，收回交由我个人统治的这个王国；随后，我再召集七人的议事会和三人的议事会，不带成见地考量，我们古老的仪轨有哪一处可以修订，以应合较新时代的需要。但这一切须待福伦尼刻死后、我稳稳立于她的位置之时方可施行。哪怕只是对我自己，任何稍稍带一点投降气味的方案，我也不肯与闻。

===Original===

And there as I walked through the desolate groves and beside the cold altars, the High Gods were pleased to show their approval of my scheme, and to give me opportunity to bind myself to it with a solemn oath and vow. At that moment from His distant resting-place in the East, our Lord the Sun leaped up to begin another day. For long enough from where I stood below the crest of the Mountain, He Himself would be invisible. But the great light of His glory spread far into the sky, and against it the Ark of the Mysteries loomed in black outline from the highest crag where it rested, lonely and terrible.

===Chinese===

就在那里，当我行过荒凉的树丛、走过冷清的祭坛时，至上诸神欣然向我昭示他们对这一谋划的嘉许，并给我机会，以庄严的誓愿把自己缚在这谋划之上。就在那一刻，太阳神主从祂东方遥远的息所一跃而起，开始新的一日。从我在山顶之下的立处望去，祂自身还要有好一阵望不见。但祂荣光的巨焰早已远远铺展在天际，衬着那光，奥秘的方舟在它安歇的最高崖上显出漆黑的轮廓，孤零零的，可怖极了。

===Original===

For anyone unauthorised to go nearer than a thousand paces to this storehouse of the Highest Mysteries meant instant death. On that day when I was initiated as one of the Seven, I had been permitted to go near and once press my lips against its ample curves; and the rank of my degree gave me the privilege to repeat that salute again once on each day when a new year was born. But what lay inside its great interior, and how it was entered, that was hidden from the Seven, even as it was from the other Priests and the common people in the city below. Only those who had been raised to the sublime elevation of the Three had a knowledge of the dreadful powers which were stored within it.

===Chinese===

这座至高奥秘（the Highest Mysteries）的宝库，凡未获授权之人，走近到千步之内便意味着立死。我入七人之列受职的那一天，曾蒙恩准走近它，把双唇在它饱满的曲面上贴过一次；我这一品级的阶位，还特许我每逢新岁降生之日，再行一次这样的亲吻礼。但它巨大的内里盛着什么，又如何进入，连七人也无从知晓，正如山下别的祭司和庶民也无从知晓一样。唯有升至三人那崇峻高位的，才识得它内中贮藏的那些可怖的力量。

===Original===

I went down on my knees where I was, and Zaemon knelt beside me, and together we recited the prayers which had been said by the Priests from the beginning of time, giving thanks to our great Lord that He has come to brighten another day. And then, with my eyes fixed on the black outline of the Ark of Mysteries I vowed that, come what might, I at least would be true servant of the High Gods to my life’s end, and that my whole strength should be spent in restoring Their worship and glory.

===Chinese===

我当场跪了下去，泽蒙跪在我身旁，我们一同诵念了祭司们自开天辟地以来一直诵念的祷词，感谢我们伟大的主又来临，来照亮另一日。随后，我目注奥秘的方舟那漆黑的轮廓立誓：无论出什么事，我至少要终身做至上诸神忠实的仆人，倾我全部的气力，恢复他们的敬奉与荣光。

## XVII. Nais the Regained / 十七　失而复得的娜伊丝

===Original===

Now, from where we stood together just below the crest of the Sacred Mountain, we could see down into the city, which lay spread out below us like a map. The harbour and the great estuary gleamed at its farther side; and the fringe of hills beyond smoked and fumed in their accustomed fashion; the great stone circle of our Lord the Sun stood up grim and bare in the middle of the city; and nearer in reared up the great mass of the royal pyramid, the gold on its sides catching new gold from the Sun. There, too, in the square before the pyramid stood the throne of granite, dwarfed by the distance to the size of a mole’s hill, in which these nine years my love had lain sleeping.

===Chinese===

此刻，我们一同站在圣山顶峰稍下的地方，可以俯瞰全城，它在我们脚下铺展开来，宛如一幅地图。港湾与巨大的河口在城的那一侧闪光；更远处的一带丘峦照着老样子腾烟冒气；太阳神主的大石环在城中央森然秃立；近处则挺起王家金字塔的庞大躯体，塔身的贴金又从太阳那里接住了新的金光。还有，在金字塔前的广场上，立着那座花岗岩御座——隔着距离缩得只有一座鼹鼠土丘大小——九年来，我的爱人便沉睡在它里面。

===Original===

Old Zaemon followed my gaze. “Ay,” he said with a sigh, “I know where your chief interest is. Deucalion when he landed here new from Yucatan was a strong man. The King whom we have chosen﻿—and who is the best we have to choose﻿—has his weakness.”

===Chinese===

老泽蒙顺着我的目光望去。「唉，」他叹了口气说，「我知道你最挂心的是什么。德乌卡利翁刚从尤卡坦登岸来到此地时，是个坚强的人。而我们推举出来的这位王——他已是我们所能推举的最佳人选了——却有他的弱点。」

===Original===

“It can be turned into additional strength. Give me Nais here, living and warm to fight for, and I am a stronger man by far than the cold viceroy and soldier that you speak about.”

===Chinese===

「这弱点可以转成额外的力量。把娜伊丝给我，让她活着、暖着，好让我有可为而战，我便远比你们说的那个冷淡的总督兼军人强得多。」

===Original===

“I have passed my word to that already, and you shall have her, but at the cost of damaging somewhat this new kingdom of yours. Maybe too at the same time we may rid you of this Phorenice and her brood. But I do not think it likely. She is too wily, and once we begin our play, she is likely to guess whence it comes, and how it will end, and so will make an escape before harm can reach her. The High Gods, who have sent all these trials for our refinement, have seen fit to give her some knowledge of how these earth tremors may be set a-moving.”

===Chinese===

「这事我早已许下话来，你定会得到她，只是要以稍稍损伤你这个新王国为代价。或许与此同时，我们还能替你除掉这个福伦尼刻和她那一窝。但我不认为有多大指望。她太狡黠，我们这边一开局，她多半就会猜到这戏从何而来、如何收场，于是赶在祸事上身之前逃之夭夭。至上诸神降下这一切磨炼来砥砺我们，却也觉得宜于让她也懂得几分如何发动这等地动。」

===Original===

“I have seen her juggle with them. But may I hear your scheme?”

===Chinese===

「我见过她摆弄这些。不过，我能听听你们的计策么？」

===Original===

“It will be shown you in good time enough. But for the present I would bid you sleep. It will be your part to go into the city tonight, and take your woman (that is my daughter) when she is set free, and bring her here as best you can. And for that you will need all a strong man’s strength.”﻿—He stepped back, and looked me up and down.﻿—“There are not many folk that would take you for the tidy clean-chinned Deucalion now, my brother. Your appearance will be a fine armour for you down yonder in the city tonight when we wake it with our earth-shaking and terror. As you stand now, you are hairy enough, and shaggy enough, and naked enough, and dirty enough for some wild savage new landed out of Europe. Have a care that no fine citizen down yonder takes a fancy to your thews, and seizes upon you as his servant.”

===Chinese===

「时机到了自会让你知道。眼下我只想劝你去睡。今夜由你进城，趁她获释之时接过你的女人（也就是我的女儿），再尽你所能把她带到这里来。这份差事，需要你拿出一个壮汉的全部气力。」——他退后一步，把我上下打量一番。——「如今没有几个人会把你认作那个下巴刮得干干净净、齐齐整整的德乌卡利翁了，我的兄弟。今夜我们以地动和恐怖惊醒下面那座城时，你这副模样便是你在城里的顶好甲胄。就照你现在这样，毛发够多，须发够乱，身子够赤裸，也够肮脏，活脱脱是个刚从欧罗巴什么地方登岸的野蛮生番。只是要留神，别叫下面城里的哪位体面公民看中了你的筋骨，把你抓去充当他的仆人。」

===Original===

“I somewhat pity him in his household if he does.”

===Chinese===

「他若真这么干，我倒有点可怜他家里的人。」

===Original===

Old Zaemon laughed. “Why, come to think of it, so do I.”

===Chinese===

老泽蒙笑了。「哎呀，细想起来，我也一样。」

===Original===

But quickly he got grave again. Laughter and Zaemon were very rare playmates. “Well, get you to bed, my King, and leave me to go into the Ark of Mysteries and prepare there with another of the Three the things that must be done. It is no light business to handle the tremendous powers which we must put into movement this night. And there is danger for us as there is for you. So if by chance we do not meet again till we stand up yonder behind the stars, giving account to the Gods, fare you well, Deucalion.”

===Chinese===

但他很快又肃然起来。欢笑与泽蒙素来难得为伴。「好了，去睡吧，我的王，让我往奥秘的方舟里去，与三人中的另一位把必办的事备妥。今夜我们必须推动的那等可怖伟力，操持起来绝非小事。我们与你一样要冒风险。所以，倘若机缘不巧，我们要到上界星斗之侧、向诸神交账之时才能再见，那么就此别过了，德乌卡利翁。」

===Original===

I slept that day as a soldier sleeps, taking full rest out of the hours, and letting no harassing thought disturb me. It is only the weak who permit their sleep to be broken on these occasions. And when the dark was well set, I roused and fetched those who should attend to the rope. Our Lady the Moon did not shine at that turn of the month: and the air was full of a great blackness. So I was out of sight all the while they lowered me.

===Chinese===

那一天我睡得像个军人，把时辰里的休息榨取得干干净净，不让任何烦心的念头搅扰自己。在这种关头还会任凭睡眠被搅破的，只有弱者。夜色坐实之后，我便起身，唤来那些照管绳索的人。那个时节，天上没有月神夫人的光：空气里弥漫着一大片浓黑。他们把我往下缒时，始终看不见我的人影。

===Original===

I reached the tumbled rocks that lay at the deep foot of the cliff, and then commenced to use a nice caution, because Phorenice’s soldiers squatted uneasily round their campfires, as though they had forebodings of the coming evil. I had no mind to further stir their wakefulness. So I crept swiftly along in the darkest of the shadows, and at last came to the spot where that passage ends which before I had used to get beneath the walls of the city.

===Chinese===

我摸到崖脚深处那片垒垒乱石，随即开始拿出细致的小心，因为福伦尼刻的士兵们正围着营火不安地蹲坐，仿佛对将至的灾祸有了预感。我不想再撩起他们的警醒。于是我拣最黑的黑影疾速爬行，终于到了那条通道的出口——先前我便是借着它钻到城墙底下的。

===Original===

The lamp was in place, and I made my way along the windings swiftly. The air, so it seemed to me, was even more noxious with vapours than it had been when I was down there before, and I judged that Zaemon had already begun to stir those internal activities which were shortly to convulse the city. But again I had difficulty in finding an exit, and this, not because there were people moving about at the places where I had to come out, but because the set of the masonry was entirely changed. In olden times the Priests’ Clan oversaw all the architects’ plans, and ruled out anything likely to clash with their secret passages and chambers. But in this modern day the Priests were of small account, and had no say in this matter, and the architects often through sheer blundering sealed up and made useless many of these outlets and hiding-places.

===Chinese===

灯还在原处，我便顺着弯道疾行。依我的感觉，这里的空气比我上次下来时烟气更加浊毒，我料定泽蒙已经开始搅动那些地底的动静，不多时便要震撼全城。但找出口又费了力气，这回倒不是在我必须钻出的地方有人走动，而是砖石的格局整个变了。古时候，祭司氏族督察营造师们的一切图样，凡有可能与其秘密通道和密室相碍的，一概剔除。可到了如今这世道，祭司们无足轻重，在这件事上说不上话，营造师们常常纯出于失手，把许多这样的出口和藏身之所封死、废弃了。

===Original===

As it was then, I had to get out of the network of tunnels and galleries where I could, and not where I would, and in the event found myself at the farther side of the city, almost up to where the outer wall joins down to the harbour. I came out without being seen, careful even in this moment of extremity to preserve the ordinances, and closed all traces of exit behind me. The earth seemed to spring beneath my feet like the deck of a ship in smooth water; and though there was no actual movement as yet to disturb the people, and indeed these slept on in their houses and shelters without alarm, I could feel myself that the solid deadness of the ground was gone, and that any moment it might break out into devastating waves of movement.

===Chinese===

事到如今，我只得拣能出去的地方、而不是我愿意的地方钻出那地道与坑道交织的网络，结果落在了城的另一头，几乎到了外墙下连港湾的地方。我出来时无人看见，即便在这危急关头也小心谨守章程，把出口的一切痕迹在身后闭合如初。地面在我脚下微微弹动，像平静水面上船的甲板；虽然还没有真的震动来惊扰百姓——他们也确实仍在家里栖处安睡，毫无警觉——我自己却感觉得出，大地那种坚实的死寂已经没了，随时可能迸发出毁灭性的层层波动。

===Original===

Gods! Should I be too late to see the untombing of my love? Would she be laid there bare to the public gaze when presently the people swarmed out into the open spaces through fear at what the great earth tremor might cause to fall? I could see, in fancy, their rude, cruel hands thrust upon her as she lay there helpless, and my inwards dried up at the thought.

===Chinese===

诸神啊！我会不会来不及看着我的爱人出墓？等大地的震颤不久后袭来、百姓惊恐着涌到空场上时，她会不会就那样赤裸裸暴露在众目睽睽之下？我在幻想中看见他们粗野残忍的手伸向毫无办法躺在那里的她，一念及此，我的五脏都枯干了。

===Original===

I ran madly down and down the narrow winding streets with the one thought of coming to the square which lay in front of the royal pyramid before these things came to pass. With exquisite cruelty I had been forced with my own hands to place her alive in her burying-place beneath the granite throne, and if thews and speed could do it, I would not miss my reward of taking her forth again with the same strong hands.

===Chinese===

我发疯似的沿窄而曲折的街巷一路往下狂奔，心里只有一个念头：赶在这些事情发生之前到达王家金字塔前的广场。当年以何等精细的残忍，我被逼亲手把她活着安放进花岗岩御座底下的墓穴；如今只要筋骨与脚力办得到，我决不肯错过这份报偿——用同样这双有力的手，再把她抱出来。

===Original===

Few disturbed that furious hurry. At first here and there some wretch who harboured in the gutter cried: “A thief! Throw a share or I pursue.” But if any of these followed, I do not know. At any rate, my speed then must have outdistanced anyone. Presently, too, as the swing of the earth underfoot became more keen, and the stonework of the buildings by the street side began to grate and groan and grit, and sent forth little showers of dust, people began to run with scared cries from out of their doors. But none of these had a mind to stop the ragged, shaggy, savage man who ran so swiftly past, and flung the mud from his naked feet.

===Chinese===

那一路狂奔，少有人搅扰。起初，这里那里在阴沟里栖身的倒霉蛋还喊：「有贼！丢下一份来，不然我就追了！」这些人里有没有真追上来的，我不知道。总之，我那时的脚程必定甩开了任何人。不多时，脚下的荡动愈见尖锐，街边楼舍的砖石开始磨轧、呻吟、错齿，簌簌落下细尘，人们便惊叫着从各家门里跑出来。但没有一个想拦住这个衣衫褴褛、毛发蓬乱、野人一般的汉子——他跑得那样快，赤裸的双脚把泥泞甩得四溅。

===Original===

And so in time I came to the great square, and was there none too soon. The place was filling with people who flocked away from the narrow streets, and it was full of darkness, and noise, and dust, and sickness. Beneath us the ground rippled in undulations like a sea, which with terrifying slowness grew more and more intense.

===Chinese===

就这样，我终于及时赶到了大广场，而且来得一点都不算早。广场正涌满从窄街里逃出来的人群，满是黑暗、喧嚣、尘土和呕恶。我们脚下的大地如海面般一波一波起伏，正以一种骇人的缓慢，愈演愈烈。

===Original===

Ever and again a house crashed down unseen in the gloom, and added to the tumult. But the great pyramid had been planned by its old builders to stand rude shocks. Its stones were dovetailed into one another with a marvellous cleverness, and were further clamped and joined by ponderous tongues of metal. It was a boast that one-half the foundations could be dug from beneath it, and still the pyramid would stand foursquare under heaven, more enduring than the hills.

===Chinese===

不时有一座房屋在幽暗中无人得见地轰然倒下，再添一层乱声。但大金字塔的古老建造者当初谋划时，便要它经得住狂暴的震撼。它的石块以奇绝的巧艺彼此嵌成燕尾，又用沉重的金属键进一步锁紧扣牢。人们曾夸口说，哪怕从它底下挖去一半地基，金字塔依旧四平八稳立于天下，比群山更经久。

===Original===

Flickering torches showed that its great stone doors lay open, and ever and again I saw some frightened inmate scurry out and then be lost to sight in the gloom. But with the royal pyramid and its ultimate fate I had little concern; I did not even care then whether Phorenice was trapped, or whether she came out sound and fit for further mischief. I crouched by the granite throne which stood in the middle of that splendid square, and heard its stones grate together like the ends of a broken bone as it rocked to the earth-waves.

===Chinese===

摇曳的火把照出它巨大的石门洞开着，不时可见哪个受惊的住客奔窜出来，随即隐没于幽暗。但王家金字塔和它的最终命运，我无心多管；那时我甚至不在乎福伦尼刻是被压在里面，还是安然脱身、还能接着作一场恶。我蹲伏在那辉煌广场中央的花岗岩御座旁，听它的石头随地浪摇撼，像断骨的两端一般彼此磨响。

===Original===

In that night of dust and darkness it was hard to see the outline of one’s own hand, but I think that the Gods in some requital for the love which had ached so long within me, gave me special power of sight. As I watched, I saw the great carved rock which formed the capstone of the throne move slightly and then move again, and then again; a tiny jerk for each earth-pulse, but still there was an appreciable shifting; and, moreover, the stone moved always to one side.

===Chinese===

在那样一个尘与暗的夜里，要看清自己手掌的轮廓都难；但我想，诸神是为我心中郁痛了这么多年的爱情作了某种报偿，特意给了我目力。我盯着看时，只见构成御座顶盖的那块御座石微微一动，又动，再动；每一阵地搏只有一下极小的抽动，但终究看得出位移；而且，那石头总是朝着一侧移动。

===Original===

There was method in Zaemon’s desperate work, and this in my blind panic of love and haste, I had overlooked. So I went up the steps of the throne on the side from which the great capstone was moving, and clung there afire with expectation.

===Chinese===

泽蒙那拼死的一番作为里自有章法，而这一点，我在爱情与匆忙的盲目慌乱里竟忽略了。于是我登上御座的台阶，到御座石移动的那一侧，满腔期望灼灼地攀在那里。

===Original===

More and more violent did the earth-swing grow, though the graduations of its increase could not be perceived, and the din of falling houses and the shrieks and cries of hurt and frightened people went louder up into the night. Thicker grew the dust that filled the air, till one coughed and strangled in the breathing, and more black did the night become as the dust rose and blotted the rare stars from sight. I clung to an angle of the granite throne, crouching on the uppermost step but one below the capstone, and could scarcely keep my place against the violence of the earth tremors.

===Chinese===

大地的摇摆愈发狂暴，虽然加厉的层次无从察觉；房屋倒塌的轰响、伤者与惊惶者的尖啼哭喊，愈来愈高地灌入夜空。弥漫空气的尘土愈来愈浓，呛得人咳嗽气结；尘土升起，把稀疏的星也遮得看不见了，夜色愈见黑沉。我攀住花岗岩御座的一角，蹲在御座石下面倒数第二级台阶上，在剧烈的地颤中几乎站不住脚跟。

===Original===

But still the huge capstone that was carved with the snake and the outstretched hand held my love fast locked in her living tomb, and I could have bit the cold granite at the impotence which barred me from her. The people who kept thronging into the square were mad with terror, but their very numbers made my case more desperate every moment. “Phorenice, Goddess, aid us now!” some cried, and when the prayer did not bring them instant relief, they fell to yammering out the old confessions of the faith which they had learned in childhood, turning in this hour of their dreadful need to those old Gods, which, through so many dishonourable years, they had spurned and deserted. It was a curious criticism on the balance of their real religion, if one had cared to make it.

===Chinese===

然而那雕着蛇与舒展之手的巨大御座石，依旧把我的爱人牢牢锁在她的活坟墓里；这份把我隔绝在她之外的无力，激得我恨不得去咬那冰冷的花岗岩。不断涌进广场的人群已吓疯了，但他们人数愈多，我的处境便每一刻都愈加无望。有人喊：「福伦尼刻，女神，快来救助我们！」祷告没能立刻解救他们，他们便开始呜呜呀呀，背起童年学旧信仰时的那些老告白，在这可怕的急难时刻求告起老诸神来——多少个不体面的年头里，他们唾弃、背弃的正是这些神。倘若有人存心作评，这倒是对他们真实信仰分量的一番妙评。

===Original===

Louder grew the crash of falling masonry; and from the royal pyramid itself, though indeed I could not even see its outline through the darkness, there came sounds of grinding stones and cracking bars of metal which told that even its superb majestic strength had a breaking strain. There came to my mind the threat that old Zaemon had thundered forth in that painted, perfumed banqueting-hall: “You shall see,” he had cried to the Empress, “this royal pyramid which you have polluted with your debaucheries torn tier from tier, and stone from stone, and scattered as feathers spread before a wind!”

===Chinese===

坍落的砖石声愈发响亮；而从王家金字塔本身——虽然隔着黑暗我连它的轮廓也看不见——传来石头磨轧、金属棒崩裂的声响，说明即便是它那超凡庄严的伟力，也有崩断的极限。老泽蒙当年在那绘彩薰香的大宴殿上雷霆般发出的威胁，此刻回到了我心头：「你将亲眼看见，」他曾向女皇厉喝，「这座被你的淫乐玷污了的王家金字塔，一层接一层、一块接一块地被撕裂，像风前四散的羽毛一样扬尽！」

===Original===

Still heavier grew the surging of the earth, and the pavement of the great square gaped and upheaved, and the people who thronged it screamed still more shrilly as their feet were crushed by the grinding blocks. And now too the great pyramid itself was commencing to split, and gape, and topple. The roofs of its splendid chambers gave way, and the ponderous masonry above shuttered down and filled them. In part, too, one could see the destruction now, and not guess at it merely from the fearful hearings of the darkness. Thunders had begun to roar through the black night above, and add their bellowings to this devil’s orchestration of uproar, and vivid lightning splashes lit the flying dust-clouds.

===Chinese===

大地的涌动愈见沉重，大广场的铺石豁裂、拱起，广场上的人群脚被碾滚的石块压碎，尖叫声愈加凄厉。而此刻，大金字塔本身也开始迸裂、豁开、倾侧。它那些华美殿室的顶盖塌了，上面的沉重砖石如百叶骤合般砸落，把殿室填满。这场毁灭如今也能部分亲眼看见了，不必单凭黑暗中骇人的声响去悬揣。雷霆开始在头顶的黑夜里轰鸣，为这场恶魔般的喧嚣大合奏再添咆哮，一片片炫目的电光照亮飞卷的尘云。

===Original===

It was perhaps natural that she should be there, but it came as a shock when a flare of the lightning showed me Phorenice safe out in the square, and indeed standing not far from myself.

===Chinese===

她会在那里，或许本属情理之中；但一道电光让我看见福伦尼刻安然无恙地站在广场上、而且确实站得离我不远时，我还是悚然一惊。

===Original===

She had taken her place in the middle of a great flagstone, and stood there swaying her supple body to the shocks. Her face was calm, and its loveliness was untouched by the years. From time to time she brushed away the dust as it settled on the short red hair which curled about her neck. There was no trace of fear written upon her face. There was some weariness, some contempt, and I think a tinge of amusement. Yes, it took more than the crumbling of her royal pyramid to impress Phorenice with the infinite powers of those she warred against.

===Chinese===

她拣了一块巨大的铺面石板，站在中央，柔软的身体随着一次次震撼摇曳。她面色平静，容颜的美未受岁月侵蚀。蜷在她颈边的红色短发落上尘埃，她时时抬手拂去。她脸上没有一丝惧色的痕迹。倒有几分倦怠，几分轻蔑，我想，还有一丝觉得好玩的意味。是啊，单是她的王家金字塔崩塌，还不足以让福伦尼刻对她交战对手的无边伟力心生敬畏。

===Original===

Gods! How the sight of her cool indifference maddened me then. I had it in me to have strangled her with my hands if she had come within my reach. But as it was, she stood in her place, swaying easily to the earth-waves as a sailor sways on a ship’s deck, and beside her, crouched on the same great flagstone, and overcome with nausea was Ylga, who again was raised to be her fan-girl. It came to my mind that Ylga was twin sister to Nais, and that I owed her for an ancient kindness, but I had leisure to do nothing for her then, and indeed it was little enough I could have done. With each shock the great capstone of the throne to which I clung jarred farther and farther from its bed place, and my love was coming nearer to me. It was she who claimed all my service then.

===Chinese===

诸神啊！她那副冷淡漠然的样子，当时是怎样令我怒狂。倘若她进到我伸手可及的地方，我满腔的血性足够叫我徒手扼死她。但事实是，她站在原处，如水手立在船甲板上一般从容地随地浪摇曳；在她旁边、蹲在同一块大石板上呕恶难支的，是伊尔加——她再度被提拔为福伦尼刻的执扇侍女。我想到伊尔加是娜伊丝的孪生姊妹，又想起旧日欠她的一份恩情；但那时我无暇为她做什么，而且说实在的，我所能做的也实在有限。每一次震撼，我攀附着的御座石便从座床上震离得愈来愈远，我的爱人正离我愈来愈近。那时，独要我全部效忠的，是她。

===Original===

Once in their blind panic a knot of the people in the square thought that the granite stone was too solid to be overturned, and saw in it an oasis of safety. They flocked towards it, many of them dragging themselves up the steep deep high steps on hands and knees because their feet had been injured by the billowing flagstones of the square.

===Chinese===

有那么一回，广场上一小簇人在盲目的恐慌里认定那块花岗岩巨石坚实得翻不动，把它看作一片安全的绿洲。他们朝它涌来，许多人因为脚已被广场上如浪翻掀的石板砸伤，只能手脚并用地往那又高又陡又深的台阶上爬。

===Original===

But I was in no mood to have the place profaned by their silly tremblings and stares: I beat at them with my hands, tearing them away, and hurling them back down the steepness of the steps. They asked me what was my title to the place above their own, and I answered them with blows and gnashing teeth. I was careless as to what they thought me or who they thought me. Only I wished them gone. And so they went, wailing and crying that I was a devil of the night, for they had no spirit left to defend themselves.

===Chinese===

但我全无心情让这地方被他们愚蠢的战栗和瞪视亵渎：我抡起双手扑打，把他们撕扯开，一个个踹下陡峭的台阶。他们问我凭什么名分占这地方、压过他们，我用拳脚和咬牙切齿作答。他们把我当作什么、认作什么人，我全不在意。我只想要他们走开。于是他们走了，一路哀号哭喊，说我是个夜鬼——他们已经没有一点自卫的气力了。

===Original===

Farther and farther the great stone that made the top of the throne slid out from its bed, but its slowness of movement maddened me. A life’s education left me in that moment, and I had no trace of stately patience left. In my puny fury I thrust at the great block with my shoulder and head, and clawed at it with my hands till the muscles rose on me in great ropes and knots, and the High Gods must have laughed at my helplessness as They looked. All was being ordered by the Three who were Their trusted servants, in Their good time. The work of the Gods may be done slowly, but it is done exceeding sure.

===Chinese===

构成御座顶的那块巨石从座床上滑出得愈来愈远，但它动得那样慢，简直令我发狂。一生的教养在那一刻离我而去，我连一丝庄重的耐心也不剩了。我以自己渺小的狂怒，用肩、用头去顶那巨块，用双手去抠它抓它，直到肌肉像粗绳和绳结一样在我身上暴起；至上诸神看在眼里，想必也要笑我的无能。一切都由三人——诸神信得过的仆役——按诸神自己的佳时调度着。诸神的功业也许成得迟缓，但必定成得万无一失。

===Original===

But at last, when all the people of the city were numb with terror, and incapable of further emotion (save only for Phorenice who still had nerve enough to show no concern), what had been threatened came to pass. The capstone of the throne slid out till it reached the balance, and the next shock threw it with a roar and a clatter to the ground. And then a strange tremor seized me.

===Chinese===

但终于，当全城的人都吓麻了、再也生不出新的情绪时（唯独福伦尼刻例外，她还有足够的神经装作毫不在意），所威胁过的一幕应验了。御座石向外滑出，直到越过平衡的临界，下一阵震撼便轰隆一声哐啷把它掀翻在地。于是，一阵奇异的战栗攫住了我。

===Original===

After all the scheming and effort, what I had so ardently prayed for had come about; but yet my inwards sank at the thought of mounting on the stone where I had mounted before, and taking my dear from the hollow where my hands had laid her. I knew Phorenice’s vengefulness, and had a high value for her cleverness. Had she left Nais to lie in peace, or had she stolen her away to suffer indignities elsewhere? Or had she ended her sleep with death, and (as a grisly jest) left the corpse for my finding? I could not tell; I dared not guess. Never during a whole hard-fighting life have my emotions been so wrenched as they were at that moment. And, for excuse, it must be owned that love for Nais had sapped my hardihood over a matter in which she was so privately concerned.

===Chinese===

机关算尽、力气使遍之后，我热切祈求的事终于来了；可一想到要登上我从前登过的那块石头，从我双手安放她的凹穴里抱起我的亲爱的，我的腑脏却往下沉。我深知福伦尼刻的记仇，也把她的机敏估得很高。她是让娜伊丝仍旧安睡在原处，还是把她偷走了、弄到别处去受凌辱？还是用死亡了结了她的睡眠，（当作一个阴森的玩笑）把尸首留给我来发现？我说不出，也不敢猜。整整一生苦斗恶战里，我的情绪从未像那一刻那样遭人拧绞。而作为辩解，必须承认：对娜伊丝的爱，在这一件与她如此切身相关的事上，早已磨软了我的胆气。

===Original===

It began to come to my mind, however, that the infernal uproar of the earth tremor was beginning to slacken somewhat, as though Zaemon knew he had done the work that he had promised, and was minded to give the wretched city a breathing space. So I took my fortitude in hand, and clambered up on to the flat of the stone. The lightning flashes had ceased and all was darkness again and stifling dust, but at any moment the sky might be lit once more, and if I were seen in that place, shaggy and changed though I might be, Phorenice, if she were standing near, would not be slow to guess my name and errand.

===Chinese===

不过我渐渐觉出，地颤那地狱般的喧嚣已开始略见松缓，仿佛泽蒙知道自己已办完答应过的活计，有心给这悲惨的城市一口喘息的工夫。于是我鼓起胆气，攀上那块石头的平面。闪电已经止歇，四下又只剩黑暗和呛人的尘土；但天空随时可能再亮，倘若我在那地方被人看见，那么任凭我如何须发蓬乱、形貌大改，只要福伦尼刻站在近旁，她要猜出我的名字和来意，绝不会慢。

===Original===

So changed was I for the moment, that I will finely confess that the idea of a fight was loathsome to me then. I wanted to have my business done and get gone from the place.

===Chinese===

我一时竟变成这样，倒不妨爽快承认：那时节，就连打斗一场的念头在我也是可憎的。我只想办完自己的事，赶紧离开那地方。

===Original===

With hands that shook, I fumbled over the face of the stone and found the clamps and bars of metal still in position where I had clenched them, and then reverently I let my fingers pass between these, and felt the curves of my love’s body in its rest beneath. An exultation began to whirl within me. I did not know if she had been touched since I last left her; I did not know if the drug would have its due effect, and let her be awakened to warmth and sight again; but, dead or alive, I had her there, and she was mine, mine, mine, and I could have yelled aloud in my joy at her possession.

===Chinese===

我用发抖的双手在石面上摸索，找到那些铁夹和铁条仍扣在我当初扣紧的位置上；然后我怀着敬畏，让手指从它们之间探进去，摸到了下面安眠的爱人身体的曲线。一阵狂喜开始在我胸中盘旋激荡。自我上次离开她之后，她可曾被惊扰，我不知道；那迷药能不能如数见效、让她重新暖过来、重见天日，我也不知道；但无论她是死是活，我总算把她得在手里，她是我的，我的，我的——这份占有的狂喜，几乎叫我放声长啸。

===Original===

Still the earth shook beneath us, and masonry roared and crashed into ruin. I had to cling to my place with one hand, whilst I unhasped the clamps of metal that made the top of her prison with the other. But at last I swung the upper half of them clear, and those which pinned down her feet I let remain. I stooped and drew her soft body up on to the flat of the stone beside me, and pressed my lips a hundred times to the face I could not see.

===Chinese===

大地仍在脚下摇撼，砖石轰然崩毁。我只得一手攀定身子，一手去解开那构成她牢狱之顶的金属搭扣。终于，我把上半圈的搭扣尽数翻起，压住她双脚的那些便任它们留着。我俯下身，把她柔软的身体拉上来，放到我身旁的石面上，对着那张我看不见的脸，上百次地印下亲吻。

===Original===

Some mad thought took me, I believe, that the mere fierceness and heat of my kisses would bring her back again to life and wakefulness. Indeed I will own plainly, that I did but sorry credit to my training in calmness that night. But she lay in my arms cold and nerveless as a corpse, and by degrees my sober wits returned to me.

===Chinese===

我想，当时有一种疯狂的念头攫住了我：单凭我亲吻的炽烈与滚热，就能把她重新唤回生命和清醒。说真的，我愿意明白承认，那一夜我实在有愧于平素那份沉静的修养。但她躺在我怀里，冰冷瘫软，像一具尸首，我的清醒神智这才一点一点回来了。

===Original===

This was no place for either of us. Let the earth’s tremors cease (as was plainly threatened), let daylight come, and let a few of these nerveless people round recover from their panic, and all the great cost that had been expended might be counted as waste. We should be seen, and it would not be long before someone put a name to Nais; and then it would be an easy matter to guess at Deucalion under the beard and the shaggy hair and the browned nakedness of the savage who attended on her. Tell of fright? By the Gods! I was scared as the veriest trembler who blundered amongst the dust-clouds that night when the thought came to me.

===Chinese===

这地方，我们两个哪一个也不能再留。只等地颤止息（这征象已明摆着），只等天光一亮、周围那些瘫软的人里有几个从恐慌里缓过神来，先前耗去的那笔巨大代价，就可能尽数算作虚掷。我们会被人看见，不出多久就会有人给娜伊丝安上名字；再顺着那部胡须、那蓬乱发，和那个侍立在她身旁、晒得赤褐的裸身野蛮人，猜出德乌卡利翁来，便是轻而易举的事了。说到受惊？凭诸神起誓！这念头袭来的时候，我吓得不亚于那一夜在尘云里跌跌撞撞的头一号抖索汉。

===Original===

With all that ruin spread around, it would be hopeless to think that any of those secret galleries which tunnelled under the ground would be left unbroken, and so it was useless to try a passage under the walls by the old means. But I had heard shouts from that frightened mob which came to me through the din and the darkness, that gave another idea for escape. “The city is accursed,” they had cried: “if we stay here it will fall on us. Let us get outside the walls where there are no buildings to bury us.”

===Chinese===

眼见破坏铺展得这样广，还想指望那些穿行地下的秘密坑道有一条完好无损，便是绝望；想循旧法从城墙底下穿出去，也就无用了。但在喧嚣与黑暗中，我听见那惊惶的人群里传来的喊叫，给了我另一条脱身的路。「这城受了诅咒，」他们喊道，「再待下去，它就要塌在我们头上了。到城墙外面去吧，那边没有房子来埋我们。」

===Original===

If they went, I could not see. But one gate lay nearest to the royal pyramid, and I judged that in their panic they would not go farther than was needful. So I put the body of Nais over my shoulder (to leave my right arm free) and blundered off as best I could through the stifling darkness.

===Chinese===

他们走了没有，我看不见。但离王家金字塔最近的只有一道城门，我料他们惊慌之下，决不肯多走一步不必走的路。于是我把娜伊丝的身体扛上肩头（好腾出右臂），在呛人的黑暗里跌跌撞撞，尽力而行。

===Original===

It was hard to find a direction; it was hard to walk in the inky darkness over ground that was tossed and tumbled like a frozen sea: and as the earth still quaked and heaved, it was hard also to keep a footing. But if I did fall myself a score of times, my dear burden got no bruise, and presently I got to the skirts of the square, and found a street I knew. The most venomous part of the shaking was done, and no more buildings fell, but enough lay sprawled over the roadway to make walking into a climb, and the sweat rolled from me as I laboured along my way.

===Chinese===

难在辨向；难在墨黑一片里走过那像一片冻海般翻腾起伏的地面；大地还在震耸，站住脚也难。但即便我自己摔了二十来跤，我亲爱的负担却没有添一处青肿；不久我到了广场的边沿，认出一条熟识的街。最毒的一段震摇已经过去，房屋也不再倒了，但瘫在路上的还是多得叫走路变成攀爬，我一路奋力前行，汗水滚滚直下。

===Original===

There was no difficulty about passing the gate. There was no gate. There was no wall. The Gods had driven their plough through it, and it lay flat, and proud Atlantis stood as defenceless as the open country. Though I knew the cause of this ruin, though, in fact, I had myself in some measure incited it, I was almost sad at the ruthlessness with which it had been carried out. The royal pyramid might go, houses and palaces might be levelled, and for these I cared little enough; but when I saw those stately ramparts also filched away, there the soldier in me woke, and I grieved at this humbling of the mighty city that once had been my only mistress.

===Chinese===

过城门毫无难处。因为已经没有城门。也没有城墙。诸神驾着他们的犁从中犁过，它平塌在地，堂堂的亚特兰蒂斯像旷野一样不设防。这场毁灭的缘由我知道，事实上还是我自己多少招引来的，可眼见它施行得这般无情，我心里仍不免近乎哀伤。王家金字塔尽可以倒，宫室民居尽可以夷平，这些我都无甚所谓；但当我看见那些威严的壁垒也这样被掳去，我身上的军人醒了过来，为这座曾是我唯一情人的雄城遭此屈辱而哀恸。

===Original===

But this was only a passing regret, a mere touch of the fighting-man’s pride. I had a different love now, that had wrapped herself round me far deeper and more tightly, and my duty was towards her first and foremost. The night would soon be past, and then dangers would increase. None had interfered with us so far, though many had jostled us as I clambered over the ruins; but this forbearance could not be reckoned upon for long. The earth tremors had almost died away, and after the panic and the storm, then comes the time for the spoiling.

===Chinese===

但这不过是一时怅惘，不过是武人自尊的一点触痛。如今我有了另一种爱，她把我缠绕得更深、更紧，我的本分头一件是向着她。夜很快就要过去，届时危险有增无减。至今还没有人来搅扰我们，虽然我攀过废墟时，许多人与我们挤撞；但这种容忍不能指望长久。地颤差不多止息了，而大恐慌与大风暴过后，接着来的便是趁乱劫掠的时辰。

===Original===

All men who were poor would try to seize what lay nearest to their hands, and those of higher station, and any soldiers who could be collected and still remained true to command, would ruthlessly stop and strip any man they saw making off with plunder. I had no mind to clash with these guardians of law and property, and so I fled on swiftly through the night with my burden, using the unfrequented ways; and crying to the few folk who did meet me that the woman had the plague, and would they lend me the shelter of their house as ours had fallen. And so in time we came to the place where the rope dangled from the precipice, and after Nais had been drawn up to the safety of the Sacred Mountain, I put my leg in the loop of the rope and followed her.

===Chinese===

穷人们个个都会伸手去抢手边最近的东西；而出身较高的，连同任何还能纠集起来、仍旧听命的兵士，则会毫不留情地拦下每一个被他们看见携赃脱身的人，把他剥个精光。我不想与这些法律与财产的守护者冲突，于是背起我的负担，拣僻静的路，在夜色里疾行；迎面遇着寥寥几个行人，我便朝他们喊，说这女人染了瘟疫，又求他们行行好，借他们家的屋顶遮一遮，因为我们家已经塌了。就这样，我们终于到了绳索从崖顶垂下来的地方；娜伊丝先被拽上圣山的安全地带，然后我把腿伸进绳圈，跟着上去。

===Original===

Now came what was the keenest anxiety of all. We took the girl and laid her on a bed in one of the houses, and there in the lit room for the first time I saw her clearly. Her beauty was drawn and pale. Her eyes were closed, but so thin and transparent had grown the lids that one could almost see the brown of the pupil beneath them. Her hair had grown to inordinate thickness and length, and lay as a cushion behind and beside her head.

===Chinese===

接着来的是一切焦灼中最尖锐的一种。我们把姑娘抬进一间屋子，安放在一张床上；在那间亮着灯的屋子里，我第一次把她看得真切。她的美消瘦而苍白。双眼阖着，但眼睑已长得又薄又透明，几乎看得见底下瞳仁的褐色。她的头发长得异常浓密修长，在脑后和头侧堆成一个垫子。

===Original===

There was no flicker of breath; there was none of that pulsing of the body which denotes life; but still she had not the appearance of ordinary death. The Nais I had placed nine long years before to rest in the hollow of the stone, was a fine grown woman, full bosomed, and well boned. The Nais that remained for me was half her weight. The old Nais it would have puzzled me to carry for an hour: this was no burden to impede a grown man.

===Chinese===

没有一丝呼吸的颤动；没有那标识生命的躯体搏动；但她也仍没有寻常死人的模样。九年之前我安放进石头凹穴里安息的那个娜伊丝，是个丰美的成年女子，胸乳饱满，骨相匀停。留给我的这个娜伊丝，只有从前一半的分量。旧日的娜伊丝，要我抱上一个时辰也嫌吃力；这一个，却绊不住一个成年男子的脚步。

===Original===

In other ways too she had altered. The nails of her fingers had grown to such a great length that they were twisted in spirals, and the fingers themselves and her hands were so waxy and transparent that the bony core upon which they were built showed itself beneath the flesh in plain dull outline. Her clay-cold lips were so white, that one sighed to remember the full beauty of their carmine. Her shoulders and neck had lost their comely curves, and made bony hollows now in which the dust of entombment lodged black and thickly.

===Chinese===

在别的方面她也变了。她的指甲长得极长，绞成了一个个螺旋；手指和手掌蜡也似地透明，撑着它们的骨芯隔着皮肉，显出一片分明而晦暗的轮廓。她泥土般冰冷的唇是那样白，叫人想起它旧日胭脂红的丰美，不由得叹息。她的肩和颈失去了好看的曲线，如今塌成嶙峋的凹窝，里面黑黑地、厚厚地积着入殓的尘埃。

===Original===

Reverently I set about preparing those things which if all went well should restore her. I heated water and filled a bath, and tinctured it heavily with those essences of the life of beasts which the Priests extract and store against times of urgent need and sickness. I laid her chin-deep in this bath, and sat beside it to watch, maintaining that bath at a constant blood heat.

===Chinese===

我满怀敬畏地着手备办那些东西——若一切顺遂，它们该能使她复原。我烧热了水，注满一浴盆，又把祭司们提炼贮存、以备急难病痛之需的兽命精华（essences of the life of beasts）浓浓地调了进去。我把她放进浴盆，水深及颏，自己坐在盆边守望，让盆水恒常保持血液的温度。

===Original===

An hour I watched; two hours I watched; three hours﻿—and yet she showed no flicker of life. The heat of her body given her by the bath, was the same as the heat of my own. But in the feel of her skin when I stroked it with my hand, there was something lacking still. Only when our Lord the Sun rose for His day did I break off my watching, whilst I said the necessary prayer which is prescribed, and quickly returned again to the gloom of the house.

===Chinese===

我守望了一个钟头；守望了两个钟头；三个钟头——她仍没有一丝生命的悸动。浴盆给了她体温，那温度已与我自己的相同。但用手抚过她的皮肤时，触感里仍旧缺着一点什么。直到太阳神主升起、开始他的一日，我才中断守望，诵了规定的必诵祷文，随即又快快回到屋子的幽暗里。

===Original===

I was torn with anxiety, and as the time went on and still no sign of life came back, the hope that had once been so high within me began to sicken and leave me downcast and despondent. From without, came the din of fighting. Already Phorenice had sent her troops to storm the passageway, and the Priests who defended it were shattering them with volleys of rocks. But these sounds of war woke no pulse within me. If Nais did not wake, then the world for me was ended, and I had no spirit left to care who remained uppermost. The Gods in Their due time will doubtless smite me for this impiety. But I make a confession of it here on these sheets, having no mind to conceal any portion of this history for the small reason that it does me a personal discredit.

===Chinese===

焦灼把我撕扯着；时光一点点过去，生命的征兆仍不回来，我心头一度那么高扬的希望开始萎病，丢下我垂头丧气、灰心失望。外面传来厮杀的喧嚣。福伦尼刻已发兵猛攻那条通路，守路的祭司们正用一阵阵滚石把他们砸得粉碎。但这些战声在我体内唤不起半点搏动。娜伊丝若不醒来，我的世界便算到了头，谁最后占得上风，我已无心去计较。诸神到了时候，必定要为这番不虔降罚于我。但我在这些书页上把它招认出来，决不肯因为区区有损我个人体面的小缘故，便把这段历史隐去一节。

===Original===

But as the hours went on, and still no flicker of life came to lessen the dumb agony that racked me, I grew more venturesome, and added more essences to the bath, and drugs also such as experience had shown might wake the disused tissues into life. I watched on with staring eyes, rubbing her wasted body now and again, and always keeping the heat of the bath at a constant. From the first I had barred the door against all who would have come near to help me. With my own hands I had laid my love to sleep, and I could not bear that others should rouse her, if indeed roused she should ever be. But after those first offers, no others came, and the snarl and din of fighting told of what occupied them.

===Chinese===

但随着时辰过去，仍无一丝生命的悸动来稍减那折磨我的无言苦楚，我便胆大起来，往浴盆里再加了些精华，又添了些经验证明能唤醒废置组织的药物。我瞪着眼守望下去，不时揉擦她消瘦的身体，始终让盆温恒定不移。从最初起，我就把门闩死，挡住一切想近前来帮忙的人。我既亲手送我的爱人入睡，便受不得旁人来唤醒她——倘若她终究醒得过来的话。但除了最初那几拨自告奋勇的，再没有人来；战事的嘶吼与喧嚣，说明了他们都忙着什么。

===Original===

It is hard to take note of small changes which occur with infinite slowness when one is all the while on the tense watch, and high strung though my senses were, I think there must have been some indication of returning life shown before I was keen enough to notice it. For of a sudden, as I gazed, I saw a faint rippling on the surface of the water of the bath. Gods! Would it come back again to my love at last﻿—this life, this wakefulness? The ripple died out as it had come, and I stooped my head nearer to the bath to try if I could see some faint heaving of her bosom some small twitching of the limbs. No, she lay there still without even a flutter of movement. But as I watched, surely it seemed to my aching eyes that some tinge was beginning to warm that blank whiteness of skin?

===Chinese===

人在始终绷紧的守望里，极难察觉那些以无限缓慢发生的小变化；我的感官虽已绷到极处，我想，在我变得足够敏锐、留意到之前，必定已有某种生机重回的征兆显露过了。因为蓦然间，就在我凝望之际，我看见浴盆的水面上漾起一道微弱的涟漪。诸神啊！这生命，这清醒，终究要回到我的爱人身上了么？涟漪像它来时那样消逝了，我把头俯得离浴盆更近，想看看能否寻见她胸口一丝微弱的起伏、四肢一点细小的抽动。没有，她躺在那里，连一丝颤动也没有。但在我注视之下，我酸痛的双眼确乎觉得，那死白的皮肤上，仿佛正开始透出一丝暖色？

===Original===

How I filled myself with that sight. The colour was returning to her again beyond a doubt. Once more the dried blood was becoming fluid and beginning again to course in its old channels. Her hair floated out in the liquid of the bath like some brown tangle of the ocean weed, and ever and again it twitched and eddied to some impulse which in itself was too small for the eye to see.

===Chinese===

我是怎样饱看这一幕的啊。血色确凿无疑地正回到她身上。干涸的血重又化开成液，开始在旧日的河道里奔流。她的头发在浴水里漂散开来，像一蓬纠结的棕色海草，不时随着某种本身细微得眼睛看不见的冲动而抽动、旋成小小的涡。

===Original===

She had slept for nine long years, and I knew that the wakening could be none of the suddenest. Indeed, it came by its own gradations and with infinite slowness, and I did not dare do more to hasten it. Further drugs might very well stop eternally what those which had been used already had begun. So I sat motionless where I was, and watched the colour come back, and the waxenness go, and even the fullness of her curves in some small measure return. And when growing strength gave her power to endure them, and she was racked with those pains which are inevitable to being born back again in this fashion to life, I too felt the reflex of her agony, and writhed in loving sympathy.

===Chinese===

她睡了整整九年，我知道这苏醒决不会是突兀的。它果然循着自己的层次、带着无限的缓慢而来，我不敢再多做什么去催促。再加药物，很可能把已经用下的那些所开启的东西，永远地堵死。于是我坐在原处一动不动，看血色回来，蜡气褪去，甚至她曲线的丰盈也小小地恢复了些许。等到渐长的气力使她经受得起了，她便被那些剧痛绞榨——以这种方式重新生还人世，这痛楚是免不了的——我也感到了她苦痛的反响，怀着爱的同情，与她一同扭动。

===Original===

Still further, too, was I wrung by a torment of doubt as to whether life or these rackings would in the end be conqueror. After each paroxysm the colour ebbed back from her again, and for a while she would lie motionless. But strength and power seemed gradually to grow, and at last these prevailed, and drove death and sleep beneath them. Her eyelids struggled with their fastenings. Her lips parted, and her bosom heaved. With shivering gasps her breath began to pant between her reddening lips. At first it rattled dryly in her throat, but soon it softened and became more regular. And then with a last effort her eyes, her glorious loving eyes, slowly opened.

===Chinese===

更有甚者，究竟生命还是这番绞榨终将为胜，这疑虑的煎熬也把我拧着。每次剧痛发作之后，血色又从她身上退去，有一阵子她会一动不动地躺着。但气力与生机似乎在渐渐增长，终于占了上风，把死亡与沉睡压到了底下。她的眼睑挣着它们的黏合。她的双唇张开，胸口起伏。她哆嗦着喘息，呼吸开始在重染上颜色的唇间急促起来。起初，那呼吸在她喉咙里干涩地咯咯作响，但很快便柔和下来，变得更有节律。然后，经过最后的努力，她的双眼，她那双光辉的、含情的眼睛，缓缓睁开了。

===Original===

I leaned over and called her softly by name.

===Chinese===

我俯下身去，柔声唤她的名字。

===Original===

Her eyes met mine, and a glow arose from their depths that gave me the greatest joy I have met in all the world.

===Chinese===

她的目光迎上我的，一股光辉从她眼底升起，给了我此生在世上所得的最大喜悦。

===Original===

“Deucalion, my love,” she whispered. “Oh, my dear, so you have come for me. How I have dreamed of you! How I have been racked! But it was worth it all for this.”

===Chinese===

「德乌卡利翁，我的爱人，」她低语道，「啊，我亲爱的，你到底来接我了。我梦了你多少回！我受了多少绞榨般的痛！但为了这一刻，一切都值了。」

## XVIII. Storm of the Sacred Mountain / 十八　圣山的风暴

===Original===

It was Nais herself who sent me to attend to my sterner duties. The din of the attack came to us in the house where I was tending her, and she asked its meaning. As pithily as might be, for she was in no condition for tedious listening, I gave her the history of her nine years’ sleep.

===Chinese===

正是娜伊丝自己打发我去尽我更为严峻的职责的。攻战的喧嚣传进我照看着她的那间屋子，她问那是什么意思。她那时的身子还经不起冗长的听讲，我便拣最简括的讲法，把她九年沉睡的始末说给了她听。

===Original===

The colour flushed more to her face. “My lord is the properest man in all the world to be King,” she whispered.

===Chinese===

她脸上愈见泛出血色。「我的夫君，是普天之下最合宜为王的人，」她低声说。

===Original===

“I refused to touch the trade till they had given me the Queen I desired, safe and alive, here upon the Mountain.”

===Chinese===

「他们不把我想要的那位王后安然无恙、活生生地送还到这圣山上来，我便不肯碰这桩买卖。」

===Original===

“How we poor women are made the chattels of you men! But, for myself, I seem to like the traffic well enough. You should not have let me stand in the way of Atlantis’ good, Deucalion. Still, it is very sweet to know you were weak there for once, and that I was the cause of your weakness. What is that bath over yonder? Ah! I remember; my wits seem none of the clearest just now.”

===Chinese===

「我们可怜的女人，就这样被你们男人当成可以买卖的家什！不过说到我自己，这桩买卖我似乎倒也做得心甘情愿。德乌卡利翁，你本不该让我妨了亚特兰蒂斯的好处。不过，知道你也有软弱了一回的时候，而且还是因我而软弱，这实在叫人心里发甜。那边那只浴盆是做什么用的？啊！我想起来了；我的脑子眼下似乎还不大清醒。」

===Original===

“You have made the beginning. Your strength will return to you by quick degrees. But it will not bear hurrying. You must have a patience.”

===Chinese===

「你已经开了个头。你的气力会很快一步接一步地回到你身上。只是急不得。你得有一份耐性。」

===Original===

“Your ear, sir, for one moment, and then I will rest in peace. My poor looks, are they all gone? You seem to have no mirror here. I had visions that I should wake up wrinkled and old.”

===Chinese===

「把你的耳朵借我片刻，先生，说完我便安心歇息。我可怜的容色，都憔悴尽了么？你这里似乎连一面镜子也没有。我先前尽梦见自己一觉醒来，满面皱纹，老态龙钟。」

===Original===

“You are as you were, dear, that first night I saw you﻿—the most beautiful woman in all the world.”

===Chinese===

「你同从前一样，亲爱的，同我头一夜见你时一模一样——你仍是普天之下最美的女子。」

===Original===

“I am pleased you like me,” she said, and took the cup of broth I offered her. “My hair seems to have grown; but it needs combing, sadly. I had a fancy, dear, once, that you liked ruddy hair best, and not a plain brown.” She closed her eyes then, lying back amongst the cushions where I had placed her, and dropped off into healthy sleep, with the smiles still playing upon her lips. I put the coverlet over her, and kissed her lightly, holding back my beard lest it should sweep her cheek. And then I went out of the chamber.

===Chinese===

「你喜欢我，我很高兴，」她说着，接过我递给她的那杯羹汤。「我的头发似乎长长了；只是乱得厉害，很该梳一梳。亲爱的，我从前有一度痴想，以为你最喜欢赤褐色的头发，而不是这种平平的棕色。」随后她阖上眼，靠在我安放她的那堆软垫里，唇边犹自漾着笑意，沉入了健康的酣睡。我给她掩上被子，轻轻吻了她一下，又拢住我的胡须，怕它扫着她的脸颊。然后我便走出了这间居室。

===Original===

That beard had grown vastly disagreeable to me these last hours, and then I went into a room in the house, and found instruments, and shaved it down to the bare chin. A change of robe also I found there and took it instead of my squalid rags. If a man is in truth a king, he owes these things to the dignity of his office.

===Chinese===

这几个钟头里，那部胡须已叫我腻烦透了，于是我走进屋中一间房，寻着了剃具，把它剃到下巴精光。我还寻到一件替换的袍子，便换下了我那身腌臜的破衣。一个人若果真是王，这些体面便是他欠其职分的。

===Original===

But, if the din of the fighting was any guide, mine was a narrowing kingdom. Every hour it seemed to grow fiercer and more near, and it was clear that some of the gates in the passage up the cleft in the cliff, impregnable though all men had thought them, had yielded to the vehemence of Phorenice’s attack. And, indeed, it was scarcely to be marvelled at. With all her genius spurred on to fury by the blow that had been struck at her by wrecking so fair a part of the city, the Empress would be no light adversary even for a strong place to resist, and the Sacred Mountain was no longer strong.

===Chinese===

不过，倘使战斗的喧嚣可作凭据，我这王国正日渐缩窄。厮杀声似乎一个钟头比一个钟头更猛烈、更逼近，而且很明显，通往崖隙的通道上那几道门——人人都曾以为是攻不破的——已有几道屈服在福伦尼刻攻势的狂猛之下了。其实这也几乎不足为怪。城中那么美好的一片被毁，这一击把她的全部天才激成了狂怒，这样一位女皇，纵是坚牢的要塞也难轻易抵挡，何况圣山早已不再坚固。

===Original===

Defences of stone, cunningly planned and mightily built, it still possessed, but these will not fight alone. They need men to line them, and, moreover, abundance of men. For always in a storm of this kind, some desperate fellows will spit at death and get to hand grips, or slingers and archers slip in their shot, or the throwing-fire gets home, or (as here) some newfangled machine like Phorenice’s fire-tubes, make one in a thousand of their wavering darts find the life; and so, though the general attacking loses his hundreds, the defenders also are not without their dead.

===Chinese===

石筑的防御工事，谋划精巧，构筑雄伟，圣山依旧不缺，可工事不会独自作战。它们需要人来据守，而且需要大量的人。因为每逢这一类强攻，总有亡命的家伙朝死亡啐上一口，抢到近前徒手相搏；或是投石手、弓手漏进一击；或是抛火（throwing-fire）掷中了标的；或是（如眼下这里）某种新奇造物，比如福伦尼刻的火管，叫他们飘忽无定的镖矢千发里有一发找着了性命；于是，攻的一将虽折损成百人马，守的一方也不是没有自家的死者。

===Original===

The slaughter, as it turned out, had been prodigious. As fast as the stormers came up, the Priests who held the lowest gate remaining to us rained down great rocks upon them till the narrow alley of the stair was paved with their writhing dead. But Phorenice stood on a spur of the rock below them urging on the charges, and with an insane valour company after company marched up to hurl themselves hopelessly against the defences. They had no machines to batter the massive gates, and their attack was as pathetically useless as that of a child who hammers against a wall with an orange; and meanwhile the terrible stones from above mowed them down remorselessly.

===Chinese===

事实证明，杀戮已经大得骇人。强攻的人马才一上来，据守我们仅存最低那道门的祭司们便巨石如雨般砸下，直到石阶间那条窄甬道被他们翻滚扭动的死者铺满。可福伦尼刻站在他们下方的一处岩嘴上督催冲锋，一队又一队的兵卒怀着癫狂的勇武开上来，全无指望地朝工事扑身送死。他们没有撞捶厚重门扇的器械，这进攻之无用，可怜得就像小孩子拿一只橘子捶墙；与此同时，头顶可怕的石块正无情地把他们成片刈倒。

===Original===

Company after company of the troops marched into this terrible deathtrap, and not a man of all of them ever came back. Nor was it Phorenice’s policy that they should do so. In her lust for this final conquest, she was minded to pour out troops till she had filled up the passes with the slain, so that at last she might march on to a level fight over the bridge of their poor bodies. It was no part of Phorenice’s mood ever to count the cost. She set down the object which was to be gained, and it was her policy that the people of Atlantis were there to gain it for her.

===Chinese===

一队又一队的兵卒开进这可怕的死阱，他们当中没有一个人回来过。这也本不合福伦尼刻方略的要求。她一心贪求这最后的征服，打定主意把兵马源源倾进来，直到用阵亡者填平各条通道，好教她最终踏着这班可怜人用尸身搭成的桥，走上前去打一场平地的仗。福伦尼刻的性子，向来不屑计算代价。她把要图得的目标定下来，她的方略便是：亚特兰蒂斯的百姓，生来就是替她去图这个目标的。

===Original===

Two gates then had she carried in this dreadful fashion, slaughtering those Priests that stood behind, them who had not been already shot down. And here I came down from above to take my share in the fight. There was no trumpet to announce my coming, no herald to proclaim my quality, but the Priests as a sheer custom picked up “Deucalion!” as a battle-cry; and some shouted that, with a King to lead, there would be no further ground lost.

===Chinese===

她便以这可怕的方式连夺了两道门，把守在门后、不曾先已被射倒的祭司尽数屠戮。正是在这里，我从上面下来，分担战斗。没有号角宣告我的到来，也没有传令官宣示我的身份，可祭司们出于纯粹的习惯，把「德乌卡利翁！」当作了呐喊的战号；有人高呼，有王率领，寸土不会再失。

===Original===

It was clear that the name carried to the other side and bore weight with it. A company of poor, doomed wretches who were hurrying up stopped in their charge. The word “Deucalion!” was bandied round and handed back down the line. I thought with some grim satisfaction, that here was evidence I was not completely forgotten in the land.

===Chinese===

很明显，这个名字传到了对面，而且带着分量。一队正急急赶来的、注定厄运的可怜虫在冲锋中停了下来。「德乌卡利翁！」这个词被口口相传，一路递回到队列后方。我带着几分冷峻的快意想道：这总算是个凭据，表明这片土地上的人还没有把我忘个干净。

===Original===

There came shouts to them from behind to carry on their advance; but they did not budge; and presently a glittering officer panted up, and commenced to strike right and left amongst them with his sword. From where I stood on the high rampart above the gate, I could see him plainly, and recognised him at once.

===Chinese===

后面传来喝令他们继续前进的呼喊；可他们纹丝不动；不一会儿，一位衣甲鲜明的军官喘着气奔上来，开始用剑在他们中间左右劈打。从我在门上高垒处所站的地方，我可以清清楚楚看见他，一眼便认了出来。

===Original===

“It matters not what they use for their battle-cry,” he was shouting. “You have the orders of your divine Empress, and that is enough. You should be proud to die for her wish, you cowards. And if you do not obey, you will die afterwards under the instruments of the tormentors, very painfully. As for Deucalion, he is dead any time these nine years.”

===Chinese===

「他们拿什么当战号，有什么要紧，」他正吼着。「你们奉的是神圣女皇的旨意，这便够了。为她的意愿而死，你们该觉得光荣，你们这班懦夫。你们若不服从，过后也要死在酷刑吏的刑具之下，苦不堪言。至于德乌卡利翁，这九年里他早就死了。」

===Original===

“There it seems you lie, my Lord Tatho,” I shouted down to him.

===Chinese===

「塔托大人，这话你说得可是谎，」我朝下面向他高声喊道。

===Original===

He started, and looked up at me.

===Chinese===

他一惊，抬头望我。

===Original===

“So you are there in real truth, then? Well, old comrade, I am sorry. But it is too late to make a composition now. You are on the side of these mangy Priests, and the Empress has made an edict that they are to be rooted out, and I am her most obedient servant.”

===Chinese===

「这么说，你当真在那里？唉，老伙计，我很遗憾。可如今再讲和已经太迟了。你站在这些癞皮祭司一边，而女皇已下了敕令，要将他们斩草除根，我是她最恭顺的臣仆。」

===Original===

“You used to be skilful of fence,” I said, and indeed there was little enough to choose between us. “If it please you to stop this pitiful killing, make yourself the champion of your side, and I will stand for mine, and we will fight out this quarrel in some fair place, and bind our parties to abide by the result.”

===Chinese===

「你从前剑术颇精，」我说——的确，我们二人之间实在难分高下。「你若愿意止住这可怜的杀戮，便请你做你那一边的斗士，我代表我这一边，我们寻一处公平的地方，把这场争执斗出个结果，并约束双方遵从这结果。」

===Original===

“It would be a grand fight between us two, old friend, and it goes hard with me to balk you of it. But I cannot pleasure you. I am general here under Phorenice, and she has given me the strongest orders not to peril myself. And besides, though you are a great man, Deucalion, you are not chief. You are not even one of the Three.”

===Chinese===

「我们两人之间打一场，定是一场好斗，老朋友，叫你扫兴，我心里也着实难受。可我无法从命。我是福伦尼刻麾下此地的主将，她给了我最严厉的命令，不许我亲身犯险。况且，德乌卡利翁，你纵是了不起的人物，却不是魁首。你连三人之一也不是。」

===Original===

“I am King.”

===Chinese===

「我是王。」

===Original===

Tatho laughed. “Few but yourself would say so, my lord.”

===Chinese===

塔托笑了。「除了你自己，肯这么说的人可不多，大人。」

===Original===

“Few truly, but what there are, they are powerful. I was given the name for the first time yesterday, and as a first blow in the campaign there was some mischief done in the city. I was there myself, and saw how you took it.”

===Chinese===

「确实不多，可仅有的这几个，都大有力量。这名字是昨天头一回加到我头上的，作为这场战事的第一击，城里已闯出了一点祸事。我本人当时就在城里，亲眼看见你们受那一击的模样。」

===Original===

“You were in Atlantis!”

===Chinese===

「你进过亚特兰蒂斯城！」

===Original===

“I went for Nais. She is on the mountain now, and tomorrow will be my Queen. Tatho, as a priest to a priest, let me solemnly bring to your memory the infinite power you bite against on this Sacred Mountain. Your teaching has warned you of the weapons that are stored in the Ark of the Mysteries. If you persist in this attack, at the best you can merely lose; at the worst you can bring about a wreck over which even the High Gods will shudder as They order it.”

===Chinese===

「我是为娜伊丝去的。她如今已在山上，明天便要做我的王后。塔托，凭着祭司对祭司，让我郑重地唤你记起，你在这圣山上死命啃咬的，是怎样一种无穷的力量。你所受的教诲早警告过你，奥秘的方舟里贮存着怎样的武器。你若执意攻下去，往好里说，你不过折损；往坏里说，你会闯出一场连至上诸神下令施行时也要为之战栗的大毁灭。」

===Original===

“You cannot scare us back now by words,” said Tatho doggedly. “And as for magic, it will be met by magic. Phorenice has found by her own cleverness as many powers as were ever stored up in the Ark of the Mysteries.”

===Chinese===

「如今你休想凭言语把我们吓退，」塔托顽固地说。「至于法术，自有法术来对付。福伦尼刻凭她自己的聪明，已找得了同奥秘的方舟里所贮存的一样多的威力。」

===Original===

“Yet she looked on helplessly enough last night, when her royal pyramid was trundled into a rubbish heap. Zaemon had prophesied that this should be so, and for a witness, why I myself stood closer to her than we two stand now, and saw her.”

===Chinese===

「可昨天夜里，她的王家金字塔滚成一堆瓦砾的时候，她眼睁睁看着，那份无助也够瞧的了。泽蒙早预言过事情会是如此，至于人证嘛，嘿，我自己当时站的地方离她比你我此刻还近，亲眼看见了她。」

===Original===

“I will own you took her by surprise somewhat there. I do not understand these matters myself; I was never more than one of the Seven in the old days; and now, quite rightly, Phorenice keeps the knowledge of her magic to herself: but it seems time is needed when one magic is to be met by another.”

===Chinese===

「我承认，你们那一下确实打了她个措手不及。这些事我自己不懂；我从前也不过是七人之一；如今呢，福伦尼刻把她的法术知识秘不示人，这也十分应该：不过一种法术要对付另一种法术，看来总需要些时间。」

===Original===

“Well,” I said, “I know little about the business either. I leave these matters now to those who are higher above me in the priesthood. Indeed, having a liking for Nais, it seems I am debarred from ever being given understanding about the highest of the higher Mysteries. So I content myself with being a soldier, and when the appointed day comes, I shall fall and kiss my mother the Earth for the last time. You, so I am told, have ambition for longer life.”

===Chinese===

「唉，」我说，「这行当我懂得也不多。这些事我如今都交给祭司职分里比我高的人。的确，只因我心里恋着娜伊丝，看来我永远无缘得授上乘奥秘中至高的那些了。所以我安于做个军人，等指定的日子一到，我便倒下去，最后一次亲吻我的母亲大地。而你呢，据我所闻，是存着更长生的雄心的。」

===Original===

He nodded. “Phorenice has found the Great Secret, and I am to be the first that will share it with her. We shall be as Gods upon the earth, seeing that Death will be powerless to touch us. And the twin sons she has borne me, will be made immortal also.”

===Chinese===

「福伦尼刻已经找到了大奥秘（the Great Secret），而我将是头一个与她共享的人。我们将如诸神一般立于世上，死亡再也无力碰我们。她给我生的一对孪生儿子，也将得成不死。」

===Original===

“Phorenice is headstrong. No, my lord, there is no need to shake your head and try to deny it. I have had some acquaintance with her. But the order has been made, and her immortality will be snatched from her very rudely. Now, mark solemnly my words. I, Deucalion, have been appointed King of Atlantis by the High Council of the Priests who are the mouthpiece of the most High Gods, and if I do not have my reign, then there will be no Atlantis left to carry either King or Empress. You know me, Tatho, for a man that never lies.”

===Chinese===

「福伦尼刻是执拗的。不，大人，不必摇头，也不必想要否认。我与她是打过些交道的。但命令已经下了，她的不死之身将被十分粗暴地夺走。现在，郑重记住我的话。我，德乌卡利翁，已由身为至上诸神喉舌的祭司最高议事会立为亚特兰蒂斯之王；我若得不到我的王朝，世上便将再没有亚特兰蒂斯来承载王或女皇。塔托，你知道我这个人从不撒谎。」

===Original===

He nodded.

===Chinese===

他点了点头。

===Original===

“Then save yourself before it is too late. You shall have again your viceroyalty in Yucatan.”

===Chinese===

「那么趁还来得及，救你自己吧。你可以重新得到你的尤卡坦总督之位。」

===Original===

“But, man, there is no Yucatan. A great horde of little hairy creatures, that were something less than human and something more than beasts, swept down upon our cities and ate them out. Oh, you may sneer if you choose! Others sneered when I came home, till the Empress stopped them. But you know what a train of driver ants is, that you meet with in the forests? You may light fires across their path, and they will march into them in their blind bravery, and put them out with their bodies, and those that are left will march on in an unbroken column, and devour all that stands in their path. I tell you, my lord, those little hairy creatures were like the ants﻿—aye, for numbers, and wooden bravery, as well as for appetite. As a result today, there is no Yucatan.”

===Chinese===

「可是，老兄，已经没有尤卡坦了。一大群多毛小人——比人少了些什么、又比兽多了些什么的东西——扑到我们的城池上，把它们啃吃了个精光。噢，你愿意冷笑就冷笑吧！我回国时别人也冷笑，直到女皇制止了他们。可你知道林子里迎面遇到的一列行军蚁（driver ants）是什么样的么？你可以在它们必经的路上点起火来，它们会凭着那股盲目的勇猛径直开进火里，用身体把火压灭，剩下的照旧结成不打散的纵队前进，把挡在路上一应东西统统吃光。我告诉你，大人，那些多毛小人就跟这些蚂蚁一样——论数目，论木头般的呆勇，也论胃口，无一不像。所以到今天，已经没有尤卡坦了。」

===Original===

“You shall have Egypt, then.”

===Chinese===

「那么，埃及归你。」

===Original===

He burst at me hotly. “I would not take seven Egypts and ten Yucatans. My lord, you think more poorly of me than is kind, when you ask me to become a traitor. In your place would you throw your Nais away, if the doing it would save you from a danger?”

===Chinese===

他激烈地冲我发作起来。「给我七个埃及、十个尤卡坦我也不换。大人，你请我去做叛徒，未免把我想得太不堪了。换作是你，倘若抛掉你的娜伊丝便能救你脱险，你会把她抛掉么？」

===Original===

“That is different.”

===Chinese===

「那是另一回事。」

===Original===

“In no degree. You have a kindness for her. I have all that and more for Phorenice, who is, besides, my wife and the mother of my children. If I have qualms﻿—and I freely confess I know you are desperate men up there, and have dreadful powers at your command﻿—my shiverings are for them and not for myself. But I think, my lord, this parley is leading to nothing, and though these common soldiers here will understand little enough of our talk, they may be picking up a word here and there, and I do not wish them to go on to their death (as you will see them do shortly) and carry evil reports about me to whatever Gods they chance to come before.”

===Chinese===

「毫无分别。你对她有一份情意。我对福伦尼刻有的是这情意还要更多，何况她还是我的妻子、我孩子的母亲。我若也有几分心悸——我爽快承认，我知道你们上面那班是亡命之徒，手中有骇人的威力可随时施展——我的战栗也是为她们母子，不是为我自己。不过我想，大人，这场阵前对话谈不出什么来，这里这些寻常兵卒纵然听不懂我们的话，零零碎碎捡上一言半语却是可能的，我不愿他们去送死（你不久便可见到他们送死的）时，还带着关于我的恶言，去到他们碰巧得见的无论哪位神面前。」

===Original===

He saluted me with his sword and drew back, and once more the missiles began to fly, and the doomed wretches, who had been halting beside the steep rock walls of the pass began once more to press hopelessly forward. They had scaling-ladders certainly, but they had no chance of getting these planted. They could do naught but fill the narrow way with their bodies, and to that end they had been sent, and to that end they humbly died. Our Priests with crow and lever wrenched from their lodging-places the great rocks which had been made ready, and sent them crashing down, so that once more screams filled the pass, and the horrid butchery was renewed.

===Chinese===

他举剑向我行了一礼，退了回去，矢石再度开始纷飞，那些本来停在通道陡峭岩壁旁的、注定厄运的可怜虫，又开始全无指望地向前挤压。他们确有云梯（scaling-ladder），却全无机会把云梯架立起来。他们一无所能，只能用自己的身体填满这条窄路；为这个目的他们被派来，也为这个目的他们俯首就死。我们的祭司们用铁撬和杠杆，把预先备好的巨石从安放的巢位里撬出，轰隆隆推将下去，于是尖叫声再度充满通道，可怕的屠杀重新开场。

===Original===

But ever and again, some arrow or some sling-stone, or some fire-tube’s dart would find its way up from below and through the defences, and there we would be with a man the less to carry on the fight. It was well enough for Phorenice to be lavish with her troops; indeed, if she wished for success, there were no two ways for it; and when those she had levied were killed, she could readily press others into the service, seeing that she had the whole broad face of the country under her rule. But with us it was different. A man down on our side was a man whose arm would bitterly be missed, and one which could in no possible way be replaced.

===Chinese===

可总有一支箭、一块投石，或火管的一镖，时时从下面钻上来、穿过防御，落到人身上，我们这边便又少了一人续战。福伦尼刻挥霍她的兵马，那不打紧；说实在的，她若要成功，也别无二法；她征来的兵死光了，还尽可以再拉别的人补上，因为全国上下广阔的土地尽在她治下。我们这边却不同。我们倒下一人，便是一条会叫人痛惜不已的臂膀，且无论如何也无法补上。

===Original===

I made calculation of the chances, and saw clearly that, if we continued the fight on the present plan, they would storm the gates one after another as they came to them, and that by the time the uppermost gate was reached, there would be no Priest alive to defend it. And so, not disdaining to fashion myself on Phorenice’s newer plan, which held that a general should at times in preference plot coldly from a place of some safety, and not lead the thick of the fighting, I left those who stood to the gate with some rough soldier’s words of cheer, and withdrew again up the narrow stair of the pass.

===Chinese===

我盘算了一下胜负之数，看得分明：我们若照眼前的打法打下去，他们便会一路强攻，一道门接一道门地夺，等打到最高一道门时，将再没有一个活着的祭司去守它了。于是，我并不耻于效法福伦尼刻那套较新的方略——即将领有时宜于退到较安全的地方冷冷地运筹，而不去亲领酣战——我向守门的这些人丢下几句军人粗豪的鼓劲话，便再次循着通道的窄石阶退了上去。

===Original===

This one approach to the Sacred Mountain was, as I have said before, vastly more difficult and dangerous in the olden days when it stood as a mere bare cleft as the High Gods made it. But a chasm had been bridged here, a shelf cut through the solid rock there, and in many places the roadway was built up on piers from distant crags below so as to make all uniform and easy. It came to my mind now, that if I could destroy this path, we might gain a breathing space for further effort.

===Chinese===

这条上圣山的独一路径，正如我先前说过的，在古时要难走、凶险得多，那时它只是至上诸神造下的一道光秃秃的裂罅。但后来这里架桥跨过了深壑，那里从整块山岩里凿出栈台，许多地方更从下方远处的岩崖立柱筑路，使全程平整易行。这时我心中一动：倘若我能把这路毁掉，我们或许能争得一个喘息之机，再作后图。

===Original===

The idea seemed good, or at least no other occurred to me which would in any way relieve our desperate situation, and I looked around me for means to put it into execution. Up and down, from the mountain to the plains below, I had traversed that narrow stair of a pass some thousands of times, and so in a manner of speaking knew every stone, and every turn, and every cut of it by heart. But I had never looked upon it with an eye to shaving off all roadway to the Sacred Mountain, and so now, even in this moment of dreadful stress, I had to traverse it no less than three times afresh before I could decide upon the best site for demolition.

===Chinese===

这主意似乎不坏，至少我再也想不出别的什么法子，能稍稍解一解我们这绝望的处境，于是我便四下里物色实行的手段。上山下山，从山顶到下面的平原，这条窄梯般的通道我总走过几千遍了，可以说每一块石头、每一个转弯、每一处开凿，都烂熟于心。可我从前从没有存着切断一切上圣山之路的心思去看它，所以此刻，纵然在这可怕的重压关头，我还不得不重新把它上上下下走了足三遍，才定下最宜拆毁的位置。

===Original===

But once the point was fixed, there was little delay in getting the scheme in movement. Already I had sent men to the storehouses amongst the Priests’ dwellings to fetch me rams, and crows, and acids, and hammers, and such other material as was needed, and these stood handy behind one of the upper gates. I put on every pair of hands that could be spared to the work, no matter what was their age and feebleness; yes, if Nais could have walked so far I would have pressed her for the labour; and presently carved balustrade, and wayside statue, together with the lettered wall-stones and the foot-worn cobbles, roared down into the gulf below, and added their din to the shrieks and yells and crashes of the fighting. Gods! But it was a hateful task, smashing down that splendid handiwork of the men of the past. But it was better that it should crash down to ruin in the abyss below, than that Phorenice should profane it with her impious sandals.

===Chinese===

但位置一定，推动这计划便几乎没有耽搁。我早已派人去祭司们居所间的库房，取来柱塞、铁撬、酸液、大锤，以及所需的别样器械，这些东西都顺手堆在上一道门的后面。凡是腾得出的人手，不论老弱，我一概派上工去；说真的，娜伊丝若是能走得那么远，连她我也拉去做工了；不多时，雕花的栏杆、路旁的造像，连同刻着铭文的墙石和被脚磨旧的铺石，便轰隆隆砸进下面的深渊，给战斗的尖啸、呼喊与崩裂声又添上一重喧响。诸神啊！砸毁前人那件件辉煌的手泽，这活计实在可憎。但总强似叫它坠入下面深渊摔个粉碎，也强似让福伦尼刻用她那双渎神的凉鞋践踏玷污。

===Original===

At first I had feared that it would be needful to sacrifice the knot of brave men who were so valiantly defending the gate then being attacked. It is disgusting to be forced into a measure of this kind, but in hard warfare it is often needful to the carrying out of his schemes for a general to leave a part of his troops to fight to a finish, and without hope of rescue, as valiantly as they may; and all he can do for their reward is to recommend them earnestly to the care of the Gods. But when the work of destroying the pathway was nearly completed, I saw a chance of retrieving them.

===Chinese===

起初我还担心，恐怕不得不牺牲掉那一小股正英勇守着受攻之门的好汉。被逼到这一步，实在叫人恶心，可在酷烈的战事里，将领为推行他的方略，常常不得不留下一部分队伍战至最后，全无生路地尽力打下去；他能为他们报酬的，只有把他们恳切托付给诸神照管。但等到毁路的工程将近完成时，我看见了把他们救回来的一个机会。

===Original===

We had not been content merely with breaking arches, and throwing down the piers. We had got our rams and levers under the living rock itself on which all the whole fabric stood; and fire stood ready to heat the rams for their work; and when the word was given, the whole could be sent crashing down the face of the cliffs beyond chance of repair.

===Chinese===

我们并不满足于仅仅折断拱券、推倒立柱。我们已把柱塞和杠杆塞到整座构筑所赖的活山岩本体之下；火也已备好，随时可把柱塞烧胀了去干它们的活；号令一下，整条路便可轰然崩下崖面，再无修复之望。

===Original===

All was, I say, finally prepared in this fashion, and then I gave the word to hold. A narrow ledge still remained undestroyed, and offered footway, and over this I crossed. The cut we had made was immediately below the uppermost gate of all, and below it there were three more massive gates still unviolated, besides the one then being so vehemently attacked. Already, the garrisons had been retired from these, and I passed through them all in turn, unchallenged and unchecked, and came to that busy rampart where the twelve Priests left alive worked, stripped to the waist, at heaving down the murderous rocks.

===Chinese===

我再说一遍，一切就这样最后备妥了，随后我下令住手。还剩一道窄窄的石檐未曾毁去，可供踏足，我便从这上面跨了过去。我们凿断的位置正在最高那道门的正下方，再往下，除当时正被猛攻的那道门外，还有三道厚重的门完好未动。这些门的守军都已撤下，我挨个儿一道道穿过，无人盘问，无人拦阻，来到那一片忙碌的壁垒上——尚活着的十二名祭司正赤着上身，在垒上搬运推下那杀人的巨石。

===Original===

For awhile I busied myself at their side, stopping an occasional fire-tube dart or arrow on my shield and passing them the tidings. The attack was growing fiercer every minute now. The enemy had packed the pass below well-nigh full of their dead, and our battering stones had less distance to fall and so could do less execution. They pressed forward more eagerly than ever with their scaling ladders, and it was plain that soon they would inevitably put the place to the storm. Even during the short time I was there, their sling-stones and missiles took life from three more of the twelve who stood with me on the defence.

===Chinese===

有一阵子我在他们身旁帮着忙，用盾牌挡下偶至的火管镖矢或箭，并给他们传递消息。此刻攻战一分钟比一分钟更猛。敌人已把下面的通道用尸首几乎填满，我们的砸人之石坠落的路程短了，杀伤也就小了。他们挟着云梯比先前更加急切地向前压，很明显，此地不久必将被他们攻陷。单是我在那里的那一小段时间里，他们的投石与矢弹便又从随我守御的十二人中取走了三条性命。

===Original===

So I gave the word for one more furious avalanche of rock to be pelted down, and whilst the few living were crawling out from those killed by the discharge, and whilst the next band of reinforcements came scrambling up over the bodies, I sent my nine remaining men away at a run up the steep stairway of the path, and then followed them myself. Each of the gates in turn we passed, shutting them after us, and breaking the bars and levers with which they were moved, and not till we were through the last did the roar of shouts from below tell that the besiegers had found the gate they bit against was deserted.

===Chinese===

于是我下令再倾下一阵狂暴的岩石雪崩，趁那阵攻势里活着的少数人正从死者堆里爬出、下一拨援兵正踩着尸身手脚并用地往上爬之际，我打发剩下的九个人撒腿沿陡峭的石阶路跑上去，自己也随在其后。一道道门我们挨个儿穿过，随手关上，并折断启闭门扇的门闩与杠杆，直到我们过了最后一道，下面才轰然响起呐喊，告诉说围攻者发现他们死啃的那道门已空无一人。

===Original===

One by one we balanced our way across the narrow ledge which was left where the path had been destroyed, and one poor Priest that carried a wound grew giddy, and lost his balance here, and toppled down to his death in the abyss below before a hand could be stretched out to steady him. And then, when we were all over, heat was put to the rams, and they expanded with their resistless force, and tore the remaining ledges from their hold in the rock. I think a pang went through us all then when we saw for ourselves the last connecting link cut away from between the poor remaining handful of our Sacred Clan on the Mountain, and the rest of our great nation, who had grown so bitterly estranged to us, below.

===Chinese===

我们一个接一个踩稳身子，跨过路上毁断处留下的那道窄石檐，有一个带着伤的可怜祭司头一晕，在那里失了平衡，还不等一只手伸出去扶稳他，便一头栽进下面的深渊殒命了。随后，等我们全都过来，火便烧上了柱塞，它们以不可抗拒的力量膨胀起来，把残余的石檐从岩中的嵌握里生生撕脱。我想，那一刻我们人人心里都掠过一阵刺痛：我们亲眼看见，山上的祭司氏族那可怜的残余一小撮，同山下我们那个已同我们积怨疏远至极的大民族之间，最后一条相连的纽带被斩断了。

===Original===

But here at any rate was a break in the fighting. There were no further preparations we could make for our defence, and high though I knew Phorenice’s genius to be, I did not see how she could very well do other than accept the check and retire. So I set a guard on the ramparts of the uppermost gate to watch all possible movements, and gave the word to the others to go and find the rest which so much they needed.

===Chinese===

不过，这至少换来了一场战斗的中歇。防务上我们再没有别的什么可筹备的了，我虽深知福伦尼刻的天才何等高妙，却也看不出她除了认下这一挫折、退兵而去，还能有什么别的做法。于是我在最高那道门的壁垒上设了岗哨，注视一切可能的动静，并吩咐其余的人各自去歇息——他们实在太需要休息了。

===Original===

For myself, dutifully I tried to find Zaemon first, going on the errand my proper self, for there was little enough of kingly state observed on the Sacred Mountain, although the name and title had been given me. But Zaemon was not to be come at. He was engaged inside the Ark of the Mysteries with another of the Three, and being myself only one of the Seven, I had not rank enough in the priesthood to break in upon their workings. And so I was free to turn where my likings would have led me first, and that was to the house which sheltered Nais.

===Chinese===

至于我自己，我恭恭敬敬先去寻泽蒙，这趟差使是亲自去的，因为圣山上虽给了我名号尊称，王家排场却讲究得极少。可泽蒙无法见到。他正与三人中的另一位在奥秘的方舟里忙于行事，而我只是七人之一，在祭司职分里品级不够，不便闯进去搅扰他们的作为。于是我倒得了自由，可以到我的心最先要去的地方去，那便是安顿着娜伊丝的屋子。

===Original===

She waked as I came in over the threshold, and her eyes filled with a welcome for me. I went across and knelt where she lay, putting my face on the pillow beside her. She was full of tender talk and sweet endearments. Gods! What an infinity of delight I had missed by not knowing my Nais earlier! But she had a will of her own through it all, and some quaint conceits which made her all the more adorable. She rallied me on the new cleanness of my chin, and on the robe which I had taken as a covering. She professed a pretty awe for my kingship, and vowed that had she known of my coming dignities she would never have dared to discover a love for me. But about my marriage with Phorenice she spoke with less lightness. She put out her thin white hand, and drew my face to her lips.

===Chinese===

我跨进门槛时她便醒了，眼里盈满了对我的欢迎。我走过去，跪在她躺卧的地方，把脸贴在她枕旁。她满心都是温存的话语和甜蜜的怜爱。诸神啊！我早不识得我的娜伊丝，错过了多少无穷的欢愉！可这中间她自有一副主见，还有些古雅的奇想，愈发显得她可爱。她取笑我下巴新近刮得干净，取笑我拿来蔽体的那件袍子。她对我这王位佯装出一番娇俏的敬畏，赌咒说倘若早知道我有今日的尊荣，她断不敢向我吐露爱意。只是谈到我与福伦尼刻的婚事，她的话音便不那么轻快了。她伸出纤白的手，把我的脸引到她唇边。

===Original===

“It is weak of me to have a jealousy,” she murmured, “knowing how completely my lord is mine alone; but I cannot help it. You have said you were her husband for awhile. It gives me a pang to think that I shall not be the first to lie in your arms, Deucalion.”

===Chinese===

「明知我的夫君完完全全只属于我一人，我却还要妒忌，这是我不济，」她喃喃道，「可我禁不住。你说过，你做过她一阵子的丈夫。一想到躺在你怀里头一个的不是我，德乌卡利翁，我心里就发疼。」

===Original===

“Then you may gaily throw your pang away,” I whispered back. “I was husband to Phorenice in mere word for how long I do not precisely know. But in anything beyond, I was never her husband at all. She married me by a form she prescribed herself, ignoring all the old rites and ceremonies, and whether it would hold as legal or not, we need not trouble to inquire. She herself has most nicely and completely annulled that marriage as I have told you. Tatho is her husband now, and father to her children, and he seems to have a fondness for her which does him credit.”

===Chinese===

「那么你尽管把这点疼快快活活丢开吧，」我低声答道。「我做福伦尼刻的丈夫，只是空有其名，为时多久我也说不确切。但在名分之外的事情上，我从来不曾做过她的丈夫。她依她自己拟定的一套仪文嫁了我，全不理会古来的种种婚仪礼节，至于这桩婚姻算不算合法，我们也犯不着费心追问。她自己早已把那桩婚事最干净彻底地废止了，这我告诉过你。如今塔托才是她的丈夫，是她孩子的父亲，而且他待她似乎颇有一片深情，这倒给他添光彩。」

===Original===

We said other things too in that chamber, those small repetitions of endearments which are so precious to lovers, and so beyond the comprehension of other folk, but they are not to be set down on these sheets. They are a mere private matter which can have no concern to anyone beyond our two selves, and more weighty subjects are piling themselves up in deep index for the historian.

===Chinese===

在那间屋子里我们还说了些别的，说了那些情人听来无比珍贵、旁人却全然无法领会的温存絮语，但它们不该落到这几页纸上来。那纯然是我们两个人的私事，与此外任何人无涉，而更重大的题目，正层层堆叠在史官的深册里等着呢。

===Original===

Phorenice, it seemed, had more rage against the Priests’ Clan on the Mountain and more bright genius to help her to a vengeance than I had credited. Her troops stormed easily the gates we had left to them, and swarmed up till they stood where the pathway was broken down. In the fierceness of their rush, the foremost were thrust over the brink by those pressing up behind, before the advance could be halted, and these went screaming to a horrid death in the great gulf below. But it was no position here that a lavish spending of men could take, and presently all were drawn off, save for some half-score who stood as outpost sentries, and dodged out of arrow-shot behind angles of the rock.

===Chinese===

看来，福伦尼刻对山上祭司氏族的怨怒，还有辅她复仇的那份灿烂的天才，都比我料想的更多。她的兵马轻易攻取了我们留给他们的几道门，蜂拥而上，直到路断之处才站住。冲锋的狂猛之中，前面的来不及叫前进止住，就被后面往上挤的人推下了断口，一路尖叫着坠进下面巨大的深渊，死得凄惨。但这里的地势，不是大手笔耗用人命便能夺取的，不多时人马便都撤了下去，只留十来个人充作前哨，躲藏在岩石棱角后闪出箭程之外。

===Original===
