It seems, too, that the Empress herself reconnoitered the place, using due caution and quickness, and so got for herself a full plan of its requirements without being obliged to trust the measuring of another eye. With extraordinary nimbleness she must have planned an engine such as was necessary to suit her purposes, and given orders for its making; for even with the vast force and resources at her disposal, the speed with which it was built was prodigious.

===Chinese===

而且，看来女皇还亲自侦察了那地方，做得又谨慎又迅捷，从而为自己弄到一份对所需一切的完整图样，不必假手旁人的眼睛来替她测度。她必是以非凡的敏捷，拟好了合乎她用途的必要器械的图样，并下令制造；因为即便以她掌中的浩大人力物力，那器械造起来的速度也堪称骇人。

===Original===

There was very little noise made to tell of what was afoot. All the woodwork and metalwork was cut, and tongued, and forged, and fitted first by skilled craftsmen below, in the plain at the foot of the cleft; and when each ponderous balk and each crosspiece, and each plank was dragged up the steep pass through the conquered gates, it was ready instantly for fitting into its appointed place in the completed machine.

===Chinese===

那边几乎没有什么声响透露出动静。全部木作和铁作，都先由崖隙脚下平原上的巧匠们在下面裁割、开榫、锻打、装配停当；等每一根沉重的大梁、每一根横档、每一块板件被拖着穿过已破的各道门、上到陡峭的通道顶时，便可即刻安进那台完整机器里既定的位置。

===Original===

The cleft was straight where they set about their building, and there was no curve or spur of the cliff to hide their handiwork from those of the Priests who watched from the ramparts above our one remaining gate. But Phorenice had a coyness lest her engine should be seen before it was completed, and so to screen it she had a vast fire built at the uppermost point where the causeway was broken off, and fed diligently with wet sedge and green wood, so that a great smoke poured out, rising like a curtain that shut out all view. And so though the Priests on the rampart above the gate picked off now and again some of those who tended the fire, they could do the besiegers no further injury, and remained up to the last quite in ignorance of their tactics.

===Chinese===

他们动工的那段崖隙笔直，没有一处弯窿或岩嘴能遮住他们的活计，躲过在我们仅存那道门上壁垒间瞭望的祭司的眼睛。可福伦尼刻存着几分羞怯，唯恐器械未成之前便被看见，于是为了遮掩，她叫人在栈道断口的最上端燃起一大堆火，勤勤恳恳添上湿莎草和青柴，于是浓烟滚滚涌出，像一道帘幕般升起，遮断了一切视线。这样一来，门上壁垒的祭司虽不时狙杀一两个看火的，却再不能给围攻者更多的伤害，直到最后还全然摸不清他们的战术。

===Original===

The passage up the cleft was in shadow during the night hours, for, though all the crest of the Sacred Mountain was always lit brightly by the eternal fires which made its defence on the farther side, their glow threw no gleam down that flank where the cliff ran sheer to the plains beneath. And so it was under cover of the darkness that Phorenice brought up her engine into position for attack.

===Chinese===

夜里那几个时辰，上行的崖隙通道沉在阴影里，因为圣山顶上虽有长明的炉火照得通亮——那是山另一面的守御——火光却照不进这一侧，这面的崖壁笔直地插向脚下的平原。福伦尼刻便是趁着这夜色的掩蔽，把她的器械推上前来，进入了进攻的位置。

===Original===

Planking had been laid down for its wheels, and the wheels themselves well greased, and it may be that she hoped to march in upon us whilst all slept. But there was a certain creaking and groaning of timbers, and laboured panting of men, which gave advertisement that something was being attempted, and the alarm was spread quietly in the hope that if a surprise had been planned, the real surprise might be turned the other way.

===Chinese===

为它的轮子铺好了板道，轮子本身也上了足油，她或许正指望趁我们酣睡之际一举开进来。可木料自有一阵吱嘎呻吟，人有一阵吃力的喘息，透露出有人正在试行什么图谋，警报便悄悄传布开来，指望倘若对方筹划的是一场偷袭，真正的意外反倒能转到我们这一边来。

===Original===

A messenger came to me running, where I sat in the house at the side of my love, and she, like the soldier’s wife she was made to be, kissed me and bade me go quickly and care for my honour, and bring back my wounds for her to mend.

===Chinese===

一名信使奔到我跟前来，那时我正坐在屋里我的爱人身旁；而她，不愧是她生来该做的那种军人之妻，吻了吻我，催我快去照管我的名誉，把伤口带回来给她缝补。

===Original===

On the rampart above the gate all was silence, save for the faint rustle of armed men, and out of the black darkness ahead, and from the other side of the broken causeway, came the sounds of which the messenger bad warned me.

===Chinese===

门上的壁垒间一片寂静，只有武装的人轻微的窸窣；从前面漆黑的黑暗里，从栈道断口的另一边，传来信使警告过我的那些声响。

===Original===

The captain of the gate came to me and whispered: “We have made no light till the King came, not knowing the King’s will in the matter. Is it wished I send some of the throwing-fire down yonder, on the chance that it does some harm, and at the same time lights up the place? Or is it willed that we wait for their surprise?”

===Chinese===

守城队长走到我跟前，低声道：「王来之前我们未曾燃灯，因为不知王在这件事上的意旨。要不要我往那边送几个抛火下去，碰巧伤他们一下，同时也照亮那地方？还是旨意要我们静候他们的偷袭？」

===Original===

“Send the fire,” I said, “or we may find that Phorenice’s brain has been one too many for us.”

===Chinese===

「把火送下去，」我说，「不然只怕要叫福伦尼刻的头脑又高出我们一筹了。」

===Original===

The captain of the gate took one of the balls in his hand, lit the fuse, and hurled it. The horrid thing burst amongst a mass of men who were labouring with a huge engine, sputtering them with its deadly fire, and lighting their garments. The plan of the engine showed itself plainly. They had built them a vast great tower, resting on wheels at its base, so that it might by pushed forward from behind, and slanting at its foot to allow for the steepness of the path and leave it always upright.

===Chinese===

守城队长拿起一枚火球，点燃引信，奋力掷出。那可憎的东西在一群正围着一件巨型器械劳作的人中间炸开，致命的火雨溅了他们一身，烧着了他们的衣裳。器械的形制由此显露无遗。他们造了一座顶天立地的巨塔，塔底安着轮子，好从后面推着前进，塔脚做成斜的，以就路径的陡峭，使塔身始终保持直立。

===Original===

It was storeyed inside, with ladders joining each floor, and through slits in the side which faced us bowmen could cover an attack. From its top a great bridge reared high above it, being carried vertically till the tower was brought near enough for its use. The bridge was hinged at the third storey of the tower, and fastened with ropes to its extreme top; but, once the ropes were cut, the bridge would fall, and light upon whatever came within its swing, and be held there by the spikes with which it was studded beneath.

===Chinese===

塔内分层，各层以梯相连，朝向我们这一面的塔壁上开着窄缝，弓手可以从缝里掩护一次进攻。塔顶之上，一座巨大的搭桥高高竖起，垂直扛着，直到塔被推到够近、可以一用的地步。桥铰接在塔的第三层，用绳索系牢在塔的最顶端；但绳索一旦斩断，桥便砸落下来，落在它摆幅之内的无论什么东西上，再用桥腹上密布的尖钉咬住不放。

===Original===

I saw, and inwardly felt myself conquered. The cleverness of Phorenice had been too strong for my defence. No war-engine of which we had command could overset the tower. The whole of its massive timbers were hung with the wet new-stripped skins of beasts, so that even the throwing-fire could not destroy it. What puny means we had to impede those who pushed it forward would have little effect. Presently it would come to the place appointed, and the ropes would be cut, and the bridge would thunder down on the rampart above our last gate, and the stormers would pour out to their final success.

===Chinese===

我看明白了，心里暗自认输。福伦尼刻的智巧压倒了我的守御。我们指挥得动的任何攻城重械，都掀不翻这座塔。它那整副厚重木料上都蒙着新剥下来还湿着的兽皮，连抛火也毁不了它。我们手头那些阻挠推塔之人的微末手段，起不了多大作用。不多时它便会到临指定的位置，绳索一斩，搭桥便雷霆般砸落在我们最后一道门上的壁垒，强攻的人便会涌出来，赢得他们最后的成功。

===Original===

Well, life had loomed very pleasant for me these few days with a warm and loving Nais once more in touch of my arms, but the High Gods in Their infinite wisdom knew best always, and I was no rebel to stay stiff-necked against their decision. But it is ever a soldier’s privilege, come what may, to warm over a fight, and the most exquisitely fierce joy of all is that final fight of a man who knows that he must die, and who lusts only to make his bed of slain high enough to carry a due memory of his powers with those who afterwards come to gaze upon it. I gripped my axe, and the muscles of my arms stood out in knots at the thought of it. Would Tatho come to give me sport? I feared not. They would send only the common soldiers first to the storm, and I must be content to do my killing on those.

===Chinese===

唉，这几日里，温热而深情的娜伊丝重新偎在我臂弯之间，生活的前景于我曾何等美好；可至上诸神以其无穷的智慧，永远最知道什么是好的，我并非悖逆之徒，不会梗着脖颈违抗他们的裁断。但不论怎样，把一场战斗烧得滚热，永远是军人的特权；而其中最精致酷烈的欢愉，莫过于一个自知必死的人的最后一战——他一心只想把杀毙的人垒成的卧榻垒得足够高，好教后来凭眺的人对他神威的记忆恰如其分。我攥紧了斧子，想到这里，双臂的肌肉一块块虬结鼓起。塔托会不会来给我添些兴致？我怕不会。他们会先只派寻常兵卒上来强攻，我只得满足于在那班人身上杀个痛快。

===Original===

And Nais, what of her? I had a quiet mind there. When any spoilers came to the house where she lay, she would know that Deucalion had been taken up to the Gods, and she would not be long in following him. She had her dagger. No, I had no fears of being parted long from Nais now.

===Chinese===

至于娜伊丝，她又当如何？这一点我心下安然。待到掳掠的人闯进她躺卧的屋子，她会知道德乌卡利翁已被接上诸神那里去了，而她随他而去，也不会太久。她有她的匕首。不，此刻我毫不担心会和娜伊丝分离长久。

## XIX. Destruction of Atlantis / 十九　亚特兰蒂斯的毁灭

===Original===

A tottering old Priest came up and touched me on the shoulder.

===Chinese===

一个步履蹒跚的老祭司走上前来，碰了碰我的肩头。

===Original===

“Well?” I said sharply, having small taste for interruption just now.

===Chinese===

「怎么？」我厉声说道，此刻我最没有兴致受人打搅。

===Original===

“News has been carried to the Three, my King, of what is threatened.”

===Chinese===

「我的王，眼前这桩危局，已经有人报给三人了。」

===Original===

“Then they will know that I stand here now, brother, to enjoy the finest fight of my life. When it is finished I shall go to the Gods, and be there standing behind the stars to welcome them when presently they also arrive. They have my regrets that they are too old and too feeble to die and look upon a fine killing themselves.”

===Chinese===

「那么他们该如何知道，我此刻正站在这里，享受平生最痛快的一战。战罢我便去到诸神那里，立在星辰背后（behind the stars）迎候他们，等他们不久也到来。他们太老、太衰弱了，不能自己也来死上一场、亲眼看看一场漂亮的杀戮——这份憾意，就请他们收下我的。」

===Original===

“I have commands from them, my King, to lay upon you, which I fear you will like but slenderly. You are forbidden to find your death here in the fighting. They have a further use for you yet.”

===Chinese===

「他们有命令交我带来给你，我的王，只怕你不会怎么乐意听。你被禁止在这场战斗里寻你的死。他们对你要派别的用场。」

===Original===

I turned on the old man angrily enough. “I shall take no such order, my brother. I am not going to believe it was ever given. You must have misunderstood. If I am a man, if I am a Priest, if I am a soldier, if I am a King, then it stands to my honour that no enemy should pass this gate whilst yet I live. And you may go back and throw that message at their teeth.”

===Chinese===

我好不恼怒地转向那老人。「这样的命令我不接受，我的兄弟。我不信它真曾下达。你一定是听岔了。我若还算一个男人，还算一个祭司，还算一个军人，还算一个王，那么只要我一息尚存，便绝不容敌人跨过这道门，这关乎我的荣誉。你尽管回去，把这口信摔到他们牙上去。」

===Original===

The old man smiled enviously. He, too, had been a keen soldier in his day. “I told them you would not easily believe such a message, and asked them for a sign, and they bore with me, and gave me one. I was to give you this jewel, my King.”

===Chinese===

老人艳羡地笑了。想当年，他自己也曾是个热切的军人。「我对他们说过，这样一条口信你不会轻易相信，便向他们讨一个凭信；他们也容了我，给了我一个。他们要我把这个交给你，我的王。」

===Original===

“How came they by that? It is a bracelet from the elbow of Nais.”

===Chinese===

「他们从哪里得来这东西？这是娜伊丝臂肘上的一只镯子。」

===Original===

“They must have stripped her of it. I did not know it came from Nais. The word I was to bring you said that the owner of the jewel was inside the Ark of the Mysteries, and waited you there. The use which the Three have for you further concerns her also.”

===Chinese===

「想必是他们从她身上剥下来的。我先前并不知道它出自娜伊丝。我奉命带给你的口信说：这宝物的主人就在奥秘的方舟里面，在那里等着你。三人要派你的用场，往后也与她相关。」

===Original===

Even when I heard that, I will freely confess that my obedience was sorely tried, and I have the less shame in setting it down on these sheets, because I know that all true soldiers will feel a sympathy for my plight. Indeed, the promise of the battle was very tempting. But in the end my love for Nais prevailed, and I gave the salutation that was needful in token that I heard the order and obeyed it.

===Chinese===

我乐于坦白承认：即便听到这话，我的服从心仍受了严酷的考验；我把这些写在这几页纸上，也不怎么羞愧，因为我知道，一切真正的军人都会对我的处境感同身受。的确，那场战斗的前景实在诱人。但到头来，我对娜伊丝的爱占了上风，我便行了应有的敬礼，以示我已听令，并且遵从。

===Original===

To the knot of Priests who were left for the defence, I turned and made my farewells. “You will have what I shall miss, my brothers,” I said. “I envy you that fight. But, though I am King of Atlantis, still I am only one of the Seven, and so am the servant of the Three and must obey their order. They speak in words the will of the most High Gods, and we must do as they command. You will stand behind the stars before I come, and I ask of you that you will commend me to Those you meet there. It is not my own will that I shall not appear there by your side.”

===Chinese===

我转向留守防务的那一小队祭司，向他们作别。「我的兄弟们，我将要错过的那一场，就归你们了，」我说。「我羡慕你们这一战。但我虽是亚特兰蒂斯的王，却仍是七人中的一员，因此是三人的仆役，必须服从他们的命令。他们以言语道出至上诸神的意旨，我们必须照他们的吩咐行事。你们会先我一步站到星辰背后去，我求你们向在那边遇见的诸位，替我美言荐引。我不能与你们并肩出现在那里，并非出于我自己的意愿。」

===Original===

They heard my words with smiles, and very courteously saluted me with their weapons, and there we parted. I did not see the fight, but I know it was good, from the time which passed before Phorenice’s hordes broke out on to the crest of the Mountain. They died hard, that last remnant of the lesser Priests of Atlantis.

===Chinese===

他们含笑听了我的话，极有礼数地举兵器向我致敬，我们便在那里分别了。那一战我没有亲见，但从福伦尼刻的部众冲上山顶之前所耗去的时间，我知道那是一场好仗。亚特兰蒂斯低阶祭司的最后残部，死得何其壮烈。

===Original===

With a sour enough feeling I went up to the head of the pass, and then through the groves, and between the temples and colleges and houses which stood on the upper slopes of the Sacred Mountain, till I reached that boundary, beyond which in milder days it was death for any but the privileged few to pass. But the time, it appeared to me, was past for conventions, and, moreover, my own temper was hot; and it is likely that I should have strode on with little scruple if I had not been interrupted. But in the temple which marked the boundary, there was old Zaemon waiting; and he, with due solemnity of words, and with the whole of some ancient ritual ordained for that purpose, sought dispensation from the High Gods for my trespass, and would not give me way till he was through with his ceremony.

===Chinese===

我怀着相当酸楚的心情登上隘口顶端，穿过那些树丛，行经矗立在圣山上坡的各座庙宇、学院与宅邸之间，直到抵达那道界线——在较为太平的年月里，除少数蒙受特权的人外，越界者一律处死。但在我看来，讲究成规的时代已经过去，况且我自己的火气正旺；若不是有人拦阻，我很可能就径直闯了过去，全无顾忌。但在标出界线的那座庙里，老泽蒙正等着；他以应有的庄重言辞，行完一整套为此目的而设的古老仪轨，为我的僭越向至上诸神求取特赦，不把仪式行完，便不肯放我过去。

===Original===

Already Phorenice’s tower and bridge were in position, for the clash and yelling of a fight told that the small handful of Priests on the rampart of the last gate were bartering their lives for the highest return in dead that they could earn. They were trained fighting men all, but old and feeble, and the odds against them were too enormous to be stemmed for over long. In a very short time the place would be put to the storm, and the roof of the Sacred Mountain would be at the open mercy of the invader. If there was any further thing to be done, it was well that it should be set about quickly whilst peace remained. It seemed to me that the moment for prompt action, and the time for lengthy pompous ceremonial was done for good.

===Chinese===

福伦尼刻的塔楼和搭桥已经就位，因为一场厮杀的金铁与呐喊之声告诉人：最后一道门的壁垒上，那一小撮祭司正在拿自己的性命做本钱，换取他们所能挣到的最高的杀敌之数。他们个个是受过训练的战阵之人，只是年老体衰，众寡之势又相差太悬殊，支撑不了多久。要不了多时，那地方便会被强攻拿下，圣山的顶面就要任凭入侵者公然宰割了。若还有什么别的事要办，趁着太平尚在，赶紧动手才是上策。在我看来，雷厉风行的时刻已到，冗长浮夸的仪典，其时代已经一去不返。

===Original===

But Zaemon was minded otherwise. He led me up to the Ark of the Mysteries, and chided my impatience, and waited till I had given it my reverential kiss, and then he called aloud, and another old man came out of the opening which is in the top of the Ark, and climbed painfully down by the battens which are fixed on its sides. He was a man I had never seen before, hoary, frail, and emaciated, and he and Zaemon were then the only two remaining Priests who had been raised to the highest degree known to our Clan, and who alone had knowledge of the highest secrets and powers and mysteries.

===Chinese===

但泽蒙另有主意。他领我上到奥秘的方舟跟前，责备我的急躁，等我对它行过虔敬的一吻，才高声呼唤；于是另一个老人从方舟顶上的开口里出来，攀着固定在它侧面的板条，艰难地爬了下来。这个人我从未见过，须发皓白，孱弱干瘦；他同泽蒙，便是彼时仅存的两位曾受擢升至我氏族所知最高品级的祭司，唯有他们两人，通晓那至高的秘密、伟力与奥秘。

===Original===

“Look!” cried Zaemon, in his shrill old voice, and swept a trembling finger over the shattered city, and the great spread of sea and country which lay in view of us below. I followed his pointing and looked, and a chill began to crawl through me. All was plainly shown. Our Lord the Sun burned high overhead in a sky of cloudless blue, and day shimmered in His heat. All below seemed from that distance peaceful and warm and still, save only that the mountains smoked more than ordinary, and some spouted fires, and that the sea boiled with some strange disorder.

===Chinese===

「看！」泽蒙用他苍老尖细的嗓音喊道，颤抖的手指扫过那座残破的城，扫过我们脚下尽收眼底的大片海洋与陆地。我顺着他的指向望去，一股寒意开始在我周身爬行。一切都昭然若揭。我们的太阳神主在万里无云的碧空里高悬灼照，白昼在祂的暑气中蒸腾闪光。从那样的高处望去，下面的一切似乎安宁、温暖而沉静，只是群山冒烟比往日更甚，有几座喷出火来，而海面沸腾着一种说不清的乱象。

===Original===

But it was the significance of the sea that troubled me most. Far out on the distant coast it surged against the rocks in enormous rolls of surf; and up the great estuary, at the head of which the city of Atlantis stands, it gushed in successive waves of enormous height which never returned. Already the lower lands on either side were blotted out beneath tumultuous waters, the harbour walls were drowned out of sight, and the flood was creeping up into the lower wards of the great city itself.

===Chinese===

但最令我心神不宁的，还是那海的意义。在遥远的海岸之外，它涌起一排排巨浪拍击礁石；又顺着亚特兰蒂斯城所滨的大河口湾上涌，灌进一浪高过一浪、去而不返的滔天巨涛。两侧的低地已经尽数没入汹涌的水下，港湾的堤墙淹得不见踪影，洪水正悄悄爬进那座大城自身的下城各区。

===Original===

“You have seen?” asked Zaemon.

===Chinese===

「你看见了？」泽蒙问。

===Original===

“I have seen.”

===Chinese===

「我看见了。」

===Original===

“You understand?”

===Chinese===

「你明白么？」

===Original===

“In part.”

===Chinese===

「明白了一半。」

===Original===

“Then let me tell you all. This is the beginning, and the end will follow swiftly. The most High Gods, that sit behind the stars, have a limit to even Their sublime patience; and that has been passed. The city of Atlantis, the great continent that is beyond, and all that are in them are doomed to unutterable destruction. Of old it was foreseen that this great wiping-out would happen through the sins of men, and to this end the Ark of the Mysteries was built under the direction of the Gods. No mortal implements can so much as scratch its surface, no waves or rocks wreck it. Inside is stored on sheets of the ancient writing all that is known in the world of learning that is not shared by the common people, also there is grain in a store, and sweet water in tanks sufficient for two persons for the space of four years, together with seeds, weapons, and all such other matters as were deemed fit.

===Chinese===

「那么让我把一切讲给你听。这是开端，结局将接踵而至。端坐在星辰背后的至上诸神，纵然那份崇高的忍耐，也有个限度；这限度已被越过了。亚特兰蒂斯城、城外那片辽阔的大陆，以及其中的一切生灵，都已注定要遭受无可言喻的毁灭。早在古时便有预见：这场大湮灭将因人类的罪孽而发生；奥秘的方舟便是为此目的、在诸神的指引之下建造的。任何凡人的器械，连划伤它的表面都办不到；任何风浪礁石，都毁不了它。里面以古文字的书页，贮存着学问世界里一切不与平民共享的所知；另有一窖存粮，有柜池里的甜水，足够两个人支用四年之期，还有种子、兵器，以及种种被认为合宜的物件。

===Original===

“Out of all this vast country it has been decreed by the High Gods that two shall not perish. Two shall be chosen, a man and a woman, who are fit and proper persons to carry away with them the ancient learning to dispose of it as they see best, and afterwards to rear up a race who shall in time build another kingdom and do honour to our Lord the Sun and the other Gods in another place. The woman is within the Ark already, and seated in the place appointed for her, and though she is a daughter of mine, the burden of her choosing is with you. For the man, the choice has fallen upon yourself.”

===Chinese===

「至上诸神已就这片广土颁下裁断：将有两人免于灭亡。要选出两个人来，一男一女，都须是合宜相称之人，让他们携带着古代的学问离去，自行斟酌处置，日后养育出一个族类，待时而起，另建一个王国，在另一片土地上尊崇我们的太阳神主与别的诸神。那女子已在方舟之内，坐在派给她的位子上；她虽是我的女儿，选不选她的担子却在你身上。至于那男子，人选已经落在你自己身上。」

===Original===

I was half numb with the shock of what was befalling. “I do not know that I care to be a survivor.”

===Chinese===

我被眼前变故的震撼弄得半身麻木。「我不知道自己是否乐意做一个幸存者。」

===Original===

“You are not asked for your wishes,” said the old man. “You are given an order from the High Gods, who know you to be Their faithful servant.”

===Chinese===

「没有人来问你的意愿，」老人说。「给你的是至上诸神的命令，他们知道你是他们忠实的仆役。」

===Original===

Habit rode strong upon me. I made salutation in the required form, and said that I heard and would obey.

===Chinese===

积习在我身上根深蒂固。我以规定的形式行了敬礼，说我听令，并愿遵从。

===Original===

“Then it remains to raise you to the sublime degree of the Three, and if your learning is so small that you will not understand the keys to many of the Powers, and the highest of the Mysteries, when they are handed to you, that fault cannot be remedied now.”

===Chinese===

「那么，剩下的事，便是把你擢升到三人那崇高的品级了；倘若你的学识浅薄，待那些伟力的钥匙、那些至高的奥秘交到你手里时，你领受不了，这缺憾如今也没法补救了。」

===Original===

Certainly the time remaining was short enough. The fight still raged down at the gate in the pass, though it was a wonder how the handful of Priests had held their ground so long. But the ocean rolled in upon the land in an ever-increasing flood, and the mountains smoked and belched forth more volleys of rock as the weight increased on their lower parts, and presently those that besieged the Mountain could not fail to see the fate that threatened them. Then there would be no withholding their rush. In their mad fury and panic they would sweep all obstruction resistlessly before them, and those who stood in their path might look to themselves.

===Chinese===

所剩的时间无疑短促之极。隘口那道门下的战斗仍在酣战，虽然那一小撮祭司竟能支撑这么久，已是个奇迹。但海洋正以愈涨愈高的洪流滚滚扑上陆地，群山下部承压愈重，便冒烟吐出更密的岩雨；要不了多久，围攻圣山的人不会看不见威胁着他们的命运。到那时，他们这一冲便再无可遏止。在疯狂的暴怒与恐慌里，他们会把一切阻碍势不可当地扫荡于前，挡在他们路上的，只好自求多福。

===Original===

But there was no hurrying Zaemon and his fellow sage. They were without temple for the ceremony, without sacrifice or incense to decorate it. They had but the sky for a roof to make their echoes, and the Gods themselves for witnesses. But they went through the work of raising me to their own degree, with all the grand and majestic form which has gathered dignity from the ages, and by no one sentence did they curtail it. A burning mountain burst with a bellowing roar as the incoming waters met its fires, but gravely they went on, in turn reciting their sentences. Phorenice’s troops broke down the last resistance, and poured in a frenzied stream amongst the groves and temples, but still they quavered never in the ritual.

===Chinese===

但泽蒙和他那位智者同侪是急不得的。他们行这仪式，没有庙宇，没有祭牲与香烟来装点。他们只有天空作穹顶，好回荡其声；只有诸神亲自作见证。但他们行完把我擢升到与他们同等品级的全部功课，一丝不苟地守着那积岁月之尊严而愈显庄严的宏大仪轨，一句也不曾删减。一座燃烧的山轰然炸响，因为涌入的海水撞上了它的火；但他们庄重地继续着，轮流诵念他们的经句。福伦尼刻的军队击破了最后的抵抗，狂潮般涌入树丛与庙宇之间，他们仍不曾在仪轨里颤一颤声。

===Original===

It had been said that this ceremony is the grandest and the most impressive of all those connected with our holy religion; and certainly I found it so; and I speak as one intimate with all the others. Even the tremendous circumstances which hemmed them in could do nothing to make these frail old men forget the deference which was due to the highest order of the Clan.

===Chinese===

世人曾说，在我们圣教的一切仪典之中，这一仪式最宏大、最摄人心魄；我确实觉得如此；而我这话，是以与其余一切仪典无不稔熟之人的身份说的。就连围困着他们的那些惊天动地的境况，也不能使这两位孱弱的老人有半分忘却：对这氏族的至高品级，应尽怎样的崇敬。

===Original===

For myself, I will freely own I was less rapt. I stood there bareheaded in the heat, a man trying to concentrate himself, and yet torn the while by a thousand foreign emotions. The awful thing that was happening all around compelled some of my attention. A continent was in the very act and article of meeting with complete destruction, and if Zaemon and the other Priest were strong enough to give their minds wholly up to a matter parochial to the priesthood, I was not so stoical. And moreover, I was filled with other anxieties and thoughts concerning Nais. Yet I managed to preserve a decent show of attention to the ceremony; making all those responses which were required of me; and trying as well as might be to preserve in my mind those sentences which were the keys to power and learning, and not mere phrasings of grandeur and devotion.

===Chinese===

至于我自己，我乐于坦承，我的心神没有那么超拔。我光着头立在暑热里，是个极力想凝神专注的人，却同时又受着千百种杂念的撕扯。四周正在发生的可怖之事，不由分说地攫去了我一部分的注意。一片大陆正正当处在彻底毁灭的关口上；泽蒙和另一位祭司若强大得足以把整个心神交给一件只关祭司本行的小事，我可没有那份坚忍。况且，我心里还满装着关于娜伊丝的种种忧虑与思虑。但我总算维持住了对仪式一份过得去的专注；凡该我应答的经句，我一一应答如仪；还尽力把那些作为伟力与学问之钥匙的句子记在心里——它们可不是徒逞庄严虔敬的浮辞。

===Original===

But it became clear that if the ceremony of my raising did not soon arrive at its natural end, it would be cut short presently with something of suddenness. Phorenice’s conquering legions swarmed out on to the crest of the Mountain, and now carried full knowledge of the dreadful thing that was come upon the country. They were out of all control, and ran about like men distracted; but knowing full well that the Priests would have brought this terrible wreck to pass by virtue of the powers which were stored within the Ark of the Mysteries, it would be their natural impulse to pour out a final vengeance upon any of these same Priests they could come across before it was too late.

===Chinese===

但情形已然分明：我的擢升仪式若不赶紧行到它自然的结尾，转眼便会被某种突变生生截断。福伦尼刻得胜的军团蜂拥冲上圣山顶端，此刻已全然知晓降临到这片国土上的可怖之事。他们全然失控，像失魂的人一样四处奔突；但他们深知，这场可怕的劫毁，正是祭司们凭奥秘的方舟里贮存的伟力招来的；趁着还来得及，把最后一场复仇倾泻到他们撞见的任何祭司身上，必是他们的本能冲动。

===Original===

It began to come to my mind that if the ceremony did not very shortly terminate, the further part of the plan would stand very small chance of completion, and I should come by my death after all by fighting to a finish, as I had pictured to myself before. My flickering attention saw the soldiers coming always nearer in their frantic wanderings, and saw also the sea below rolling deeper and deeper in upon the land.

===Chinese===

我心里渐渐明白：仪式若不立刻终了，方略的后半部分便几乎没有完成的机会，而我也终归要在血战到底中迎来我的死亡，一如我先前给自己描绘的那样。我忽明忽灭的注意力，看见士兵们在狂乱的奔突中愈逼愈近，也看见下面的海愈来愈深地滚上陆地。

===Original===

The fires, too, which ringed in half the mountain, spurted up to double their old height, and burned with an unceasing roar. But for all distraction these things gave to the two old Priests who were raising me, we might have been in the quietness of some ancient temple, with no so much as a fly to buzz an interruption.

===Chinese===

环着半座山的那些火，也窜起双倍于先前的高高的火头，带着不息的轰响燃烧。这些事对那两位为我行擢升礼的老祭司纵有万般搅扰，他们却恍若置身于某座古庙的静穆之中，连一只苍蝇嗡嗡打搅的份都没有。

===Original===

But at last an end came to the ceremony. “Kneel,” cried Zaemon, “and make obeisance to your mother the Earth, and swear by the High Gods that you will never make improper use of the powers over Her which this day you have been granted.”

===Chinese===

但仪式总算行到了尽头。「跪下，」泽蒙喊道，「向你的母亲大地叩拜，凭至上诸神起誓：今日授与你的统辖于她的诸般伟力，你永不用于不正当的用途。」

===Original===

When I had done that, he bade me rise as a fully installed and duly initiated member of the Three. “You will have no opportunity to practise the workings of this degree with either of us, my brother,” said he, “for presently our other brother and I go to stand before the Gods to deliver to Them an account of our trust, and of how we have carried it out. But what items you remember here and there may turn of use to you hereafter. And now we two give you our farewells, and promise to commend you highly to the Gods when soon we meet Them in Their place behind the stars. Climb now into the Ark, and be ready to shut the door which guards it, if there is any attempt by these raging people to invade that also. Remember, my brother, it is the Gods’ direct will that you and the woman Nais go from this place living and sound, and you are expressly forbidden to accept challenge or provocation to fight on any pretext whatever. But as long as may be done in safety, you may look out upon Atlantis in her death-throes. It is very fitting that one of the only two who are sent hence alive, should carry the full tale of what has befallen.”

===Chinese===

我行完这一切，他便命我起身，作为三人中的一员，正式就位、如仪受了秘授。「你将没有机会与我们两人中任何一位演练这一品级的功用了，我的兄弟，」他说，「因为片刻之后，我同我们那位兄弟就要去站到诸神面前，把我们受托之责及其奉行的始末，向他们交割。但你零零星星记住的那些条目，日后或许于你有用。现在我们两个向你道别，并答应你：不久之后，我们在他们星辰背后的居所见到诸神时，定替你竭力美言。现在爬进方舟去吧，把守它的那道门随时备好关上，倘若这些狂乱的人竟想连它也侵犯的话。记住，我的兄弟，你与那女子娜伊丝要活着、完好地离开此地，这是诸神直接的意旨；无论以何种借口，你都不得接受挑战或挑衅而与人交战。但只要尚能安全行事，你可以留在外面，看一看亚特兰蒂斯的临终挣扎。被活着打发出这片土地的仅有两人，其中之一应当带着这场浩劫的完整始末，这是十分合宜的。」

===Original===

I went to the top of the Ark of Mysteries then, climbing there by the battens which are fastened to the sides, and then descended by the stair which is inside and found Nais in a little chamber waiting for me.

===Chinese===

于是我走向奥秘的方舟顶上，攀着拴在侧面的板条爬了上去，再从里面的楼梯下来，在一间小室里寻见了等候我的娜伊丝。

===Original===

“I was bidden stay here by Zaemon,” she said, “who forced me to this place by threats and also by promises that my lord would follow. He is very ungentle, that father of mine, but I think he has a kindness for us both, and anyway he is my father and I cannot help loving him. Is there no chance to save him from what is going to happen?”

===Chinese===

「泽蒙吩咐我留在这里，」她说，「他一半用威吓，一半用我夫君随后便来的许诺，把我逼到了这个地方。我那位父亲，是个很不温柔的人，可我想他对我们两个都存着一份慈心，况且他终究是我的父亲，我没法不爱他。眼看就要临头的事，就没有一点机会救他躲过么？」

===Original===

“He will not come into this Ark, for I asked him. It has been ordained from the ancient time when first the Ark was built, that when the day for its purpose came, one woman and one man should be its only tenants, and they are here already. Zaemon’s will in the matter is not to be twisted by you or by me. He has a message to be delivered to the Gods, and (if I know him at all), he grudges every minute that is lost in carrying it to them.”

===Chinese===

「他不会进这方舟里来的，因为我问过他了。自方舟初造的古时便已规定：它使命之日来到时，一女一男当是它仅有的居人，而这两个人已经在这里了。在这件事上，泽蒙的意志不是你我扭得动的。他有一封要呈交诸神的文书，而且（倘若我还算了解他）送往诸神那里去的路上每耽搁一分钟，他都心疼。」

===Original===

I left her then, and went out again up the stair, and stood once more on the roof of the Ark. On the Mountain top men still ran about distracted, but gradually they were coming to where the Ark rested on the highest point. For the moment, however, I passed them lightly. The drowning of the great continent that had been spread out below filled the eye. Ocean roared in upon it with still more furious waves. The plains and the level lands were foaming lakes. The great city of Atlantis had vanished eternally. The mountains alone kept their heads above the flood, and spewed out rocks, and steam, and boiling stone, or burst when the waters reached them and created great whirlpools of surging sea, and twisted trees, and bubbling mud.

===Chinese===

我便离开她，重又循楼梯上去，再度站到方舟的顶上。山顶上人们仍在失魂地奔突，但渐渐地，他们都朝方舟安歇的最高点聚拢过来。不过眼下，我只把他们轻轻放过。下面那片曾经铺展开来的大陆正在没顶，这景象占满了整只眼睛。海洋带着愈加狂暴的巨浪，吼着扑上陆地。平原与平陆都成了翻腾着白沫的湖泊。伟大的亚特兰蒂斯城已经永远消逝了。唯余群山还把头颅探在洪水之上，喷吐着岩块、蒸汽与滚沸的石头；待洪水触及，便轰然炸裂，造出一个个由翻涌的海水、拧绞的树木与冒泡泥浆搅成的大漩涡。

===Original===

In the space of a few breaths every living creature that dwelt in the lower grounds had been smothered by the waters, save for a few who huddled in a pair of galleys that were driven oarless inland, over what had once been black forest and hunting land for the beasts. And even as I watched, these also were swallowed up by the horrid turmoil of sea, and nothing but the sea beasts, and those of the greater lizards which can live in such outrageous waters, could have survived even that state of the destruction. Indeed, none but those men who had now found standing-ground on the upper slopes of the Sacred Mountain survived, and it was plain that their span was short, for the great mass of the continent sank deeper and more deep every minute before our aching eyes, beneath the boiling inrush of the seas.

===Chinese===

不过几口气的工夫，栖居低地的一切活物都已闷死在水下；只有寥寥数人，蜷在两条被洪水驱着、无桨漂向内陆的桨船里——漂过那片从前曾是黑森林与野兽猎场的地方。就连这些，也在我眼睁睁看着的时候，被那可怕的海的骚乱吞没了；除却海中的兽类，和那些能在如此暴戾的水里存活的巨蜥，再没有什么能在这毁灭的境地中幸存。的确，除了那些此刻在圣山上坡找到立足之地的人，无人幸存；而他们的命数显然也已不长，因为在我们发痛的眼睛前面，大陆的巨大躯体正一分钟比一分钟沉得更深，沉入沸腾倒灌的海水之下。

===Original===

But though the great mass of the soldiery were dazed and maddened at the prospect of the overwhelming which threatened them, there were some with a strength of mind too valiant to give any outward show of discomposure. Presently a compact little body of people came from out the houses and the temples, and headed directly across the open ground towards the Ark. On the outside marched Phorenice’s personal guards with their weapons new blooded. They had been forced to fight a way through their own fellow soldiers. The poor demented creatures had thought it was everyone for himself now, till these guards (by their mistress’s order) proved to them that Phorenice still came first.

===Chinese===

但尽管士兵中的大众，面对威胁着他们的灭顶之灾已然目眩神狂，却仍有一些人，心志刚勇得不肯露出半分慌乱。不多时，一小队紧凑齐整的人马从房屋庙宇之间出来，径直穿过空地，朝方舟走来。外围行进着福伦尼刻的贴身卫队，兵器上血渍犹新。他们是从自己袍泽的士兵中间杀出一条路来的。那些可怜的癫狂之徒，原以为如今已是人各为己，直到这些卫兵（奉他们女主人之命）向他们证明：福伦尼刻依然排在第一。

===Original===

And in the middle of them, borne in a litter of gold and ivory by her grotesque European slaves, rode the Empress, still calm, still lovely, and seemingly divided in her sentiments between contempt and amusement. Her two children lay in the litter at her feet. On her right hand marched Tatho gorgeously apparelled, and with a beard curled and plaited into a thousand ringlets. On the other side, plying her industry with unruffled defence, walked Ylga, once again fan-girl, and so still second lady in this dwindling kingdom.

===Chinese===

在他们中间，女皇乘在一乘金与象牙的敞轿里，由她那班奇形怪状的欧罗巴奴隶抬着，依旧沉静，依旧可爱，神情之间，轻蔑与兴味似乎各居其半。她的两个孩子躺在轿中她的脚边。她右手边，行着衣饰华艳的塔托，胡须卷拢编结成千百个小卷。另一侧走着的，是伊尔加——她再度充任执扇侍女，在这日见凋零的王国里便仍是第二贵妇，只不动声色、恪勤不懈地尽着她的职守。

===Original===

The party of them halted half a score of paces from the Ark by Phorenice’s order. “Do not go nearer to those unclean old men. They carry a rank odour with them, and for the moment we are short of essences to sweeten the air of their neighbourhood.” She lifted her eyebrows and looked up at me. “Truly a quiet little gathering of old acquaintances. Why, there is Deucalion, that once I took the flavour of and threw aside when he cloyed me.”

===Chinese===

这一行人奉福伦尼刻之命，在离方舟十步开外停了下来。「别再挨近那些不洁的老头儿。他们身上带着一股浊臭，眼下我们又缺香精，来熏香他们左近的空气。」她扬起眉毛，抬眼望着我。「真是老相识们一场安静的小聚呀。哟，这不是德乌卡利翁么——我从前尝过他的滋味，吃得腻了，便扔到一边去了。」

===Original===

“I have Nais here,” I said, “and presently we two will be all that are left alive of this nation.”

===Chinese===

「娜伊丝在我这里，」我说，「不久之后，我们两个就会是这个国族里仅存的活口了。」

===Original===

“Nais is quite welcome to my leavings,” she laughed. “I will look down upon your country cooings when presently I go back to the Place behind the stars from which I came. You are a very rustic person, Deucalion. They tell me too that three or four of these smelling old men up here have named you King. Did you swell much with dignity? Or did you remember that there was a pretty Empress left that would still be Empress so long as there was an Atlantis to govern? Come, sir, find your tongue. By my face! you must have hungered for me very madly these years we have been parted, if new-grown ruggedness of feature is an evidence.”

===Chinese===

「娜伊丝要我的残羹剩炙，尽管拿去好了，」她笑道。「等一会儿我回到我来自的星辰背后的地方（the Place behind the stars），自会居高临下地看你们乡下人的呢喃咕哝。你可真是个乡巴佬，德乌卡利翁。他们还告诉我，上边这些发臭的老头儿里，有三四个立了你做王。你可曾把自己尊严得胀了起来？还是说，你想起了还留着一位漂亮的女皇——只要还有个亚特兰蒂斯可供治理，她便仍是女皇？来呀，先生，倒是开口说话。凭我这张脸！倘若新添的这副粗砺面相能作凭据，我们分开的这些年里，你准是想我想得发了狂。」

===Original===

“Have your gibe. I do not gibe back at a woman who presently will die.”

===Chinese===

「尽管讥笑吧。对一个不久就要死的女人，我不还嘴。」

===Original===

“Bah! Deucalion, you will live behind the times. Have they not told you that I know the Great Secret and am indeed a Goddess now? My arts can make life run on eternally.”

===Chinese===

「呸！德乌卡利翁，你注定要活在时代后头了。他们没告诉你么：我已通晓大奥秘，如今当真是一位女神了？我的法术，能让生命永无止境地流淌下去。」

===Original===

“Then the waters will presently test them hard,” I said, but there the talk was taken into other lips. Zaemon went forward to the front of the litter with the Symbol of our Lord the Sun glowing in his hand, and burst into a flow of cursing. It was hard for me to hear his words. The roar of the waters which poured up over the land, and beat in vast waves against the Sacred Mountain itself, grew nearer and more loud. But the old man had his say.

===Chinese===

「那么，海水马上就要狠狠考验这法术一场了，」我说；但话头随即转到了别人的嘴上。泽蒙手中擎着灼灼放光的太阳神主圣徽，走到敞轿前面，迸出一长串诅咒。他的话我很难听清。漫上陆地、以巨浪拍击圣山本身的水的轰鸣，愈来愈近，愈来愈响。但老人还是把他的话说完了。

===Original===

Phorenice gave orders to her guards for his killing; yes, tried even to rise from the litter and do the work herself; but Zaemon held the Symbol to his front, and its power in that supreme moment mastered all the arts that could be brought against it. The majesty of the most High Gods was vindicated, and that splendid Empress knew it and lay back sullenly amongst the cushions of her litter, a beaten woman.

===Chinese===

福伦尼刻下令卫兵杀他；不错，她甚至想从轿中起身，亲手了结；但泽蒙把圣徽举在胸前，在那至高的时刻，圣徽的伟力压倒了一切能搬来与它抗衡的法术。至上诸神的威严得了申张，那位气派堂堂的女皇也知道败局已定，怏怏地躺回敞轿的软垫之间，一个被打败了的女人。

===Original===

Only one person in that rigid knot of people found power to leave the rest, and that was Ylga. She came out to the side of the Ark, and leaned up, and cried me a farewell through the gathering roar of the flood.

===Chinese===

那一群僵立的人中，只有一个有能力脱身离开，那就是伊尔加。她走到方舟旁边，仰起身子，穿过愈聚愈响的洪水轰鸣，向我喊了声道别。

===Original===

“I would I might save you and take you with us,” I said.

===Chinese===

「但愿我能救你，带你与我们同走，」我说。

===Original===

“As for that,” she said, with a gesture, “I would not come if you asked me. I am not a woman that will take anything less than all. But I shall meet what comes presently with the memory that you will have me always somewhere in your recollection. I know somewhat of men, even men of your stamp, Deucalion, and you will never forget that you came very near to loving me once.”

===Chinese===

「至于那个嘛，」她做了个手势，说，「你便是求我，我也不会来。我不是个肯拿『全部』以外任何东西的女人。但一会儿临到头上的事来时，我会带着这样一段记忆去迎它：在你心底的某个角落，你会永远存着我。对男人我略知一二，哪怕是你们这一类的男人，德乌卡利翁；你永远不会忘记，你曾经差一点就爱上我了。」

===Original===

I think, too, she said something further, concerning Nais, but the bellowing rush of the waters drowned all other words. A great mist made from the stream sent up by the swamped burning mountains stopped all accurate view, though the blaze from the fires lit it like gold. But I had a last sight of a horde of soldiery rushing up the slopes of the Mountain, with a scum of surge billowing at their heels, and licking many of them back in its clutch. And then my eye fell on old Zaemon waving to me with the Symbol to shut down the door in the roof of the Ark.

===Chinese===

我想，她随后还说了些别的话，是关于娜伊丝的，但洪水咆哮的奔流吞没了其余的一切言语。燃烧的群山被淹没后喷起的水流，腾成一大片雾障，遮断了一切清晰的视野，虽然烈焰的光把它照得一片金亮。但我最后望见的一幕，是一大群士兵往山坡上奔逃，脚跟后面翻涌着浪沫的怒潮，把许多人一舔回它的攫握之中。然后，我的眼睛落到老泽蒙身上，他正朝我挥动圣徽，示意我关上方舟顶上的门。

===Original===

I obeyed his last command, and went down the stair, and closed all ingress behind me. There were bolts placed ready, and I shot these into their sockets, and there were Nais and I alone, and cut off from all the rest of our world that remained.

===Chinese===

我服从了他最后一道命令，走下楼梯，把身后的一切入口尽数关闭。门闩都已备置停当，我把它们一一推进闩眼，于是只剩下娜伊丝和我两个人，同我们这个世界上残余的其他一切，隔绝开了。

===Original===

I went to the place where she lay, and put my arms tightly around her. Without, we heard men beating desperately on the Ark with their weapons, and some even climbed by the battens to the top and wrenched to try and move the door from its fastenings. The end was coming very nearly to them now, and the great crowd of them were mad with terror.

===Chinese===

我走到她躺卧的地方，紧紧用双臂搂住她。外面，我们听见人们拿兵器拼死捶打方舟，有的甚至攀着板条爬上顶去，死命扳撬，想把这扇门从锁闩上挪开。末日如今已近在他们眼前，那大群大群的人都恐怖得疯了。

===Original===

I would have given much to have known how Phorenice fared in that final tumult, and how she faced it. I could see her, with her lovely face, and her wondrous eyes, and her ruddy hair curling about her neck, and by all the Gods! I thought more of her at that last moment than of the poor land she had conquered, and misgoverned, and brought to this horrid destruction. There is no denying the fascination which Phorenice carried with her.

===Chinese===

若能知道福伦尼刻在那最后的骚乱中境况如何、她是如何面对的，我情愿付出许多。我看得见她：那张可爱的脸，那双神奇的眼睛，那绕着颈项卷曲的红发——凭尽诸神起誓！在那最后一刻，我想她竟多过想那片可怜的国土——她征服过、失治过、又亲手送进这场可怖毁灭的国土。福伦尼刻身上带着的那股魔力，是无法否认的。

===Original===

But the end did not dally long with its coming. There was a little surge that lifted the Ark a hand’s breadth or so in its cradle, and set it back again with a jar and a quiver. The blows from axes and weapons ceased on its lower part, but redoubled into frenzied batterings on its rounded roof. There were some screams and cries also which came to us but dully through the thickness of its ponderous sheathing, though likely enough they were sent forth at the full pitch of human lungs outside. And when another surge came, roaring and thundering, which picked up the great vessel as though it had been a feather, and spun it giddily; and after that we touched earth or rock no more.

===Chinese===

但结局没有拖沓多久便来了。有一股小涌浪，把方舟在它的座窝里托起一掌来高，又哐当一颤地把它放回原处。斧斤与兵器的敲打在它下半截停了，却加倍疯狂地擂在它浑圆的顶部。还有一些尖叫与呼号传进我们耳中，只是隔着它厚重外壳的层层裹覆，传来得发钝，虽然外面放开肺腑喊出的音量，多半已到了人声的极限。接着又一股涌浪来了，吼着、轰着，把那巨大的容器像拈一根羽毛似的拈起，天旋地转地一旋——自那以后，我们再也没有触到过陆地或岩石。

===Original===

We tossed about on the crest and troughs of delirious seas, a sport for the greedy Gods of the ocean. The lamp had fallen, and we crouched there in darkness, dully weighed with the burden of knowledge that we alone were saved out of what was yesterday a mighty nation.

===Chinese===

我们在癫狂之海的浪峰与浪谷之间颠簸抛掷，成了贪婪的海洋诸神的一个玩物。灯已经倒了，我们蜷缩在黑暗里，被一份知晓的重负沉沉地压着：从昨天还是一个大国的这一切之中，得救的唯有我们两人。

## XX. On the Bosom of the Deep / 二十　在深海的怀抱上

===Original===

The Ark was rudderless, oarless, and machineless, and could travel only where the High Gods chose. The inside was dark, and full of an ancient smell, and crowded with groanings and noise. I could not find the firebox to relight the fallen lamp, and so we had to endure blindly what was dealt out to us. The waves tossed us in merciless sport, and I clung on by the side of Nais, holding her to the bed. We did not speak much, but there was full companionship in our bereavement and our silence.

===Chinese===

方舟没有舵，没有桨，也没有机械，只能行到至上诸神所择定的地方。里面一片黑暗，弥漫着一股古老的气味，挤满呻吟与声响。我找不到火匣（firebox）来重新点亮倒落的灯，于是我们只得盲然忍受分派到头上的一切。海浪把我们无情地抛掷戏耍，我紧紧守在娜伊丝身旁，把她按定在床榻上。我们说话不多，但在共同的丧亡与沉默里，有着十足相伴的情谊。

===Original===

When Atlantis sank to form new ocean bed, she left great whirlpools and spoutings from her drowned fires as a fleeting legacy to the Gods of the Sea. And then, I think (though in the black belly of the Ark we could not see these things), a vast hurricane of wind must have come on next so as to leave no piece of the desolation incomplete. For seven nights and seven days did this dreadful turmoil continue, as counted for us afterwards by the reckoner of hours which hung within the Ark, and then the howling of the wind departed, and only the roll of a long still swell remained. It was regular and it was oily, as I could tell by the difference of the motion, and then for the first time I dared to go up the stair, and open the door which stood in the roof of the Ark.

===Chinese===

亚特兰蒂斯沉落下去、化作新的洋床时，留下巨大的漩涡，被淹熄的炉火喷涌激溅，作为一份转瞬即逝的遗产，馈赠给海洋诸神。随后，我想（尽管在方舟漆黑的腹中我们看不见这一切），必又有一场浩大的飓风随之袭来，好叫这荒芜不留一角未竟。这场可怖的骚乱持续了七夜又七天——这是方舟里悬着的时辰计（reckoner of hours）事后替我们数算出来的——然后风的呼啸退去了，只剩一道悠长而平静的涌浪起伏。它均匀，它油滑，我从颠簸样式之别里分辨得出；于是我头一回敢于登上梯级，去打开方舟顶上立着的那道门。

===Original===

The sweet air came gushing down to freshen the foulness within, and as the Ark rode dryly over the seas, I went below and brought up Nais to gain refreshment from the curing rays of our Lord the Sun. Duly the pair of us adored Him, and gave thanks for His great mercy in coming to light another day, and then we laid ourselves down where we were to doze, and take that easy rest which we so urgently needed.

===Chinese===

清甜的空气涌灌而下，涤新了里面的浊秽。方舟干干爽爽地行过海面，我便下到下面，把娜伊丝带上来，让她从太阳神主疗愈的光线里领受焕新。我们两人如仪敬拜祂，感谢祂大发慈悲，又来照亮新的一天；然后我们就地躺下打盹，去取那我们急切需要的安适休息。

===Original===

Yet, though I was tired beyond words, for long enough sleep would not visit me. Wearily I stared out over the oily sunlit waters. No blur of land met the eye. The ring of ocean was unbroken on every side, and overhead the vault of heaven remained unchanged. The bosom of the deep was littered with the poor wreckage of Atlantis, to remind one, if there had been a need, that what had come about was fact, and not some horrid dream. Trees, squared timber, a smashed and upturned boat of hides, and here and there the rounded corpse of a man or beast shouldered over the swells, and kept convoy with our Ark as she drifted on in charge of the Gods and the current.

===Chinese===

然而，尽管我疲惫得无以言表，睡意却久久不肯来顾。我倦怠地凝望那油亮的、阳光照彻的水面。眼底望不见一丝陆地的影。四面八方，海洋的环合浑然不断；头顶上，苍穹依旧未改。深海的怀抱上散布着亚特兰蒂斯可怜的残骸，提醒人——若果有此需要——既成的事实并非某场骇人的梦。树木、方正的木料、一只砸烂翻覆的皮船，还有人或牲畜圆滚的尸身，这里一处那里一处，在涌浪上推肩而过，与我们的方舟结队同行——它就这样在诸神与洋流的看管之下继续漂流。

===Original===

But sleep came to me at last, and I dropped off into unconsciousness, holding the hand of Nais in mine, and when next I woke, I found her open-eyed also and watching me tenderly. We were finely rested, both of us, and rest and strength bring one complacency. We were more ready now to accept the station which the High Gods had made for us without repining, and so we went below again into the belly of the Ark to eat and drink and maintain strength for the new life which lay before us.

===Chinese===

但睡眠终于还是来到我身上，我握着娜伊丝的手，沉入了无知无觉。待我再度醒来，见她也正睁着眼，温柔地注视着我。我们两个都歇得极好，而休息与气力能教人知足。此刻我们更乐于毫无怨怼地接受至上诸神为我们安排的身份，于是我们又下到方舟的腹中吃喝，为摆在眼前的新生活养住气力。

===Original===

A wonderful vessel was this Ark, now we were able to see it at leisure and intimately. Although for the first time now in all its centuries of life it swam upon the waters, it showed no leak or suncrack. Inside, even its floor was bone dry. That it was built from some wood, one could see by the grainings, but nowhere could one find suture or joint. The living timbers had been put in place and then grown together by an art which we have lost today, but which the Ancients knew with much perfection; and afterwards some treatment, which is also a secret of those forgotten builders, had made the wood as hard as metal and impervious to all attacks of the weather.

===Chinese===

如今我们能从容而贴近地细看它了——这方舟真是一件奇妙的造物。尽管在它历数百年的生命里，这是头一回浮游水上，它却不见一处渗漏或晒裂。里面，连地面都干燥透顶。从木纹看得出它是用某种木料造的，但哪里也寻不见一道嵌缝或一个接榫。那些活着的木料被安放到位，再由一门今日已经失传、古人却掌握得十分圆熟的技艺，生长合为一体；随后又经某种处置——这同样是那些被遗忘的建造者的秘密——木料变得坚硬如金属，风霜雨雾的侵袭都奈何它不得。

===Original===

In the gloomy cave of its belly were stored many matters. At one end, in great tanks on either side of central alley, was a prodigious store of grain. Sweet water was in other tanks at the other end. In another place were drugs and samples, and essences of the life of beasts; all these things being for use whilst the Ark roamed under the guidance of the Gods on the bosom of the deep. On all the walls of the Ark, and on all the partitions of the tanks and the other woodwork, there were carved in the rude art of bygone time representations of all the beasts which lived in Atlantis; and on these I looked with a hunter’s interest, as some of them were strange to me, and had died out with the men who had perpetuated them in these sculptures. There was a good store of weapons too and the tools for handicrafts.

===Chinese===

方舟腹中那幽暗的洞穴里，贮存着许多物件。一端，中央走道两侧的大柜池里，是数量惊人的存粮；另一端别的柜池里是甜水。另一处放着药物与诸般样品，还有兽命精华；这一切，都是供方舟在诸神的指引下、在深海的怀抱上游荡时取用的。方舟所有的壁面上，在柜池隔板和其余木作的每一处，都以往古时代的粗犷技艺，刻着一切曾在亚特兰蒂斯生活的兽类；我怀着猎人的兴致注视它们，其中有几种于我是陌生的，早已随了把它们镌存于这些雕刻里的人们一同绝灭。另有一批上好的兵器，以及各色手艺的工具。

===Original===

Now, for many weeks, our life endured in this Ark as the Gods drove it about here and there across the face of the waters. We had no government over direction; we could not by so much as a hair’s breadth a day increase her speed. The High Gods that had chosen the two of us to be the only ones saved out of all Atlantis, had sole control of our fate, and into Their hands we cheerfully resigned our future direction.

===Chinese===

就这样，一连许多个星期，我们的生命在这方舟里延续，任凭诸神驱着它在水面上四处漂行。方向全然不由我们做主；它的速度，我们连一天一丝一发也添不上去。从整个亚特兰蒂斯里拣选我们两个作为唯一获救者的至上诸神，独揽着我们命运的掌管；我们未来的去向，也欣然交托在他们的手里。

===Original===

Of that land which we reached in due time, and where we made our abiding place, and where our children were born, I shall tell of in its place; but since this chronicle has proceeded so far in an exact order of the events as they came to pass, it is necessary first to narrate how we came by the sheets on which it is written.

===Chinese===

至于我们日后及时抵达的那片土地——我们在那里安居成家，我们的孩子在那里降生——我将在它应在的地方再讲；但既然这部编年史至今一直严格循着事件发生的先后次序进行，就有必要先叙述一番：我们是怎样得到书写它的这些书页的。

===Original===

In a great coffer, in the centre of the Ark’s floor, the whole of the Mysteries learned during the study of ages were set down in accurate writing. I read through some of them during the days which passed, and the awfulness of the Powers over which they gave control appalled me. I had seen some of these Powers set loose in Atlantis, and was a witness of her destruction. But here were Powers far higher than those; here was the great Secret of Life and Death which Phorenice also had found, and for which she had been destroyed; and there were other things also of which I cannot even bring my stylo to scribe.

===Chinese===

方舟地面正中有一口大柜，里面以精确的文字，录下了历代研习所得的全部奥秘。在后来过去的日子里，我通读了其中几卷，它们所授驾驭之权的那些伟力，其可怖令我胆寒。这些伟力中有几种，我曾在亚特兰蒂斯亲见它们被释放出来，并且做了她毁灭的见证。但这里的伟力，远高于那些；这里就是那大奥秘、那生死之秘——福伦尼刻也曾寻得它，并因它而遭毁灭；还有些别的东西，我甚至无法驱使手中的尖笔（stylo）去书写。

===Original===

The thought of being custodian of these writings was more than I could endure, and the more the matter rested in my mind, the more intolerable became the burden. And at last I took hot irons, and with them seared the wax on the sheets till every letter of the old writings was obliterated. If I did wrong, the High Gods in Their infinite justice will give me punishment; if it is well that these great secrets should endure on earth, They in their infinite power will dictate them afresh to some fitting scribes; but I destroyed them there as the Ark swayed with us over the waves; and later, when we came to land, I rewrote upon the sheets the matters which led to great Atlantis being dragged to her death-throes.

===Chinese===

做这些文书的看管人，这念头超出我所能承受；这件事在我心里搁得越久，这副担子就越发难忍。终于，我取来烧热的铁，用它把书页上的蜡一一烫熔，直到古文书上的每一个字迹都灭尽无存。我若行了错事，至上诸神以他们无尽的公正，自会给我惩罚；这些伟大的秘密若理当存续于世间，他们以无尽的伟力，自会重新口授给某个合宜的书记；但我确乎在那里毁掉了它们——当时方舟正载着我们在浪涛上摇晃；后来，待我们上了陆地，我又在这些书页上，重写了那些招致伟大的亚特兰蒂斯被拖入临终挣扎的种种事由。

===Original===

Nais, that I love so tenderly﻿—

===Chinese===

娜伊丝，我是那样温柔地爱着——

---

===Original===

			

[Translator’s Note: The remaining sheets are too broken to be legible.]

===Chinese===

［译者注：余下的书页破碎过甚，无法辨读。］

## Endnotes / 尾注
===Original===
- 
					

===Chinese===
- 
					

===Original===
Translator’s Note: Professor Reeder of the Wyoming State University has recently unearthed the skeleton of a Brontosaurus, 130 ft. in length, which would have weighed 50 tons when alive. It was 35 ft. in height at the hips, and 25 ft. at the shoulder, and 40 people could be seated with comfort within its ribs. Its thigh bone was 8 ft. long. The fossils of a whole series of these colossal lizards have been found. ↩︎

===Chinese===
译者注：怀俄明州立大学的里德教授近来发掘出一具雷龙骨架，身长 130 英尺，活着时体重可达 50 吨。其臀部高 35 英尺，肩高 25 英尺，肋骨范围内可从容容纳 40 人。股骨长 8 英尺。这一系列巨蜥的整组化石均有出土。↩︎

## Prefatory / 序言

===Original===

The Legatees of Deucalion

===Chinese===

德乌卡利翁的遗产继承人

===Original===

We were both of us not a little stiff as the result of sleeping out in the open all that night, for even in Grand Canary the dew-fall and the comparative chill of darkness are not to be trifled with. For myself on these occasions I like a bit of a run as an early refresher. But here on this rough ground in the middle of the island there were not three yards of level to be found, and so as Coppinger proceeded to go through some sort of dumbbell exercises with a couple of lumps of bristly lava, I followed his example. Coppinger has done a good deal of roughing it in his time, but being a doctor of medicine amongst other things﻿—he takes out a new degree of some sort on an average every other year﻿—he is great on health theories, and practises them like a religion.

===Chinese===

那一夜我们俩都在露天里过的夜，浑身不免有些僵直，因为即便在大加那利岛（Grand Canary），夜露和入夜后那阵相对的寒凉也轻慢不得。就我自己而言，每逢这种场合，总喜欢清早跑上一小段，提提精神。可在这岛中央的乱石地上，连三码平整的地方也寻不出来，于是科平杰（Coppinger）拿起两块毛刺刺的熔岩块做起某种哑铃操来，我也就依样画瓢。科平杰这辈子风餐露宿得多了，但他是个医学博士，此外还兼着好些头衔——平均每隔一年便考出一个新学位——于养生一道极是讲究，并且像奉行宗教一般身体力行。

===Original===

There had been rain two days before, and as there was still a bit of stream trickling along at the bottom of the barranca, we went down there and had a wash, and brushed our teeth. Greatest luxury imaginable, a toothbrush, on this sort of expedition.

===Chinese===

两天前下过雨，沟谷（barranca）底还有一小股溪水涓涓流着，我们便下到那里洗漱一番，刷了牙。在这种探险远行里，一支牙刷真是想象得到的头等奢侈品。

===Original===

“Now,” said Coppinger when we had emptied our pockets, “there’s precious little grub left, and it’s none the better for being carried in a local Spanish newspaper.”

===Chinese===

「喏，」我们把口袋掏空之后，科平杰说道，「剩下的吃食少得可怜，又一路包在一张本地西班牙报纸里，滋味好不到哪儿去。」

===Original===

“Yours is mostly tobacco ashes.”

===Chinese===

「你那份多半是烟草灰。」

===Original===

“It’ll get worse if we leave it. We’ve a lot more bad scrambling ahead of us.”

===Chinese===

「再搁下去还要更糟。我们前面还有好一段难爬的路呢。」

===Original===

That was obvious. So we sat down beside the stream there at the bottom of the barranca, and ate up all of what was left. It was a ten-mile tramp to the fonda at Santa Brigida, where we had set down our traps; and as Coppinger wanted to take a lot more photographs and measurements before we left this particular group of caves, it was likely we should be pretty sharp set before we got our next meal, and our next taste of the **Patron’s** splendid old country wine. My faith! If only they knew down in the English hotels in Las Palmas what magnificent wines one could get﻿—with diplomacy﻿—up in some of the mountain villages, the old vintage would become a thing of the past in a week.

===Chinese===

这是明摆着的。于是我们在沟谷底那道溪水旁坐下，把剩下的东西吃了个精光。到圣布里吉达（Santa Brigida）的客栈（fonda）——我们的行囊就撂在那里——足有十英里的徒步路程；而科平杰还想在离开这批洞穴之前多拍些照片、多量些尺寸，所以不等吃上下一顿饭、再尝一口**店主**（Patron）那绝妙的陈年乡土葡萄酒，我们多半先要饿得发慌。我的天！拉斯帕尔马斯（Las Palmas）那些英国旅馆里的人若是知道，只消稍施外交手腕，便能在这山村里弄到何等了得的美酒，那老陈窖不出一个礼拜便要成为绝响了。

===Original===

Now to tell the truth, the two mummies he had gathered already quite satisfied my small ambition. The goatskins in which they were sewn up were as brittle as paper, and the poor old things themselves gave out dust like a puffball whenever they were touched. But you know what Coppinger is. He thought he’d come upon traces of an old Guanche university, or sacred college, or something of that kind, like the one there is on the other side of the island, and he wouldn’t be satisfied till he’d ransacked every cave in the whole face of the cliff. He’d plenty of stuff left for the flashlight thing, and twenty-eight more films in his kodak, and said we might as well get through with the job then as make a return journey all on purpose. So he took the crowbar, and I shouldered the rope, and away we went up to the ridge of the cliff, where we had got such a baking from the sun the day before.

===Chinese===

老实说，他已经到手的那两具木乃伊，尽够满足我这点小小的抱负了。缝裹它们的山羊皮脆得像纸，那两件可怜的老古董本身，更是谁一碰便像马勃菌似的喷出一蓬尘雾。可科平杰的脾性你是知道的。他认定自己撞见了一所古关切人（Guanche）大学、或者祭司学院之类机构的踪迹，就像岛另一侧的那一座，不把整面崖壁上的洞穴搜个底朝天，他绝不肯甘心。他闪光灯那套东西的用料还很充足，柯达（kodak）相机里还剩二十八张胶片，又说与其日后专为这事再跑一趟，不如趁现在一鼓作气干完。于是他拿起撬棍，我扛上绳索，我们就朝崖顶进发——头一天我们在那上面可让太阳烤得够呛。

===Original===

Of course these caves were not easy to come at, or else they would have been raided years before. Coppinger, who on principle makes out he knows all about these things, says that in the old Guanche days they had ladders of goatskin rope which they could pull up when they were at home, and so keep out undesirable callers; and as no other plan occurs to me, perhaps he may be right. Anyway the mouths of the caves were in a more or less level row thirty feet below the ridge of the cliff, and fifty feet above the bottom; and Spanish curiosity doesn’t go in much where it cannot walk.

===Chinese===

这些洞穴当然不易接近，否则早几年就被人搜刮一空了。科平杰这人向来自命对此道无所不通，说在古关切人的年代，人们用山羊皮绳结梯，人在家时便把梯子收上去，好挡掉不受欢迎的来客；我想不出别的说法，那么他或许是对的。无论如何，这些洞口大致排成一列齐平的线，在崖顶以下三十英尺、谷底以上五十英尺处；而西班牙人的好奇心，凡是走路到不了的地方，向来不大肯屈尊前往。

===Original===

Now laddering such caves from below would have been cumbersome, but a light knotted rope is easily carried, and though it would have been hard to climb up this, our plan was to descend on each cave mouth from above, and then slip down to the foot of the cliffs, and start again *ab initio* for the next.

===Chinese===

从底下给这些洞穴架梯子未免笨重，一条打结的轻绳却便于携带；虽说沿它攀上去会很难，我们的方案却是从上方吊到每个洞口，然后溜到崖脚，再*从头*（*ab initio*）开始对付下一处。

===Original===

Coppinger is plucky enough, and he has a good head on a height, but there is no getting over the fact that he is portly and nearer fifty than forty-five. So you can see he must have been pretty keen. Of course I went first each time, and got into the cave mouth, and did what I could to help him in; but when you have to walk down a vertical cliff face fly-fashion, with only a thin bootlace of a rope for support, it is not much real help the man below can give, except offer you his best wishes.

===Chinese===

科平杰胆气很足，登高时头脑也稳，可有一桩事实是绕不过去的：他身躯肥硕，年纪四十五靠上、直奔五十。所以你可以想见他的劲头有多足。当然，每一回都是我先行下去，进到洞口里，再尽力接他进来；可当你得学苍蝇的样子走下一面垂直的崖壁，手里只攥着一根鞋带似的细绳作倚仗时，底下那位能帮上的实事不多，除了奉上他最美好的祝愿。

===Original===

I wanted to save him as much as I could, and as the first three caves I climbed to were small and empty, seeming to be merely store-places, I asked him to take them for granted, and save himself the rest. But he insisted on clambering down to each one in person, and as he decided that one of my granaries was a prison, and another a pot-making factory, and another a schoolroom for young priests, he naturally said he hadn’t much reliance on my judgment, and would have to go through the whole lot himself. You know what these thoroughgoing archaeologists are for imagination.

===Chinese===

我想能替他省多少是多少，而我爬到的头三个洞又小又空，看样子不过是个储物的处所，我便请他姑且认下，剩下的力气也替他自己省了。可他偏要亲自爬到每一处去，而且他断定，我说的粮仓里有一处其实是牢房，另一处是制陶的作坊，还有一处是少年祭司的课堂，于是他顺理成章地说，他对我的判断力不甚放心，只好全部亲自过目。这些彻头彻尾的考古学家在想象力上是什么路数，你是见识过的。

===Original===

But as the day went on, and the sun rose higher, Coppinger began clearly to have had enough of it, though he was very game, and insisted on going on much longer than was safe. I must say I didn’t like it. You see the drop was seldom less than eighty feet from the top of the cliffs. However, at last he was forced to give it up. I suggested marching off to Santa Brigida forthwith, but he wouldn’t do that. There were three more cave-openings to be looked into, and if I wouldn’t do them for him, he would have to make another effort to get there himself. He tried to make out he was conferring a very great favour on me by offering to take a report solely from my untrained observation, but I flatly refused to look at it in that light. I was pretty tired also; I was soaked with perspiration from the heat; my head ached from the violence of the sun; and my hands were cut raw with the rope.

===Chinese===

但随着白日渐晚、日头升高，科平杰分明已是够了，尽管他斗志不减，硬撑着干到远超安全的地方。我必须说，我不喜欢这情形。要知道从崖顶放下去，落差很少有低于八十英尺的。然而他到底还是不得不作罢。我提议即刻开拔去圣布里吉达，他又不肯。还有三个洞口要看，我若不肯替他去办，他就得再拼一把力，亲自过去。他竭力装出恩赐的姿态，说肯只凭我这不内行的观察采纳一份报告，已算是给了我天大的情面，我却断然拒绝作此想。我也已经相当疲乏了；我让暑气蒸得浑身汗透；烈日晒得我头痛；手掌也叫绳子割勒得皮开肉绽。

===Original===

Coppinger might be tired, but he was still enthusiastic. He tried to make me enthusiastic also. “Look here,” he said, “there’s no knowing what you may find up there, and if you do lay hands on anything, remember it’s your own. I shall have no claim whatever.”

===Chinese===

科平杰人可以累，热情却不减。他还想叫我也热乎起来。「你瞧，」他说，「那上面会寻着什么，谁也说不准，你若真摸到了什么，记住，那是你自己的。我绝无分毫权利主张。」

===Original===

“Very kind of you, but I’ve got no use for any more mummies done up in goatskin bags.”

===Chinese===

「多谢好意，可我再用不上什么山羊皮口袋裹着的木乃伊了。」

===Original===

“Bah! That’s not a burial cave up there. Don’t you know the difference yet in the openings? Now, be a good fellow. It doesn’t follow that because we have drawn all the rest blank, you won’t stumble across a good find for yourself up there.”

===Chinese===

「呸！上头那个可不是葬窟。洞口的分别你到现在还认不出来？来，做个好伙计。别因为我们其余的全落了空，就说你自己不会在那上面撞上个好彩头。」

===Original===

“Oh, very well,” I said, as he seemed so set on it; and away I stumbled over the fallen rocks, and along the ledge, and then scrambled up by that fissure in the cliff which saved us the two-mile round which we had had to take at first. I wrenched out the crowbar, and jammed it down in a new place, and then away I went over the side, with hands smarting worse at every new grip of the rope. It was an awkward job swinging into the cave mouth because the rock above overhung, or else (what came to the same thing) it had broken away below; but I managed it somehow, although I landed with an awkward thump on my back, and at the same time I didn’t let go the rope. It wouldn’t do to have lost the rope then: Coppinger couldn’t have flicked it into me from where he was below.

===Chinese===

「哦，也好，」我说，他既然这么一门心思；我便跌跌撞撞跨过塌落的石块，沿着石台走了一程，再从崖上那道裂隙攀上去——正是它替我们省下了起初不得不多绕的两英里路。我拔出撬棍，换个新地方砸牢，随即翻过崖沿下去，每换一次握绳，手掌便灼痛得愈发厉害。荡进洞口是桩别扭的活计，因为上方的岩石向外悬挑，或者说（反正是一回事）下方塌落了；但我总算设法进去了，只是背着地摔了个生硬的闷墩，同时绳子也没撒手。那时节万不能丢了绳子：科平杰在他下面所站的位置，没法把绳头甩进洞来递给我。

===Original===

Now from the first glance I could see that this cave was of different structure to the others. They were for the most part mere dens, rounded out anyhow; this had been faced up with cutting tools, so that all the angles were clean, and the sides smooth and flat. The walls inclined inwards to the roof, reminding me of an architecture I had seen before but could not recollect where, and moreover there were several rooms connected up with passages. I was pleased to find that the other cave-openings which Coppinger wanted me to explore were merely the windows or the doorways of two of these other rooms.

===Chinese===

头一眼我便看得出，这洞穴的构造与其余的不同。那些多半只是兽窟似的洞子，胡乱掏得圆浑；这一处却经切削工具整修过，棱角尽数干净利落，壁面平滑齐整。四壁向屋顶内收，令我想起一种从前见过的建筑，却记不起在哪里见的；况且还有几个房间，以通道相连。我很高兴地发现，科平杰要我去探的另外几个洞口，不过是其中两个房间的窗户或门户。

===Original===

Of inscriptions or markings on the walls there was not a trace, though I looked carefully, and except for bats the place was entirely bare. I lit a cigarette and smoked it through﻿—Coppinger always thinks one is slurring over work if it is got through too quickly﻿—and then I went to the entrance where the rope was, and leaned out, and shouted down my news.

===Chinese===

墙上铭文或刻痕半点也无，我细细找过了；除了蝙蝠，这地方空空如也。我点上一支烟，从头到尾吸完——科平杰总以为，活计干得太快便是敷衍——然后我走到系绳的洞口，探出身子，朝下面喊出我的消息。

===Original===

He turned up a very anxious face. “Have you searched it thoroughly?” he bawled back.

===Chinese===

他仰起一张焦灼的脸。「你彻底搜过了吗？」他大声回喊。

===Original===

“Of course I have. What do you think I’ve been doing all this time?”

===Chinese===

「当然搜过了。你以为我这一路都在干什么？」

===Original===

“No, don’t come down yet. Wait a minute. I say, old man, do wait a minute. I’m making fast the kodak and the flashlight apparatus on the end of the rope. Pull them up, and just make me half a dozen exposures, there’s a good fellow.”

===Chinese===

「别，先别下来。等一等。喂，老兄，千万等一等。我正把柯达相机和闪光灯具拴到绳头上。把它们拉上去，替我拍上六张，做个好伙计。」

===Original===

“Oh, all right,” I said, and hauled the things up, and got them inside. The photographs would be absolutely dull and uninteresting, but that wouldn’t matter to Coppinger. He rather preferred them that way. One has to be careful about halation in photographing these dark interiors, but there was a sort of ledge like a seat by the side of each doorway, and so I lodged the camera on that to get a steady stand, and snapped off the flashlight from behind and above.

===Chinese===

「哦，好吧，」我说着把东西拉上来，搬进洞里。这些照片拍出来必定平板无趣至极，但科平杰不会在意，他倒宁可它们是那样。拍摄这些黑暗的内景得提防光晕，好在每个门洞旁都有一种像座位的石台，我便把相机搁在那上面取稳，从后面和上方引闪拍下。

===Original===

I got pictures of four of the chambers this way, and then came to one where the ledge was higher and wider. I put down the camera, wedged it level with scraps of stone, and then sat down myself to recharge the flashlight machine. But the moment my weight got on that ledge, there was a sharp crackle, and down I went half a dozen inches.

===Chinese===

就这样我拍了四个房间的照片，随后到得一间，石台更高更宽。我放下相机，拿碎石片把它垫平，自己便坐下去给闪光机再装填一回。可我的体重刚压上那石台，就听得咔啦一声脆响，我往下陷了六英寸。

===Original===

Of course I was up again pretty sharply, and snapped up the kodak just as it was going to slide off to the ground. I will confess, too, I was feeling pleased. Here at any rate was a Guanche cupboard of sorts, and as they had taken the trouble to hermetically seal it with cement, the odds were that it had something inside worth hiding. At first there was nothing to be seen but a lot of dust and rubble, so I lit a bit of candle and cleared this away. Presently, however, I began to find that I was shelling out something that was not cement. It chipped away, in regular layers, and when I took it to the daylight I found that each layer was made up of two parts. One side was shiny stuff that looked like talc, and on this was smeared a coating of dark toffee-coloured material, that might have been wax. The toffee-coloured surface was worked over with some kind of pattern.

===Chinese===

我当然一骨碌便又爬了起来，在柯达相机正要滑落地面的一刹那把它抄住。我也得承认，我心里正得意。这好歹算得上一个关切人的橱柜了，他们既然肯费事用水泥把它封得严严实实，多半里面藏着什么值当一藏的东西。起初除了大堆尘土碎石，什么也看不见，我便点起一小截蜡烛，把这些清开。然而不多时我便发现，自己剥出来的并不是水泥。它成层成片、规规矩矩地剥落，拿到日光下一看，每层都由两部分组成。一面是亮闪闪、看着像滑石（talc）的东西，上面涂着一层深色太妃糖似的材料，或许是蜡。那太妃色的表面上还满布着某种刻画的纹样。

===Original===

Now I do not profess to any knowledge on these matters, and as a consequence took what Coppinger had told me about Guanche habits and acquirements as more or less true. For instance, he had repeatedly impressed upon me that this old people could not write, and having this in my memory, I did not guess that the patterns scribed through the wax were letters in some obsolete character, which, if left to myself, probably I should have done. But still at the same time I came to the conclusion that the stuff was worth looting, and so set to work quarrying it out with the heel of my boot and a pocketknife.

===Chinese===

这些门道我原不敢充内行，因此科平杰关于关切人习性造诣的那些话，我大体照单全收。比如，他曾再三向我郑重言明，这个古老民族不会书写；这话记在心里，我便没有猜到，那些透过蜡层刻下的纹样竟是一种久已废绝的文字——若由我自己琢磨，多半是会猜到的。不过与此同时，我也断定这物件值得取走，于是动起手来，用靴跟和一把折刀把它开凿出来。

===Original===

The sheets were all more or less stuck together, and so I did not go in for separating them farther. They fitted exactly to the cavity in which they were stored, but by smashing down its front I was able to get at the foot of them, and then I hacked away through the bottom layers with the knife till I got the bulk out in one solid piece. It measured some twenty inches by fifteen, by fifteen, but it was not so heavy as it looked, and when I had taken the remaining photographs, I lowered it down to Coppinger on the end of the rope.

===Chinese===

书页都或多或少黏作一团，我也就不再费事去分拆它们。它们与藏纳它们的洞龛严丝合缝，但我把龛面砸塌之后，便够得着它们的底了，再用刀把底下几层砍断，终于把整块弄了出来，成了一大块结结实实的东西。尺寸约莫二十英寸长，十五英寸宽，十五英寸厚，看着沉，其实不然；拍完余下的照片，我便把它系在绳头，吊下去交给科平杰。

===Original===

There was nothing more to do in the caves then, so I went down myself next. The lump of sheets was on the ground, and Coppinger was on all fours beside it. He was pretty nearly mad with excitement.

===Chinese===

洞里再没有别的事可做，接着我便自己也下去了。那一大块书页搁在地上，科平杰四肢着地趴在旁边，激动得几乎发狂。

===Original===

“What is it?” I asked him.

===Chinese===

「这是什么？」我问他。

===Original===

“I don’t know yet. But it is the most valuable find ever made in the Canary Islands, and it’s yours, you unappreciative beggar; at least what there is left of it. Oh, man, man, you’ve smashed up the beginning, and you’ve smashed up the end of some history that is probably priceless. It’s my own fault. I ought to have known better than set an untrained man to do important exploring work.”

===Chinese===

「我还不知道。但这是加那利群岛（Canary Islands）有史以来最贵重的发现，而且它是你的，你这个不识货的家伙——至少剩下这部分是。噢，老兄啊老兄，你把一部多半无价的史书，开头上砸掉了，结尾也砸掉了。这得怪我自己。我本该明白事理，不该派一个没受过训练的人去做重要的探查工作。」

===Original===

“I should say it’s your fault if anything’s gone wrong. You said there was no such thing as writing known to these ancient Canarios, and I took your word for it. For anything I knew the stuff might have been something to eat.”

===Chinese===

「要我说，真出了岔子也是你的错。你说过，这些古加那利人（Canarios）压根儿不知文字为何物，我信了你的话。据我所知，这玩意儿没准儿还是什么吃食呢。」

===Original===

“It isn’t Guanche work at all,” said he testily. “You ought to have known that from the talc. Great heavens, man, have you no eyes? Haven’t you seen the general formation of the island? Don’t you know there’s no talc here?”

===Chinese===

「这根本不是关切人的手笔，」他不耐烦地说。「凭那滑石你就该看出来。老天爷，老兄，你长没长眼睛？这岛的大体地质构造你没瞧见过？你不知道这岛上根本没有滑石？」

===Original===

“I’m no geologist. Is this imported literature then?”

===Chinese===

「我又不是地质学家。这么说，这是进口的文学了？」

===Original===

“Of course. It’s Egyptian: that’s obvious at a glance. Though how it’s got here I can’t tell yet. It isn’t stuff you can read off like a newspaper. The character’s a variant on any of those that have been discovered so far. And as for this waxy stuff spread over the talc, it’s unique. It’s some sort of a mineral, I think: perhaps asphalt. It doesn’t scratch up like animal wax. I’ll analyse that later. Why they once invented it, and then let such a splendid notion drop out of use, is just a marvel. I could stay gloating over this all day.”

===Chinese===

「当然是。是埃及货：一眼便知。至于它怎么到的这里，我还说不上。这东西可念不得像念报纸那样顺当。那文字是迄今发现过的各种文字的一个变体。至于涂在滑石上的这层蜡状物，更是独一无二。我想是某种矿物：兴许是沥青。它不像动物蜡那样一刮就起屑。这个我以后再化验。他们当年发明了它，随后竟任由这样妙的一个主意失传不用，真是件奇事。对着它，我能赏玩上一整天。」

===Original===

“Well,” I said, “if it’s all the same for you, I’d rather gloat over a meal. It’s a good ten miles hard going to the fonda, and I’m as hungry as a hawk already. Look here, do you know it is four o’clock already? It takes longer than you think climbing down to each of these caves, and then getting up again for the next.”

===Chinese===

「这个嘛，」我说，「你若不介意，我倒宁可去赏玩一顿饭。到客栈足有十英里难走的路，我已经饿得像只鹰了。你瞧，你知道已经四点钟了吗？一处处洞穴这样爬下去、再爬上来去赶下一处，比你想的费时得多。」

===Original===

Coppinger spread his coat on the ground, and wrapped the lump of sheets with tender care, but would not allow it to be tied with a rope for fear of breaking more of the edges. He insisted on carrying it himself too, and did so for the larger part of the way to Santa Brigida, and it was only when he was within an ace of dropping himself with sheer tiredness that he condescended to let me take my turn. He was tolerably ungracious about it too. “I suppose you may as well carry the stuff,” he snapped, “seeing that after all it’s your own.”

===Chinese===

科平杰把外套铺在地上，怀着万般小心把那块书页裹好，却不许用绳子捆它，生怕再多磕坏边缘。他还执意亲自来拿，去圣布里吉达的路上果真拿了大半程，直到他自己眼看要叫纯粹的疲乏拖垮，才屈尊让我换一手。态度还相当不客气。「我看这东西不妨由你拿，」他没好气地说，「说到底它到底是你的。」

===Original===

Personally, when we got to the fonda, I had as good a dinner as was procurable, and a bottle of that old Canary wine, and turned into bed after a final pipe. Coppinger dined also, but I have reason to believe he did not sleep much. At any rate I found him still poring over the find next morning, and looking very heavy-eyed, but brimming with enthusiasm.

===Chinese===

就我而言，到了客栈，我吃了顿这里拿得出的最好的晚饭，喝了一瓶那种陈年加那利酒，抽完最后一斗烟便上床睡了。科平杰也用了晚饭，但我有理由相信，他没怎么睡。无论如何，第二天早晨我见他还在埋头细看那件发现物，两眼沉重发涩，热情却满得要溢出来。

===Original===

“Do you know,” he said, “that you’ve blundered upon the most valuable historical manuscript that the modern world has ever yet seen? Of course, with your clumsy way of getting it out, you’ve done an infinity of damage. For instance, those top sheets you shelled away and spoiled, contained probably an absolutely unique account of the ancient civilisation of Yucatan.”

===Chinese===

「你可知道，」他说，「你跌跌撞撞撞见的，是现代世界迄今见过的最珍贵的历史手稿？当然，凭你那副笨手笨脚的取法，你造成的损害无穷无尽。比如，你剥掉又糟蹋掉的那些顶层书页里，多半载着一份绝对独一无二的记载，写的正是尤卡坦（Yucatan）的古代文明。」

===Original===

“Where’s that, anyway?”

===Chinese===

「那地方究竟在哪儿？」

===Original===

“In the middle of the Gulf of Mexico. It’s all ruins today, but once it was a very prosperous colony of the Atlanteans.”

===Chinese===

「在墨西哥湾正当中。今天全是废墟了，但从前它是亚特兰蒂斯人一座非常繁盛的殖民地。」

===Original===

“Never heard of them. Oh yes, I have though. They were the people Herodotus wrote about, didn’t he? But I thought they were mythical.”

===Chinese===

「从没听说过。哦不，倒是听说过的。他们就是希罗多德（Herodotus）写到过的那些人，对吧？可我一直当他们是神话里的。」

===Original===

“They were very real, and so was Atlantis, the continent where they lived, which lay just north of the Canaries here.”

===Chinese===

「他们真实得很，亚特兰蒂斯也是——那片他们居住的大陆，就在这加那利群岛的正北面。」

===Original===

“What’s that crocodile sort of thing with wings drawn in the margin?”

===Chinese===

「页边上画的那头带翅膀的鳄鱼模样玩意儿是什么？」

===Original===

“Some sort of beast that lived in those bygone days. The pages are full of them. That’s a cave-tiger. And that’s some sort of colossal bat. Thank goodness he had the sense to illustrate fully, the man who wrote this, or we should never have been able to reconstruct the tale, or at any rate we could not have understood half of it. Whole species have died out since this was written, just as a whole continent has been swept away and three civilisations quenched. The worst of it is, it was written by a highly-educated man who somewhat naturally writes a very bad fist. I’ve hammered at it all the night through, and have only managed to make out a few sentences here and there”﻿—he rubbed his hands appreciatively. “It will take me a year’s hard work to translate this properly.”

===Chinese===

「是那些远去年月里生息过的某种兽类。书页里画满了这类东西。那是一头洞虎。那是一种硕大无朋的蝙蝠。谢天谢地，写这部书的人有见地，把图画全了，否则我们根本无从重构这个故事，或者至少一半也看不懂。自这书写成以来，整个整个的物种已经死绝，正如整整一片大陆被扫荡以尽、三大文明被扑灭。最麻烦的是，执笔的是位受过高等教育的人，字迹自然也就写得一塌糊涂。我磕了一整夜，也不过零零星星认出几个句子」——他搓着手，暗自得意。「要把这东西好好译出来，得花我一年苦功。」

===Original===

“Every man to his taste. I’m afraid my interest in the thing wouldn’t last as long as that. But how did it get there? Did your ancient Egyptian come to Grand Canary for the good of his lungs, and write it because he felt dull up in that cave?”

===Chinese===

「人各有所好。恐怕我对这东西的兴趣撑不了那么久。可它是怎么到那儿去的？莫非你那位古埃及人是为着养肺才来大加那利，又因为在洞里闷得发慌，才写下它的？」

===Original===

“I made a mistake there. The author was not an Egyptian. It was the similarity of the inscribed character which misled me. The book was written by one Deucalion, who seems to have been a priest or general﻿—or perhaps both﻿—and he was an Atlantean. How it got there, I don’t know yet. Probably that was told in the last few pages, which a certain vandal smashed up with his pocketknife, in getting them away from the place where they were stowed.”

===Chinese===

「在那一点上我看走了眼。作者不是埃及人。是铭刻文字的相似误导了我。这部书是一位德乌卡利翁（Deucalion）写的，此人似乎做过祭司，或做过将军——兴许两样都做过——而且是个亚特兰蒂斯人。它是怎么到那儿去的，我还不知道。多半原本写在末尾几页里，可惜那几页已被某位汪达尔人用折刀捣毁了，就为了把它们从收藏的地方弄出来。」

===Original===

“That’s right, abuse me. Deucalion you say? There was a Deucalion in the Greek mythology. He was one of the two who escaped from the Flood: their Noah, in fact.”

===Chinese===

「好啊，尽着骂我吧。你是说德乌卡利翁？希腊神话里有一位德乌卡利翁。他是从大洪水里逃生的两人之一：其实就是他们的挪亚（Noah）。」

===Original===

“The swamping of the continent of Atlantis might very well correspond to the Flood.”

===Chinese===

「亚特兰蒂斯大陆遭灭顶之沉，很可能正对应着那场大洪水。」

===Original===

“Is there a Pyrrha then? She was Deucalion’s wife.”

===Chinese===

「那么有一位皮拉（Pyrrha）吗？她是德乌卡利翁的妻子。」

===Original===

“I haven’t come across her yet. But there’s a Phorenice, who may be the same. She seems to have been the reigning Empress, as far as I can make out at present.”

===Chinese===

「我还没有碰到她。不过有一位福伦尼刻（Phorenice），或许就是同一人。就我目前辨读出来的看，她似乎是在位的女皇。」

===Original===

I looked with interest at illustrations in the margin. They were quite understandable, although the perspective was all wrong. “Weird beasts they seem to have had knocking about the country in those days. Whacking big size too, if one may judge. By Jove, that’ll be a cave-tiger trying to puff down a mammoth. I shouldn’t care to have lived in those days.”

===Chinese===

我饶有兴味地看起页边的插图来。透视虽全然不对，倒还看得明白。「那些年头满乡乱窜的，看来尽是些怪物。个头也大得吓人，瞧这架势便知。天哪，这准是一头洞虎在扑翻一头猛犸（mammoth）。我可不愿意活在那个年代。」

===Original===

“Probably they had some way of fighting the creatures. However, that will show itself as I get along with the translation.” He looked at his watch﻿—“I suppose I ought to be ashamed of myself, but I haven’t been to bed. Are you going out?”

===Chinese===

「他们多半有对付这些畜生的法子。不过，等我译下去，自会见分晓。」他看了看表——「按说我该惭愧，可我还没上过床。你要出去？」

===Original===

“I shall drive back to Las Palmas. I promised a man to have a round at golf this afternoon.”

===Chinese===

「我要坐车回拉斯帕尔马斯。我应承了人家，今天下午打一场高尔夫。」

===Original===

“Very well, see you at dinner. I hope they’ve sent back my dress shirts from the wash. O, lord! I am sleepy.”

===Chinese===

「很好，晚饭见。但愿他们已把我的礼服衬衫从洗衣房送回来了。噢，老天！我真困。」

===Original===

I left him going up to bed, and went outside and ordered a carriage to take me down, and there I may say we parted for a considerable time. A cable was waiting for me in the hotel at Las Palmas to go home for business forthwith, and there was a Liverpool boat in the harbour which I just managed to catch as she was steaming out. It was a close thing, and the boatmen made a small fortune out of my hurry.

===Chinese===

我送他上楼去睡，自己出门叫了辆马车送我下山，可以说，我们就在那里分了手，一别便是颇长的时日。拉斯帕尔马斯的旅馆里有封电报等着我，叫我即刻回国料理生意，港里正有一班开往利物浦的船，它鸣着汽笛出港时，我堪堪赶了上去。真是千钧一发，我这一急，倒叫那些船夫小发了一笔财。

===Original===

Now Coppinger was only an hotel acquaintance, and as I was up to the eyes in work when I got back to England, I’m afraid I didn’t think very much more about him at the time. One doesn’t with people one just meets casually abroad like that. And it must have been at least a year later that I saw by a paragraph in one of the papers, that he had given the lump of sheets to the British Museum, and that the estimated worth of them was ten thousand pounds at the lowest valuation.

===Chinese===

科平杰原不过是旅馆里结识的相与，我回到英国后又忙得脚不沾地，恐怕当时就没怎么再想起他。在国外这般萍水偶识的人，本也谈不上多挂心。大约至少过了一年，我才从一份报纸的短讯里得知，他已把那一大块书页赠给了大英博物馆（British Museum），而估价最低也有一万英镑。

===Original===

Well, this was a bit of revelation, and as he had so repeatedly impressed on me that the things were mine by right of discovery, I wrote rather a pointed note to him mentioning that he seemed to have been making rather free with my property. Promptly came back a stilted letter beginning, “Doctor Coppinger regrets” and so on, and with it the English translation of the wax-upon-talc MSS. He “quite admitted” my claim, and “trusted that the profits of publication would be a sufficient reimbursement for any damage received.”

===Chinese===

这可有点叫人恍然醒悟。他既曾再三向我郑重言明，这些东西凭发现之权理当归我，我便给他写了封相当露骨的短笺，说他对我这笔财物，未免动用得太随意了些。旋即回来一封拘板生硬的信，开头是『科平杰博士深表遗憾』云云，随信附来那份蜡覆滑石（wax-upon-talc）手稿的英文译文。他『完全承认』我的权利主张，并『相信出版的收益，足以为所受的任何损害作抵偿』。

===Original===

Now I had no idea that he would take me unpleasantly like this, and wrote back a pretty warm reply to that effect; but the only answer I got to this was through a firm of solicitors, who stated that all further communications with Dr. Coppinger must be made through them.

===Chinese===

我没料到他会这样恶声相待，便又回了一封相当火气的信表明此意；可我得到的唯一答复，却出自一家律师行之手，来函声称，此后与科平杰博士的一切往来函件，均须经由他们转致。

===Original===

I will say here publicly that I regret the line he has taken over the matter; but as the affair has gone so far, I am disposed to follow out his proposition. Accordingly the old history is here printed; the credit (and the responsibility) of the translation rests with Dr. Coppinger; and whatever revenue accrues from readers, goes to the finder of the original talc-upon-wax sheets, myself.

===Chinese===

我愿在此公开说一句，他在这件事上所取的路数令我遗憾；但事已至此，我倾向于把他的提议奉行到底。因此，这部古老的历史就在此印行；翻译的声誉（连同责任）归于科平杰博士；而从读者处获得的无论何种收益，都归原始滑石覆蜡（talc-upon-wax）书页的发现者，也就是我本人。

===Original===

If there is a further alteration in this arrangement, it will be announced publicly at a later date. But at present this appears to be most unlikely.

===Chinese===

此项安排若再有变更，当于日后另行公告。但在目前，这看来是极不可能的。
